````
- path: addons\opencode\README.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus OpenCode Integration
      
      Full bridge between [OpenCode](https://opencode.ai) (V2 plugin API, V1 compatible) and the
      HyperNexus control plane (Go kernel, default `http://127.0.0.1:7778`).
      
      ## What you get
      
      | Surface | Detail |
      |---|---|
      | **29 tools** | `hypernexus_*` namespace: memory (store/search/fts/vector/list/delete), scratchpad, context harvest, observations, tool discovery (3 layers), MCP bridge (status/servers/tools/search/call), imported sessions, handoff, skills, code search, knowledge graph, billing, audit, subagent dispatch |
      | **11 commands** | `/hn-status` `/hn-store` `/hn-remember` `/hn-search` `/hn-tools` `/hn-session` `/hn-context` `/hn-plan` `/hn-summary` `/hn-restore` `/hn-purge` |
      | **3 skills** | `hypernexus`, `hypernexus-ops`, `hypernexus-workflows` (registered programmatically + shipped as SKILL.md) |
      | **Session hooks** | prompt admission (`@memory:key` expansion, suggestion feed, session priming with active goal), per-turn context injection (fast FTS recall + status + proactive tool suggestions), compaction enrichment, model-request headers, retry observability |
      | **Tool hooks** | destructive-pattern RBAC pre-check, fire-and-forget audit trail, structured file-change/file-read observations for the knowledge graph, auto-store of substantial results to L2 |
      | **Permission hook** | kernel-aware RBAC (`HYPERNEXUS_RBAC=audit\|ask\|deny`) |
      | **Shell hook** | injects `HYPERNEXUS_URL` / `HYPERNEXUS_CLIENT` into spawned shells |
      | **MCP** | auto-registers the kernel's stdio MCP server (`hypernexus mcp`) when not already configured |
      | **Worktree** | HyperNexus-managed local worktree strategy (`.hypernexus-worktree.json` linkage) |
      | **Events** | turn-end observation to the kernel audit trail |
      | **V1 shim** | `server()` export keeps OpenCode 1.x / V1-API harnesses working from the same package |
      
      ## Install
      
      ```bash
      # Project-scoped (writes <project>/.opencode/)
      node addons/opencode/install.mjs --project /path/to/project
      
      # Global (writes ~/.config/opencode/)
      node addons/opencode/install.mjs --global
      
      # Verify (fails on drift)
      node addons/opencode/install.mjs --project . --check
      
      # Remove
      node addons/opencode/install.mjs --project . --uninstall
      ```
      
      Restart OpenCode afterwards (`opencode service restart`), or just start a new session.
      
      ## Configuration
      
      | Env | Meaning |
      |---|---|
      | `HYPERNEXUS_URL` | Kernel base URL (default `http://127.0.0.1:7778`) |
      | `HYPERNEXUS_MCP_COMMAND` | Override the MCP stdio command |
      | `HYPERNEXUS_DASHBOARD_TOKEN` | Sent as `Authorization: Bearer` for write APIs when the kernel requires it |
      | `HYPERNEXUS_RBAC` | `audit` (default) / `ask` / `deny` for destructive-pattern enforcement |
      | `HYPERNEXUS_HOOK_BUDGET_MS` / `HYPERNEXUS_FAST_BUDGET_MS` / `HYPERNEXUS_SLOW_BUDGET_MS` / `HYPERNEXUS_CALL_BUDGET_MS` | Per-call budgets (400 / 2500 / 8000 / 12000) |
      
      Plugin options (via `opencode.jsonc` → `plugins: [{ package: ..., options: {...} }]`):
      `baseUrl`, `mcp` (bool), `mcpCodemode` (bool), `tools`, `commands`, `skills`, `worktree`,
      `hooks`, `suggestions`, `autoStore`, `contextInjection`, `env` — set any to `false` to disable that surface.
      
      ## Troubleshooting
      
      | Symptom | Cause | Fix |
      |---|---|---|
      | MCP shows `✗ hypernexus failed: Connection closed` after a kernel swap | killing `hypernexus` processes kills the stdio MCP child; OpenCode holds the stale state | `opencode service restart` (a config touch does **not** clear it) — the stdio server itself is healthy; verify standalone with `hypernexus.exe mcp` |
      | Kernel "down" right after `Start-Process` | boot takes 10–40s on a saturated box before binding 7778 | wait and re-check `/health`; test batteries gate on readiness now |
      | Battery timeouts during heavy load | this is a 2-core box shared with sibling autopilot agents at 100% CPU | budgets are load-tolerant (sweep 90s/call); re-run before concluding a regression |
      | `Copy-Item` fails replacing `hypernexus.exe` | running processes lock the exe | kill all `hypernexus` processes → verify gone → copy → start; never delete the staged `hypernexus-new.exe` after a failed copy |
      | Go/Node parse errors on JSON we wrote | PowerShell `Set-Content -Encoding utf8` writes a BOM | write bytes without BOM (`fs.writeFileSync` utf8, or strip bytes 239,187,191) |
      
      ## Degradation contract
      
      - Every kernel call is budgeted (`AbortSignal.timeout`) and **never throws** — a hung or
        offline kernel degrades to a short note, never stalls a turn.
      - Hot-path hooks use FTS-only search (≤400ms). Vector search is reserved for explicit tools.
      - Per-event side effects (audit, auto-store, suggestion feed) are fire-and-forget.
      - Kernel response shapes (`data.data`, `{record:…}`, JSON-string rows) are normalized in the
        adapter so tool output stays stable across kernel versions.
      
      ## Files
      
      ```
      addons/opencode/
        install.mjs                 installer (project/global/uninstall/check)
        plugin/hypernexus/index.js  the plugin (V2 setup + V1 server shim)
        plugin/hypernexus/rpc.js    shared RPC contract (tool/command/capability names)
        plugin/hypernexus/package.json
        skills/*/SKILL.md           skill docs
        agent/hypernexus-orchestrator.md
        legacy/                     retired V1-experimental adapters (kept for reference)
      ```
      
      ## Tests
      
      ```bash
      node bin/test-opencode-ext.mjs     # plugin battery: tools, commands, hooks, degradation
      node bin/test-mcp-tools.mjs       # kernel MCP server battery (tools/list + tools/call)
      node bin/test-core-noext.mjs      # core kernel battery WITHOUT any extension loaded
      ```
      
- path: addons\crush\meta-model.crushrc.snippet
  content: |
      # HyperNexus — Meta model wiring for Crush (REFERENCE SNIPPET, no secrets inside).
      # =============================================================================
      # Model: muse-spark-1.3-contributor via Meta's OpenAI-compatible endpoint.
      # Auth is read LIVE from pi's auth.json at every Crush startup (pi owns refresh,
      # so the OAuth token auto-renews; nothing is stored in this file).
      #
      # This exact block is what `node addons/crush/install.mjs --global` manages in
      # ~/.config/crush/crushrc between the hypernexus:begin/end markers. Windows note:
      # Crush resolves config shell-style, and $HOME is C:\Users\<you> — the node -e
      # reader below uses process.env.HOME with a USERPROFILE fallback via %USERPROFILE%
      # expansion so it works on both Windows and POSIX shells.
      #
      # VERIFY: crush models | findstr meta   ->  meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor
      #         crush run -m meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor "reply with exactly: CRUSH_META_OK"
      
      # --- Meta provider (OpenAI-compatible, pi-OAuth token, live-read) ---
      provider add meta --type openai-compat \
        --name "Meta" \
        --base-url "https://api.meta.ai/v1" \
        --api-key "$(node -e "const p=require('path');const h=process.env.HOME||process.env.USERPROFILE;console.log(require(p.join(h,'.pi','agent','auth.json')).meta.access)")"
      
      # --- Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor as both large and small model ---
      model add meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor \
        --name "Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor" \
        --context-window 1048576 \
        --supports-images true
      
      model large meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor
      model small meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor
      
- path: addons\shared\README.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus — Shared Extension Contract
      
      `hypernexus.manifest.json` is the **single source of truth** for the HyperNexus
      integration surface across coding harnesses. It is what lets **pi**, **MiMoCode**,
      and future harnesses (and an **autonomous orchestrator**) share one discoverable
      capability set instead of each drifting on its own.
      
      ## Why this exists
      
      Several harnesses run side-by-side, each with its own extension/plugin built by a
      different agent or session:
      
      | Harness | Adapter | API |
      |---|---|---|
      | pi | `addons/pi/hypernexus.ts` | `@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent` (ExtensionAPI) |
      | MiMoCode | `addons/mimo/plugin/hypernexus/index.js` | `@mimo-ai/plugin` (Plugin SDK) |
      | *(next)* | `addons/<harness>/` | *that harness's plugin/extension API* |
      
      Without a shared contract each adapter reimplements the kernel calls, response
      normalization, and budgets — and they drift. The manifest declares the **what**
      (capabilities + kernel bindings + budgets); each adapter supplies only the **how**
      (the harness's registration primitive).
      
      ## The rules every adapter must honor
      
      1. **Budgeted + never throws.** Every kernel call uses `AbortSignal.timeout(budgetMs)`
         and resolves to a fallback on failure. A hung kernel must never stall a turn.
      2. **Hot-path = FTS only.** Turn-critical hooks (`before_agent_start`, `context`,
         `input`) call `/api/memory/fts-search` (~15ms) within `hotPathBudgetMs` (400ms).
         Never call `/api/memory/search` (vector+embed, ~800ms) on a hot path.
      3. **Fire-and-forget side effects.** Audit logs and memory writes on per-event hooks
         are dispatched, not awaited, in the turn path.
      4. **Normalize at the boundary.** The kernel returns `data[].record` (FTS) vs `data[]`
         (search) vs `{data}` (bridge). Hide these differences behind one helper so harness
         code reads a single shape.
      5. **Kernel is the source of truth.** If an adapter is unavailable, call the kernel
         binding in the manifest directly.
      
      ## Adding a new harness
      
      1. Copy the `_template` entry in `manifest.json` → `harnesses.<yourname>`.
      2. Implement an adapter that binds each `capabilities[].id` to your harness's tool
         registration primitive. Use the `kernel` block for the call and the `contract`
         block for budgets/error handling.
      3. Map the capability id → your tool name in `capabilities[].harness.<yourname>`.
      4. Wire the `hooks` (at minimum: `context` fast-FTS injection + fire-and-forget
         suggestion feed) so behavior matches across harnesses.
      
      The capability ids are stable — an orchestrator can call `memory_search` and let the
      active harness resolve it to `hypernexus_memory_search`, or call the kernel URL directly.
      
      ## Autonomous orchestration
      
      An autonomous agent reads `manifest.json`:
      
      - **Discover** — enumerate `capabilities[]` (id, description, category).
      - **Route** — pick a capability; call `kernel.method` + `kernel.path`, or the
        harness-native tool in `harness.<active>`.
      - **Compose** — follow `orchestration.workflows` (MEM-FIRST, DISCOVER, HARVEST,
        HANDOFF, OPS) and the shared loop `RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY`.
      - **Budget** — respect `budgetMs`; never exceed `hotPathBudgetMs` on turn paths.
      - **Degrade** — on kernel failure, return a short note; never throw into the turn.
      
      ## Keeping adapters in sync
      
      The manifest is generated/validated against the adapters' tool lists. When you add or
      rename a tool in any adapter, update `capabilities[]` and the `harness{}` bindings so
      the orchestrator sees the same surface everywhere.
````

````
- path: addons\crush\README.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus for Crush — full integration
      
      Crush (`charm.land`/crush) integration for the HyperNexus AI control plane
      (Go kernel `http://127.0.0.1:7778`, dashboard 7779): persistent multi-tier
      memory (L1 scratchpad / L2 vault / L3 cold archive), semantic MCP tool
      discovery + routing, skill registry, sessions, knowledge graph, code search,
      system + billing status — plus Meta `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` OAuth wiring
      borrowed from pi (`~/.pi/agent/auth.json`, pi owns refresh).
      
      ## What you get (parity with pi / opencode / mimo / jcode)
      
      | Surface | This addon |
      |---|---|
      | MCP server | `hypernexus` stdio (`bin/hypernexus.exe mcp`, 58 tools) — discover via `mcp_list_tools`, route via `mcp_call_tool` |
      | Slash commands | 8 files in `commands/` (`/hn-status`, `/hn-search`, `/hn-store`, `/hn-session`, `/hn-context`, `/hn-summary`, `/hn-tools`, `/hn-purge`); symlink/copy into `.crush/commands/` for project use |
      | Skills | 3 skills (`hypernexus`, `hypernexus-ops`, `hypernexus-workflows`) — user-invocable + model-invoked |
      | Hooks | `hooks/hn-pre-tool.sh` (PreToolUse allow-gate, fail-open) + `hooks/hn-session.sh` (SessionStart FTS boostrap) |
      | Meta model | `meta-model.crushrc.snippet` — `meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor` via OpenAI-compat, token read LIVE from pi auth.json at every startup |
      | Overlay | `prompt-overlay.md` (discipline loop), `preferred-tools.md` (routing), `swarm-prompt-block.md` |
      | Installer | `install.mjs` — `--global` (into `~/.config/crush/`) or project (into `.crush/`), `--check`, `--uninstall` |
      
      ## Quick start
      
      ```bash
      # 1. Install everything into the global Crush config:
      node addons/crush/install.mjs --global
      
      # 2. Restart Crush (or run crush with the repo as cwd), then:
      #    /hn-status    kernel + MCP + memory + billing, one screen
      #    /hn-search    cross-session recall
      #    /hn-tools     26k+ MCP tool discovery + routing
      
      # 3. Verify the install without touching anything:
      node addons/crush/install.mjs --global --check
      
      # 4. Meta subscription model (already live in ~/.config/crush/crushrc):
      #    model shows as meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor in `crush models`
      ```
      
      ## Files
      
      - `install.mjs` — idempotent installer (`--global`, `--check`, `--uninstall`, `--target`)
      - `meta-model.crushrc.snippet` — Meta provider/model crushrc fragment (reference)
      - `mcp.crushrc.snippet` — HyperNexus MCP server fragment (reference, forward-slash command: this is what fixes the `connection closed` class of errors)
      - `prompt-overlay.md` — memory-first discipline + tool map (paste into system prompt or keep as reference)
      - `preferred-tools.md` — which HyperNexus surface to use for what
      - `swarm-prompt-block.md` — multi-agent persistence contract
      - `hooks/hn-pre-tool.sh` — PreToolUse gate (default: allow everything, fail open)
      - `hooks/hn-session.sh` — SessionStart bootstrap (fast FTS context, no vector timeouts)
      - `commands/hn-*.md` — 8 slash-command prompt files
      - `skills/*/SKILL.md` — 3 skills with `user-invocable: true`
      
- path: addons\crush\install.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /**
       * install.mjs — HyperNexus → Crush integration installer
       * ========================================================
       * Installs the full HyperNexus control-plane surface for Crush. Idempotent:
       * managed blocks are marked with `# hypernexus:begin` / `# hypernexus:end`
       * (crushrc) or `<!-- hypernexus:begin -->` / `<!-- hypernexus:end -->` (markdown)
       * and can be re-run or removed safely.
       *
       * Modes:
       *   node addons/crush/install.mjs               install into this project (.crush/)
       *   node addons/crush/install.mjs --global      install into ~/.config/crush/
       *   node addons/crush/install.mjs --check       verify installation (exit 1 on gaps)
       *   node addons/crush/install.mjs --uninstall   remove managed blocks/files
       *
       * What it installs (global → ~/.config/crush/  |  project → .crush/):
       *   crushrc block        Meta provider (pi-OAuth live-read) + hypernexus MCP
       *                        server (FORWARD-SLASH exe path — backslashes break the
       *                        MCP handshake) + skills path + hooks + permissions
       *   hooks dir has hn-pre-tool and hn-session scripts
       *   skills (3): hypernexus, hypernexus-ops, hypernexus-workflows
       *                        (each user-invocable: crush exposes them as /skills)
       *   commands (8 prompt files, reference; symlink or copy
       *                        the ones you want into .crush/commands/ — crush loads
       *                        project commands from there automatically)
       *   prompt-overlay.md    discipline loop + tool map (managed block)
       *   preferred-tools.md   routing preferences (managed block)
       *   (+ global) skills mirror into ~/.config/crush/skills/ so every project sees them
       *
       * The installer never stores secrets: the Meta token is read LIVE from
       * ~/.pi/agent/auth.json at every Crush startup (pi owns OAuth refresh).
       * After install: restart Crush so the new crushrc block + MCP server load.
       */
      
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import path from "node:path";
      import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
      
      const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
      const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..");
      const HOME = process.env.USERPROFILE || process.env.HOME || "";
      
      const MODE = process.argv.includes("--uninstall")
        ? "uninstall"
        : process.argv.includes("--check")
          ? "check"
          : "install";
      const GLOBAL = process.argv.includes("--global");
      
      function argValue(flag) {
        const i = process.argv.indexOf(flag);
        if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] && !process.argv[i + 1].startsWith("--")) return process.argv[i + 1];
        const eq = process.argv.find((a) => a.startsWith(flag + "="));
        return eq ? eq.slice(flag.length + 1) : "";
      }
      const TARGET_OVERRIDE = argValue("--target");
      const TARGET = TARGET_OVERRIDE || (GLOBAL ? path.join(HOME, ".config", "crush") : path.join(REPO_ROOT, ".crush"));
      
      const MD_BEGIN = "<!-- hypernexus:begin";
      const MD_END = "<!-- hypernexus:end -->";
      const RC_BEGIN = "# hypernexus:begin";
      const RC_END = "# hypernexus:end";
      
      // ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function readIfExists(p) {
        try {
          return fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      }
      
      function upsertManagedBlock(filePath, blockText, begin = MD_BEGIN, end = MD_END) {
        const block = blockText.trim();
        const existing = readIfExists(filePath) ?? "";
        const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(begin);
        const endIdx = existing.indexOf(end);
        let next;
        if (beginIdx >= 0 && endIdx > beginIdx) {
          next = existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + block + existing.slice(endIdx + end.length);
        } else {
          next = existing.trimEnd() + (existing.trimEnd() ? "\n\n" : "") + block + "\n";
        }
        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, next, "utf8");
        return existing !== next;
      }
      
      function removeManagedBlock(filePath, begin = MD_BEGIN, end = MD_END) {  const existing = readIfExists(filePath);
        if (existing === null) return false;
        const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(begin);
        const endIdx = existing.indexOf(end);
        if (beginIdx < 0 || endIdx < beginIdx) return false;
        const next = (existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + existing.slice(endIdx + end.length)).replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n");
        if (next.trim() === "") {
          fs.rmSync(filePath, { force: true });
          return true;
        }
        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, next, "utf8");
        return true;
      }
      
      /** Forward-slash exe path — backslashes break Crush's shell expansion of the MCP command. */
      function resolveExe() {
        const exe = process.platform === "win32" ? "hypernexus.exe" : "hypernexus";
        const candidate = path.join(REPO_ROOT, "bin", exe);
        if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) return candidate.split(path.sep).join("/");
        return exe;
      }
      
      function crushrcBlock(exe) {
        const wsRoot = REPO_ROOT.split(path.sep).join("/");
        const skillsDir = GLOBAL ? "$HOME/.config/crush/skills" : path.join(TARGET, "skills").split(path.sep).join("/");
        const hooksDir = GLOBAL ? "$HOME/.config/crush/hooks" : path.join(TARGET, "hooks").split(path.sep).join("/");
        return `${RC_BEGIN} (managed by HyperNexus addons/crush/install.mjs — do not edit inside this block)
      # --- Meta provider (OpenAI-compatible, pi-OAuth live-read, pi owns refresh) ---
      provider add meta --type openai-compat \\
        --name "Meta" \\
        --base-url "https://api.meta.ai/v1" \\
        --api-key "$(node -e "const p=require('path');const h=process.env.HOME||process.env.USERPROFILE;console.log(require(p.join(h,'.pi','agent','auth.json')).meta.access)")"
      
      # --- Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor as both large and small model ---
      # muse-spark is a thinking model: max_tokens must cover reasoning + answer.
      # Pi-verified floor for short replies is max-tokens 1024 (32 tokens truncates
      # mid-reasoning and yields empty content). The model requests below reflect that.
      model add meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor \\
        --name "Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor" \\
        --context-window 1048576 \\
        --default-max-tokens 131072 \\
        --can-reason true \\
        --supports-images true \\
        --reasoning-effort high
      
      model large meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor --max-tokens 131072 --reasoning-effort high
      model small meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor --max-tokens 16384
      
      # --- HyperNexus MCP: full control-plane tool access (58 tools) ---
      # NOTE: forward slashes are REQUIRED — backslash paths break the MCP handshake.
      mcp add hypernexus \\
        --command "${exe}" \\
        --args mcp \\
        --env HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT "${wsRoot}" \\
        --env HYPERNEXUS_URL "http://127.0.0.1:7778" \\
        --timeout 30
      
      # --- Skills ---
      option skill-path "${skillsDir}"
      
      # --- Hooks: PreToolUse allow-gate + SessionStart FTS bootstrap (fast, fail-open) ---

- path: addons\opencode\install.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /**
       * HyperNexus OpenCode installer
       * =============================
       *
       * Installs the HyperNexus plugin + skills + orchestrator agent into an OpenCode
       * project (.opencode/) or the global OpenCode config (~/.config/opencode/), and
       * registers the kernel's stdio MCP server (`hypernexus mcp`) when missing.
       *
       * Usage:
       *   node install.mjs [--project <dir>] [--global] [--uninstall] [--check] [--no-mcp]
       *
       * Modes:
       *   (default)   install into <dir>/.opencode/   (dir defaults to cwd)
       *   --global    install into ~/.config/opencode/
       *   --uninstall remove installed artifacts
       *   --check     verify installation state and exit non-zero on drift
       *   --no-mcp    skip MCP server registration
       *
       * All writes are UTF-8 WITHOUT BOM (Go/Node parsers reject BOM).
       */
      
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import path from "node:path";
      import os from "node:os";
      import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
      import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
      
      const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
      const SRC_PLUGIN = path.join(HERE, "plugin", "hypernexus");
      const SRC_SKILLS = path.join(HERE, "skills");
      const SRC_AGENT = path.join(HERE, "agent", "hypernexus-orchestrator.md");
      
      const args = process.argv.slice(2);
      const has = (flag) => args.includes(flag);
      const optValue = (flag) => {
        const i = args.indexOf(flag);
        return i >= 0 && args[i + 1] && !args[i + 1].startsWith("--") ? args[i + 1] : null;
      };
      
      const globalMode = has("--global");
      const uninstall = has("--uninstall");
      const check = has("--check");
      const noMcp = has("--no-mcp");
      
      const projectDir = path.resolve(optValue("--project") || process.cwd());
      const opencodeDir = globalMode ? path.join(os.homedir(), ".config", "opencode") : path.join(projectDir, ".opencode");
      
      const PLUGIN_DEST = path.join(opencodeDir, "plugins", "hypernexus");
      const SKILLS_DEST = path.join(opencodeDir, "skills");
      const AGENT_DEST = path.join(opencodeDir, "agent", "hypernexus-orchestrator.md");
      
      function log(msg) {
        console.log(`[hypernexus-install] ${msg}`);
      }
      
      function writeUtf8(file, content) {
        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
        fs.writeFileSync(file, content, "utf8"); // utf8 → no BOM
      }
      
      function copyUtf8(src, dest) {
        writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, "utf8"));
      }
      
      // ── JSONC-safe config editing ────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function stripJsonc(text) {
        let out = "";
        let inStr = false;
        let escaped = false;
        for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
          const c = text[i];
          if (inStr) {
            out += c;
            if (escaped) escaped = false;
            else if (c === "\\") escaped = true;
            else if (c === '"') inStr = false;
            continue;
          }
          if (c === '"') {
            inStr = true;
            out += c;
            continue;
          }
          if (c === "/" && text[i + 1] === "/") {
            while (i < text.length && text[i] !== "\n") i++;
            out += "\n";
            continue;
          }
          if (c === "/" && text[i + 1] === "*") {
            i += 2;
            while (i < text.length && !(text[i] === "*" && text[i + 1] === "/")) i++;
            i++;
            continue;
          }
          out += c;
        }
        return out.replace(/,\s*([}\]])/g, "$1"); // trailing commas
      }
      
      function findConfigFile() {
        for (const name of ["opencode.jsonc", "opencode.json"]) {
          const p = path.join(opencodeDir, name);
          if (fs.existsSync(p)) return p;
        }
        return path.join(opencodeDir, "opencode.jsonc");
      }
      
      function mcpCommand() {
        const override = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_MCP_COMMAND;
        if (override) return override.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        const exe = process.platform === "win32" ? "hypernexus.exe" : "hypernexus";
        for (const c of [
          path.join(projectDir, "bin", exe),
          path.join(HERE, "..", "..", "bin", exe),
          path.join(os.homedir(), ".hypernexus", "bin", exe),
        ]) {
          if (fs.existsSync(c)) return [c, "mcp"];
        }
        return [process.platform === "win32" ? "hypernexus.exe" : "hypernexus", "mcp"];
      }
      
      function registerMcpConfig() {
        // Prefer the OpenCode CLI (preserves unrelated config), fall back to direct edit.
        const cmd = mcpCommand();
        try {
          execFileSync("opencode", ["mcp", "add", "hypernexus", ...(globalMode ? ["--global"] : []), "--", ...cmd], {
            stdio: "inherit",
            cwd: globalMode ? projectDir : projectDir,
          });
          log(`MCP server 'hypernexus' registered via opencode CLI (${cmd.join(" ")})`);
          return;
        } catch {
          /* fall through to config edit */
        }
        const file = findConfigFile();
        let config = {};
        if (fs.existsSync(file)) {
          try {
            config = JSON.parse(stripJsonc(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")));
          } catch (err) {
            log(`WARNING: could not parse ${file}: ${err.message}. Skipping MCP registration.`);
            return;
          }
        }
        config["$schema"] = config["$schema"] || "https://opencode.ai/config.json";
        config.mcp = config.mcp || {};
        config.mcp.servers = config.mcp.servers || {};
        if (config.mcp.servers.hypernexus) {
````

````
---
name: hypernexus
description: "HyperNexus AI control plane — persistent L2 vector memory, semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, billing, and agent orchestration. Use when the task benefits from cross-session memory (scratchpad, facts, observations), tool discovery across MCP servers, knowledge ingestion, system health checks, or routing through the HyperNexus Go kernel on port 7778. Trigger on mentions of 'hypernexus', 'HN', 'scratchpad', 'cross-session memory', 'MCP tools', 'tool discovery', 'knowledge graph', 'agent memory', or 'control plane'."
---

# HyperNexus Integration (OpenCode)

HyperNexus is a local AI control plane running a Go kernel on port 7778 (dashboard 7779) with persistent multi-tier memory (L1 scratchpad, L2 vector store, L3 cold archive), semantic tool discovery across 60+ MCP servers / 26,000+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, and agent orchestration.

## When to Use

- Start of any multi-step task → harvest prior context (`hypernexus_context_harvest` / `hypernexus_memory_search`)
- After a decision, gotcha, or non-obvious finding → store it (`hypernexus_memory_store` / `hypernexus_record_observation`)
- When you need a capability you don't have → discover tools (`hypernexus_mcp_search` / `hypernexus_tool_discover`) then route (`hypernexus_mcp_call`)
- When the user asks about system health, billing, or the repo → `hypernexus_system_overview`, `hypernexus_billing_status`, `hypernexus_knowledge_graph`

## OpenCode Tools (registered by the plugin)

| Tool | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `hypernexus_memory_search` | Keyword + semantic search over persistent memory |
| `hypernexus_memory_fts_search` | FAST keyword search (use inside tight loops) |
| `hypernexus_memory_vector_search` | Semantic recall incl. L3 cold archive |
| `hypernexus_memory_list` | List recent memories |
| `hypernexus_memory_store` | Store a fact / decision |
| `hypernexus_memory_delete` | Delete by id or tag fan-out |
| `hypernexus_memory_relate` | Link memories in the knowledge graph |
| `hypernexus_memory_stats` | Tier counts: vault / scratchpad / relations |
| `hypernexus_record_observation` | Record a structured observation (discovery/decision/progress/warning/fix) |
| `hypernexus_knowledge_graph` | Entities/relations graph |
| `hypernexus_context_harvest` | Pull relevant L2 context for a task |
| `hypernexus_scratchpad` | L1 working memory get/set/append |
| `hypernexus_session_search` | Search imported sessions |
| `hypernexus_session_context` | Session bootstrap: goal & cross-session links |
| `hypernexus_session_handoff` | Write/pick up handoff artifacts |
| `hypernexus_session_list` | List recent sessions across harnesses |
| `hypernexus_mcp_search` | Search live MCP tool inventory |
| `hypernexus_mcp_status` / `hypernexus_mcp_servers` / `hypernexus_mcp_tools` | MCP bridge status / servers / tools |
| `hypernexus_tool_search` | Semantic tool discovery (26k+ catalog) |
| `hypernexus_tool_discover` | Native router discovery (L1+L2+L3) |
| `hypernexus_suggest_tools` | Proactive tool suggestions from conversation |
| `hypernexus_mcp_call` | Route a call to a downstream MCP tool |
| `hypernexus_code_search` | symbols / definitions / references / files |
| `hypernexus_repomap` | Ranked repo map with symbol summaries |
| `hypernexus_workflow` / `hypernexus_orchestrate` | Engineering workflows / orchestration loop |
| `hypernexus_browser` | Scrape URL or check browser-service status |
| `hypernexus_skill_search` / `hypernexus_skill_manage` | Skill registry |
| `hypernexus_status` / `hypernexus_system_overview` | Kernel + memory + MCP health |
| `hypernexus_billing_status` | Provider quotas, depletion, fallback chain |
| `hypernexus_audit_log` | Compliance audit trail |
| `hypernexus_subagent` | Dispatch sub-tasks to orchestration |

## HTTP API (port 7778)

  -d '{"title":"...","content":"...","tags":["..."],"namespace":"project"}'
curl -s "http://localhost:7778/api/memory/search?query=<term>&limit=<n>"
curl -s "http://localhost:7778/api/memory/fts-search?q=<term>&limit=<n>"   # fast path

hn-context.md
hn-purge.md
hn-search.md

hn-status.md
hn-store.md
hn-summary.md
````

```
- path: addons\crush\skills\hypernexus\SKILL.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hypernexus
      description: "HyperNexus AI control plane — persistent L2 vector memory, semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, billing, and agent orchestration. Use when the task benefits from cross-session memory (scratchpad, facts, observations), tool discovery across MCP servers, knowledge ingestion, system health checks, or routing through the HyperNexus Go kernel on port 7778. Trigger on mentions of 'hypernexus', 'HN', 'scratchpad', 'cross-session memory', 'MCP tools', 'tool discovery', 'knowledge graph', 'agent memory', or 'control plane'."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Integration (Crush)
      
      HyperNexus is a local AI control plane running a Go kernel on port 7778 (dashboard 7779) with persistent multi-tier memory (L1 scratchpad, L2 vector store, L3 cold archive), semantic tool discovery across 60+ MCP servers / 26,000+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, and agent orchestration.
      
      ## When to Use
      
      - Start of any multi-step task → recall context (`search_memory`, fast FTS `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=`, scratchpad `memory_scratchpad_get`)
      - After a decision, gotcha, or non-obvious finding → store it (`add_memory` + `memory_extract_relations`)
      - When you need a capability you don't have → discover tools (`mcp_list_servers` / `mcp_list_tools`, semantic `GET /api/mcp/tools/search?query=`) then route (`mcp_call_tool`)
      - When the user asks about system health, billing, or the repo → `system_status`, `billing_status`, `repomap`, `codebase_search`
      
      ## Crush Tools (via the hypernexus MCP server, 58 tools)
      
      The MCP server exposes these exact tool names. Prefer the MCP tool; the
      HTTP fallback works even when MCP is unreachable.
      
      | MCP tool | Purpose | HTTP fallback |
      |---|---|---|
      | `search_memory` | Semantic + keyword memory search (vector+FTS; slow ~6s on cold start, fast after warmup) | `GET /api/memory/search?query=` / `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=` (fast, ~15ms) |
      | `add_memory` | Store a fact / decision (content, tags, category) | `POST /api/memory/add` |
      | `delete_memory` | Delete a memory by id | `DELETE /api/memory/delete?id=` |
      | `memory_stats` | Tier counts: vault / scratchpad / relations | `GET /api/memory/stats` |
      | `memory_scratchpad_get` / `memory_scratchpad_set` / `memory_scratchpad_append` | L1 working memory (goal, persona, human) | `GET/POST /api/memory/scratchpad/*` |
      | `memory_extract_relations` / `memory_extract_relations_batch` | Knowledge graph: entities + relations | `POST /api/memory/extract-relations` |
      | `mcp_list_servers` | Live MCP server inventory | `GET /api/mcp/servers` |
      | `mcp_list_tools` | Tool inventory across connected servers | `GET /api/mcp/tools` |
      | `mcp_status` | Bridge status: connected count, fallback | `GET /api/mcp/status` |
      | `mcp_server_test` | Probe a downstream server (tools/list, ping) | — |
      | `mcp_call_tool` | Route a call (serverName, toolName, arguments) | `POST /api/agent/tool` |
      | `system_status` | Kernel liveness + version | `GET /health` |
      | `billing_status` | Provider quotas, depletion, fallback chain | `GET /api/billing/status` |
      | `get_system_stats` | OS / runtime metrics | — |
      | `codebase_search` | symbols / definitions / references (empty result when kernel catalog has no match) | `GET /api/mcp/native/search?query=` |
      | `codebase_outline` | Symbol outline for a file | — |
      | `repomap` | Ranked repo map with symbol summaries | — |
      | `read` / `write` / `edit` / `find` / `grep` / `ls` (+ `read_file` / `write_file` / `search` / `run_shell_command` / `bash` / `apply_search_replace`) | File + shell access through the kernel | local tools |
      | `code_interpreter` | Run python/node snippets (stateful session) | — |
      | `add_bookmark` | Save URL with title + tags | — |
      | `jira_create_issue` / `confluence_search` | Atlassian integration | — |
      | `cloud_troubleshoot` / `generate_devops_pipeline` | Ops generators | — |
      | `install_mcp_server` | Install an MCP server via npx | — |
      | `launch_webview` | Open a system browser window | — |
      | `download_llamafile` | Fetch a standalone model binary | — |
      | `list_processes` / `kill_process` | Process management (Windows) | — |
      
      [read_files: showing lines 1-50 of 96. Use code_search to locate the part you need and read a window around it, or call read_files again with offset=51 to continue.]
- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-status.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-status
      description: "Report HyperNexus kernel health, MCP bridge inventory, memory stats, and billing from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-status', 'hypernexus status', or 'kernel health'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-status
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Report HyperNexus control-plane status, without taking any action:
      
      1. Kernel liveness and version — `hypernexus MCP system_status` (fallback:
         `curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/health`).
      2. MCP bridge inventory — `hypernexus MCP mcp_status` (servers connected, tool count).
      3. Memory tiers — `hypernexus MCP memory_stats` (L2 vault / scratchpad / relations).
      4. Billing — `hypernexus MCP billing_status` (quotas, depleted models, fallback chain).
      
      Present as a short bullet list. If the kernel is unreachable, say so plainly and point
      at `bin\hypernexus.exe serve` — never show a mock state.
      
- path: addons\crush\hooks\hn-session.sh
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env bash
      # hn-session.sh — HyperNexus SessionStart bootstrap for Crush (fast, no network stall).
      # =============================================================================
      # Runs at session start. Fetches the freshest ~5 L2 memory rows via the FTS
      # endpoint (~15ms; NEVER the vector search, which times out past the hook budget)
      # plus the scratchpad `goal` key, and injects them as context.
      # Fail-open: any error prints nothing and exits 0 (never brick a session).
      HN_BASE="${HYPERNEXUS_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:7778}"
      GOAL=""
      if command -v curl.exe >/dev/null 2>&1; then _curl=curl.exe; else _curl=curl; fi
      if command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then _py=python3; else _py=python; fi
      # Python may be a broken Microsoft Store stub on Windows — verify it runs.
      if [ -n "$_py" ]; then
        if ! "$_py" --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then _py=""; fi
      fi
      # Never throws: without a working python, skip the live memory boost.
      if [ -n "$_py" ]; then
      MEMO="$("$_curl" -s -m 3 "$HN_BASE/api/memory/fts-search?q=session%20handoff%20goal&limit=3" 2>/dev/null | "$_py" -c "
      import json,sys
      try:
          d=json.load(sys.stdin)
          rows=d.get('data',d) if isinstance(d,dict) else d
          out=[]
          for r in (rows or [])[:3]:
              rec=r.get('record',r) if isinstance(r,dict) else {}
              c=str(rec.get('content',''))[:220].replace(chr(10),' ')
              if c: out.append('- '+c)
          print('\n'.join(out))
      except Exception:
          pass
      " 2>/dev/null)"
      else
      MEMO=""
      fi
      CTX="HyperNexus discipline for this session: RECALL before re-deriving (search project memory via the hypernexus MCP server tools memory_search/search_memory, fast path /api/memory/fts-search) -> DISCOVER via mcp_list_servers + mcp_list_tools on capability gaps -> PERSIST non-obvious outcomes with add_memory (tags: topic,kind) + memory_extract_relations -> VERIFY with system_status/billing_status around production changes. MCP server 'hypernexus' (stdio, 58 tools) exposes the full tool plane; skills hypernexus / hypernexus-ops / hypernexus-workflows are on the skill path; commands /hn-status /hn-search /hn-store /hn-session /hn-context /hn-summary /hn-tools /hn-purge are available."
      if [ -n "$MEMO" ]; then CTX="$CTX Recent L2 memory:\n$MEMO"; fi
      # Emit JSON. Prefer python for safe escaping; fall back to a static heredoc.
      if [ -n "$_py" ]; then
      CTX="$CTX" "$_py" -c "
      import json,os
      print(json.dumps({'hookSpecificOutput':{'additionalContext':os.environ.get('CTX','')}}))
      "
      else
      cat <<'JSON'
      {"hookSpecificOutput":{"additionalContext":"HyperNexus discipline for this session: RECALL before re-deriving (search project memory via hypernexus MCP memory_search/search_memory, fast path /api/memory/fts-search) -> DISCOVER via mcp_list_servers + mcp_list_tools on capability gaps -> PERSIST non-obvious outcomes with add_memory (tags: topic,kind) -> VERIFY with system_status around production changes. MCP server 'hypernexus' (stdio) exposes the full tool plane; skills hypernexus / hypernexus-ops / hypernexus-workflows are on the skill path."}}
      JSON
      fi
      exit 0
      
```

````
- path: addons\shared\hypernexus.manifest.json
  content: |
      {
        "$schema": "https://hypernexus.site/schemas/extension-manifest.v1.json",
        "name": "hypernexus",
        "displayName": "HyperNexus",
        "version": "1.0.21",
        "description": "Universal AI control plane integration: persistent multi-tier memory (L1/L2/L3), semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, skill registry, code search, and agent orchestration.",
        "kind": "harness-extension",
        "generatedFor": "multi-harness + autonomous-orchestration",
        "kernel": {
          "baseUrl": "http://127.0.0.1:7778",
          "health": "/health",
          "overrideEnv": [
            "HYPERNEXUS_URL",
            "HYPERNEXUS_TRPC_UPSTREAM"
          ],
          "mcp": {
            "command": "hypernexus.exe",
            "args": [
              "mcp"
            ],
            "transport": "stdio",
            "toolNamespace": "mcp__hypernexus"
          }
        },
        "harnesses": {
          "pi": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent (ExtensionAPI)",
            "adapter": "addons/pi/hypernexus.ts",
            "install": "~/.pi/agent/extensions/hypernexus.ts",
            "surfaces": [
              "tools",
              "commands",
              "shortcuts",
              "hooks",
              "widget",
              "event-bus"
            ]
          },
          "mimo": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "@mimo-ai/plugin (Plugin SDK)",
            "adapter": "addons/mimo/plugin/hypernexus/index.js",
            "install": "~/.config/mimocode/",
            "surfaces": [
              "tools",
              "hooks",
              "commands",
              "agents",
              "tui"
            ]
          },
          "opencode": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "OpenCode V2 Plugin API (Plugin.define/setup ctx) + V1 server() shim",
            "adapter": "addons/opencode/plugin/hypernexus/index.js",
            "install": "<project>/.opencode/plugins/hypernexus/ or ~/.config/opencode/plugins/hypernexus/",
            "installer": "addons/opencode/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "tools",
              "commands",
              "skills",
              "session-hooks",
              "tool-hooks",
              "permission-hook",
              "shell-hook",
              "mcp",
              "worktree",
              "events",
              "agent"
            ],
            "notes": "36 tools + 11 /hn-* commands + 3 skills; auto-registers `hypernexus mcp` stdio server; hot-path FTS context injection; @memory:key prompt expansion; RBAC via HYPERNEXUS_RBAC."
          },
          "jcode": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Jcode MCP manager (mcpServers stdio config) + skills / prompt-overlay / preferred-tools / swarm-prompt surfaces",
            "adapter": "addons/jcode/",
            "install": "<project>/.jcode/ or ~/.jcode/ — mcp.json, skills/, prompt-overlay.md, preferred-tools.md, swarm-prompt.md block",
            "installer": "addons/jcode/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "mcp",
              "skills",
              "prompt-overlay",
              "preferred-tools",
              "swarm-prompt"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via `hypernexus mcp` stdio server registered in .jcode/mcp.json (toolNamespace mcp__hypernexus). 3 skills + discipline overlay + tool routing + swarm persistence block. Core works without the extension over HTTP. Stdio server serves last-good inventory on slow kernel (15s TTL cache + retry) and dispatches requests concurrently."
          },
          "droid": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Factory Droid plugin (.factory-plugin/plugin.json) + MCP stdio + hooks + skills/commands/droids/output-styles",
            "adapter": "addons/droid/plugin/hypernexus/",
            "install": "addons/droid/marketplace/ via `node addons/droid/install.mjs` then `droid plugin marketplace add <dir>` + `droid plugin install hypernexus@marketplace --scope user`",
            "installer": "addons/droid/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "mcp",
              "plugin-bundle",
              "commands",
              "skills",
              "hooks",
              "droids",
              "output-style"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via `hypernexus mcp` stdio server registered in plugin mcp.json (~/.factory/mcp.json). 3 skills + 11 /hn-* commands + 2 droids + 1 output style. 8 lifecycle hooks (SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit/PreToolUse/PostToolUse/Stop/SessionEnd/PreCompact/SubagentStop) as Node scripts (no bash dependency on Windows); hot-path FTS recall; @memory:key expansion; RBAC via HYPERNEXUS_RBAC (audit/ask/deny). Meta muse-spark-1.3-contributor runs natively via apiKeyHelper (addons/droid/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs mints Model API key from pi auth.json identity)."
          },
          "_template": {
            "status": "template",
            "api": "<harness plugin/extension API>",
            "adapter": "addons/<harness>/",
            "install": "<harness install dir>",
            "note": "Copy this entry to add a new harness. Bind each capability below to your harness's tool-registration primitive. Keep budgets + never-throw contract identical."
          },
          "codewhale": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Codewhale MCP manager (mcpServers stdio config) + Agent Plugins v1.0.0 bundle (commands/skills/mcp.json) + skills / prompt-overlay surfaces",
            "adapter": "addons/codewhale/",
            "install": "<project>/.codewhale/ or ~/.codewhale/ — mcp.json, plugins/hypernexus/ bundle (plugin.json + plugin.toml + commands/ + skills/), skills/ mirror, AGENTS.md managed block",
            "installer": "addons/codewhale/install.mjs",

- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-search.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-search
      description: "Search HyperNexus L2 memory (semantic + keyword, optional tag/category filters) from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-search' or 'search memory'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-search <query> [--tag t] [--category c] [--limit n] [--cold]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Search cross-session memory and summarize what matters:
      
      1. Call `hypernexus MCP search_memory` with `query`, `limit` (default 8), and the
         `tag`/`category` filters when given. Add `include_cold: true` when `--cold` is set.
      2. If the query yields nothing, retry once with fewer/simpler keywords — keyword
         matching needs natural-language tokens (a hyphenated single token rarely matches).
      3. Summarize hits grouped by relevance; include ids so follow-up
         `hypernexus MCP delete_memory` calls are possible. Empty query → recent memories.
      
      Never stall: if the kernel is down, report it and continue without memory context.
      
- path: addons\opencode\skills\hypernexus-ops\SKILL.md
  content: |
      ---
      name: hypernexus-ops
      description: "Operate and verify the HyperNexus kernel from OpenCode: health checks, MCP bridge inventory, billing/quotas, RBAC audit trail, and verify-before/after discipline for deployments and incidents. Trigger on 'kernel health', 'mcp servers', 'billing', 'quotas', 'audit', 'deploy verification', or 'hypernexus ops'."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Ops
      
      ## Tools
      
      - `hypernexus_status` / `hypernexus_system_overview` — health before and after production changes
      - `hypernexus_mcp_status` / `hypernexus_mcp_servers` / `hypernexus_mcp_tools` — MCP bridge inventory
      - `hypernexus_mcp_search` / `hypernexus_tool_search` — find the right downstream tool
      - `hypernexus_billing_status` — quotas, depleted models, fallback chain
      - `hypernexus_audit_log` — record compliance-relevant actions
      
      ## Verify loop (OPS workflow)
      
      1. VERIFY before change: `hypernexus_status`
      2. Act (with your normal tools)
      3. VERIFY after change: `hypernexus_status`
      4. PERSIST: `hypernexus_memory_store` with the outcome and tags `["ops", "deploy"]`
      
      ## Health endpoints
      
      ```bash
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/health                  # kernel liveness + version
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/system/overview     # full system view
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/mcp/status          # MCP bridge status
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/billing/status      # provider billing
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/billing/fallback-chain
      ```
      
      ## RBAC
      
      The plugin's permission hook enforces HyperNexus RBAC policy for destructive patterns:
      
      - `HYPERNEXUS_RBAC=audit` (default) — log only, never block
      - `HYPERNEXUS_RBAC=ask` — escalate destructive patterns to a permission prompt
      - `HYPERNEXUS_RBAC=deny` — deny destructive patterns outright
````

```
- path: addons\droid\bin\meta-muse-key.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /*
       * meta-muse-key.mjs — mint a Meta Model API key from the pi auth.json identity token.
       *
       * Mirrors pi's own Meta OAuth flow (see @earendil-works/pi-ai dist/auth/oauth/meta.js):
       *   1. Read identity token from ~/.pi/agent/auth.json -> meta.refresh
       *   2. POST https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key with Authorization: Bearer <identity>
       *   3. Print ONLY the minted api_key to stdout.
       *
       * Droid calls this as an `apiKeyHelper`: stdout is used as the credential at
       * request time, refreshed on a TTL and after a 401. Nothing secret is logged.
       *
       * Usage:
       *   node meta-muse-key.mjs            # prints minted key
       *   node meta-muse-key.mjs --check     # exit 0 if mint works, 1 otherwise
       *
       * Env overrides:
       *   META_AUTH_PATH      path to pi auth.json (default ~/.pi/agent/auth.json)
       *   META_KEY_MINT_URL   default https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key
       */
      
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import os from "node:os";
      import path from "node:path";
      
      const AUTH_PATH =
        process.env.META_AUTH_PATH || path.join(os.homedir(), ".pi", "agent", "auth.json");
      const MINT_URL = process.env.META_KEY_MINT_URL || "https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key";
      const TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
      
      function fail(msg) {
        process.stderr.write(`[meta-muse-key] ${msg}\n`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      function readIdentityToken() {
        let raw;
        try {
          raw = fs.readFileSync(AUTH_PATH, "utf8");
        } catch (e) {
          fail(`cannot read auth file ${AUTH_PATH}: ${e.message}. Run /login meta in pi first.`);
        }
        let data;
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(raw);
        } catch {
          fail(`auth file ${AUTH_PATH} is not valid JSON.`);
        }
        const cred = data?.meta;
        if (!cred || cred.type !== "oauth" || typeof cred.refresh !== "string" || !cred.refresh) {
          fail(
            `no Meta OAuth credential in ${AUTH_PATH} (expected .meta.refresh). Run /login meta in pi to sign in.`
          );
        }
        return cred.refresh;
      }
      
      async function mintApiKey(identityToken, signal) {
        const res = await fetch(MINT_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            Accept: "application/json",
            Authorization: `Bearer ${identityToken}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "x-api-version": "1.0.0",
          },
          body: "{}",
          signal,
        });
        let json = null;
        try {
          json = await res.json();
        } catch { /* non-JSON body */ }
        if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
          fail(
            `Meta session expired (HTTP ${res.status}). Identity tokens are not renewable — run /login meta in pi to sign in again.`
          );
        }
        if (!res.ok) {
          const detail = json && typeof json === "object"
            ? ["error_description", "detail", "message", "error"].map((k) => json[k]).find((v) => typeof v === "string" && v.trim())
            : null;
          fail(`Meta API key mint failed (HTTP ${res.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}.`);
        }
        const apiKey = json?.api_key;
        if (typeof apiKey !== "string" || !apiKey) {
          const actionUrl = typeof json?.action_url === "string" ? json.action_url : null;
          fail(`Meta did not issue an API key.${actionUrl ? ` Complete setup at ${actionUrl}` : ""}`);
        }
        return apiKey;
      }
      
      const checkOnly = process.argv.includes("--check");
      const identity = readIdentityToken();
      try {
        const key = await mintApiKey(identity, AbortSignal.timeout(TIMEOUT_MS));
        if (checkOnly) {
          process.stderr.write("[meta-muse-key] mint OK (key hidden)\n");
        } else {
          process.stdout.write(key);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (e?.name === "TimeoutError" || e?.name === "AbortError") fail(`mint request timed out after ${TIMEOUT_MS}ms.`);
        fail(e.message || String(e));
      }

PreToolUse allow-gate (destructive-pattern deny) + SessionStart FTS bootstrap (fail-open, no python required). Meta meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor via pi-OAuth live-read (pi owns refresh). Core works without the MCP server over HTTP http://127.0.0.1:7778."

Meta muse-spark-1.3-contributor via META_MUSE_API_KEY env ref (pi owns OAuth refresh; scripts/sync-meta-oauth-reasonix.ps1). Core works without the extension over HTTP."
          }
        },
        "contract": {
          "hotPathBudgetMs": 400,
          "defaultBudgetMs": 2500,
          "rules": [
            "Every kernel call is budgeted (AbortSignal.timeout) and never throws — a hung kernel degrades to a short note, never stalls a turn.",
            "Hot-path hooks (before_agent_start / context / input) use FTS-only search (/api/memory/fts-search, ~15ms) within hotPathBudgetMs. Never call the full /api/memory/search (~800ms vector+embed) on a hot path.",
            "Side-effect writes on per-event hooks (audit log, memory store) are fire-and-forget — do not await them in the turn path.",
            "Explicit user-invoked tools may use the full semantic search and defaultBudgetMs.",
            "Normalize kernel response shapes at the adapter boundary so harness code never reads `data[].record` vs `data[]` differences."
          ]
        },
        "capabilities": [
          {
            "id": "memory_store",
            "name": "Store memory",
            "category": "memory",
            "description": "Persist a fact / observation / decision to L2 vector memory.",
            "kernel": {
              "method": "POST",
              "path": "/api/memory/add",
              "body": {
                "content": "<string|json>",
                "tags": [],
                "category": "general"

            },
```

````
- path: addons\crush\prompt-overlay.md
  content: |
      ## HyperNexus Discipline (Crush prompt overlay)
      
      Every turn in this workspace runs memory-first through the HyperNexus kernel
      (`http://127.0.0.1:7778`; MCP server `hypernexus`, 58 tools via stdio):
      
      ```
      RECALL -> DISCOVER (if gap) -> EXECUTE -> PERSIST -> VERIFY
      ```
      
      - **RECALL** — before re-deriving anything: FTS fast path
        (`/api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8`, ~15ms) for hot loops, semantic
        `search_memory` for depth. Include scratchpad `goal`.
      - **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` →
        semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search`, then route with `mcp_call_tool`. Persist what
        worked (`add_memory`, tags `tool-routing`).
      - **PERSIST** — non-obvious outcomes immediately: `add_memory`
        (tags `topic,kind`) + `memory_extract_relations` for entities/relations.
      - **VERIFY** — around production changes: `system_status` + `billing_status`
        before and after; never present mock state when the kernel is down.
      
      ### Tool map (MCP `hypernexus` server)
      
      | Need | Tool |
      |---|---|
      | fast recall | `memory_scratchpad_get/set/append`, FTS search endpoint |
      | durable recall | `search_memory`, `add_memory`, `delete_memory`, `memory_stats` |
      | graph | `memory_extract_relations`, `memory_extract_relations_batch` |
      | discovery/routing | `mcp_list_servers`, `mcp_list_tools`, `mcp_call_tool`, `mcp_status`, `mcp_server_test` |
      | code | `codebase_search`, `codebase_outline`, `repomap` |
      | ops | `system_status`, `billing_status`, `get_system_stats`, `list_processes` |
      | files/shell | `bash`, `read`, `write`, `edit`, `find`, `grep`, `ls` |
      | misc | `code_interpreter`, `apply_search_replace`, `add_bookmark`, `jira_create_issue`, `confluence_search`, `cloud_troubleshoot`, `generate_devops_pipeline`, `install_mcp_server`, `launch_webview`, `download_llamafile`, `echo`, `hello_world` |
      
      ### Skills & commands
      
      Skills: `hypernexus` (recall/store/discover), `hypernexus-ops` (health/billing/runbook),
      `hypernexus-workflows` (MEM-FIRST / DISCOVER / GRAPH / HANDOFF / OPS / HARVEST).
      Commands: `/hn-status` `/hn-search` `/hn-store` `/hn-session` `/hn-context`
      `/hn-summary` `/hn-tools` `/hn-purge`.

- path: addons\crush\preferred-tools.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus preferred tools (Crush)
      
      Route every HyperNexus need through the `hypernexus` MCP server first; fall back
      to the HTTP kernel API only when the MCP server is unreachable.
      
      1. **Memory recall** — `search_memory` (semantic) → FTS endpoint (hot loop) →
````

```

- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-store.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-store
      description: "Persist a fact to HyperNexus L2 memory with tags from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-store', 'remember this', or 'store memory'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-store <content> [--tags a,b] [--category general] [--importance 0.5]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Store the provided content as a durable L2 memory:
      
      1. Call `hypernexus MCP add_memory` with `content`, `tags` (comma-separated from
         `--tags`, default `jcode`), `category` (from `--category`, default `general`),
         and `importance` (from `--importance`, default 0.5).
      2. Confirm with the returned id (kernel wraps the record at `data.memory.id`).
      3. If the content names entities/relations worth keeping, also run
         `hypernexus MCP memory_extract_relations` on it.
      
      If `--tags` was omitted and the content clearly belongs to a topic, choose one
      descriptive tag yourself. On kernel-down, say the memory was not persisted and why.
      
- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-tools.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-tools
      description: "Discover tools across the assimilated HyperNexus MCP catalog and route calls from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-tools', 'find a tool', or 'tool discovery'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-tools [query] [--call serverName.toolName {json-args}]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Tool discovery and routing through the HyperNexus MCP bridge:
      
      - With a query (no `--call`): `hypernexus MCP mcp_list_tools` and/or the semantic
        search over `/api/mcp/tools/search` to find candidate tools for the need; present
        name, server, and one-line purpose for the best matches.
      - With `--call`: invoke via `hypernexus MCP mcp_call_tool` using
        `serverName`, `toolName`, `arguments` (JSON). Report the result envelope.
      - With no arguments: `hypernexus MCP mcp_status` + `mcp_list_servers` — the live
        bridge inventory (connected servers, tool counts).
      
      After a successful routed call, suggest persisting which tool worked
      (`hypernexus MCP add_memory`, tags `tool-routing`). If the bridge is unavailable,
      fall back to `curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/api/mcp/tools`.
      
- path: addons\crush\swarm-prompt-block.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus swarm prompt block (Crush)
      
      Paste at session start for multi-agent work, or keep enforced via the
      SessionStart hook (`hooks/hn-session.sh`):
      
      > You are `[muse-spark-1.3-contributor in crush in ~/workspace/HyperNexus]`.
      > Recall HyperNexus L2 memory before acting; persist every non-obvious outcome
      > with `add_memory` (tags `topic,kind`, append-only, safe to retry) and wire
      > entities via `memory_extract_relations`. Summarize assignments tagged
      > `session,summary,<worker-label>`; keep scratchpad key `goal` current when work
      > continues. Kernel calls are budgeted and never stall a turn: hot paths use
      > FTS-only search (400ms); explicit tools may use full semantic search.
      > If the kernel is down, say so in one short sentence and continue with local tools.
      
```

````json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/README.md",
  "instructions": "Create Freebuff addon README",
  "content": "# HyperNexus for Freebuff — full integration (Codebuff platform)\n\nFreebuff (`freebuff`, built on the [Codebuff](https://codebuff.com) platform,\nconfig dir `~/.config/manicode/`) integration for the HyperNexus AI control\nplane (Go kernel `http://127.0.0.1:7778`, dashboard 7779): persistent\nmulti-tier memory (L1 scratchpad / L2 vault / L3 cold archive), semantic MCP\ntool discovery + routing, skill registry, sessions, knowledge graph, code\nsearch, system + billing status — plus Meta `muse-spark-1.3-contributor`\nwiring borrowed from pi (`~/.pi/agent/auth.json`, pi owns refresh).\n\n## What Freebuff natively supports (verified surfaces)\n\n| Freebuff surface | This addon |\n|---|---|\n| `AGENTS.md` (project root, fallback `CLAUDE.md`) | managed discipline block via installer |\n| `.agents/mcp.json` (standard `mcpServers` format, projectDir/parents) | `hypernexus` stdio server (`bin/hypernexus.exe mcp`, 58 tools) |\n| `.agents/skills/*` and `.claude/skills/*` → `/skill:<name>` | 3 core skills + 8 `hn-*` command-skills |\n| `freebuff --continue` / `/export` | session resume + transcript export (native) |\n| `/model` picker | already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (1M context; paid-plan locked per Freebuff README — see Meta section below) |\n\nFreebuff has **no custom-provider / apiKeyHelper surface** (unlike Crush\n`crushrc` or Droid `apiKeyHelper`), so Meta OAuth is wired as a live-read\nhelper + direct-API docs rather than a provider block. Nothing secret is ever\nstored in this repo.\n\n## What you get (parity with pi / opencode / crush / mimo / jcode)\n\n| Surface | This addon |\n|---|---|\n| MCP server | `hypernexus` stdio (`bin/hypernexus.exe mcp`, 58 tools) — discover via `mcp_list_tools`, route via `mcp_call_tool` |\n| Skills (root `.agents/skills/`) | `hypernexus`, `hypernexus-ops`, `hypernexus-workflows` — model- + user-invocable via `/skill:<name>` |\n| Command-skills | `hn-status`, `hn-search`, `hn-store`, `hn-session`, `hn-context`, `hn-summary`, `hn-tools`, `hn-purge` → `/skill:hn-*` |\n| Meta model | `meta-model.freebuff.md` + `bin/meta-muse-key.mjs` — mint a Meta Model API key from pi OAuth live at call time (pi owns refresh) |\n| Overlay | `prompt-overlay.md` (discipline loop), `preferred-tools.md` (routing), `swarm-prompt-block.md` |\n| Installer | `install.mjs` — project (`.agents/`) or home-global (`~/.agents/`), `--check`, `--uninstall` |\n\n## Quick start\n\n```bash\n# 1. Install into this project (writes .agents/ + AGENTS.md managed block):\nnode addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project .\n\n# 2. Or cover every project under $HOME (writes ~/.agents/):\nnode addons/freebuff/install.mjs --global\n\n# 3. Restart freebuff in this repo (trust .agents when prompted, or run with --trust-agents), then:\n#    /skill:hn-status    kernel + MCP + memory + billing, one screen\n#    /skill:hn-search    cross-session recall\n#    /skill:hn-tools    26k+ MCP tool discovery + routing\n\n# 4. Verify the install without touching anything:\nnode addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --check\n\n# 5. Meta subscription model (no secrets stored — pi owns OAuth refresh):\nnode addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check   # mint OK?\n```\n\n## Files\n\n- `install.mjs` — idempotent installer (`--project <dir>` default cwd, `--global`, `--check`, `--uninstall`)\n- `mcp.freebuff.snippet.json` — HyperNexus MCP server fragment for `.agents/mcp.json` (reference)\n- `meta-model.freebuff.md` — Meta `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` wiring for Freebuff (reference, no secrets)\n- `bin/meta-muse-key.mjs` — mint a Meta Model API key from pi `auth.json` identity (live-read, never stored)\n- `prompt-overlay.md` — memory-first discipline + tool map (managed into `AGENTS.md`)\n- `preferred-tools.md` — which HyperNexus surface to use for what\n- `swarm-prompt-block.md` — multi-agent persistence contract\n- `skills/*/SKILL.md` — 3 core skills (freebuff-flavored)\n- `commands/hn-*.md` — 8 command prompt files (installed as `/skill:hn-*` command-skills)\n"
}
````

````json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md",
  "instructions": "Create Meta model wiring doc for Freebuff",
  "content": "# HyperNexus — Meta model wiring for Freebuff (REFERENCE, no secrets inside)\n\nModel: `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` via Meta's Model API.\nAuth is read LIVE from pi's `auth.json` at every call (pi owns OAuth refresh;\nnothing is stored in this file or any config).\n\n## Native surface: the `/model` picker already ships it\n\nFreebuff's `/model` picker already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (1M-token\ncontext). Per the Freebuff README it **requires a paid plan**: without one it\nis listed but locked, and selecting it opens the plans page. So \"native\"\naccess = pick it in `/model` once your plan unlocks it — no config needed.\n\n## Subscription/OAuth path: mint a Model API key live from pi\n\nWhen you want the Meta subscription credential (not Freebucks) for direct\nMeta API calls from inside a Freebuff session, mint it live — exactly like\nthe Droid `apiKeyHelper` does:\n\n```bash\n# Check the mint works (key hidden):\nnode addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check\n\n# Mint a key for the current shell only (never commit, never store):\nexport META_MUSE_API_KEY=\"$(node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs)\"\n```\n\nHow it works (mirrors pi's own Meta OAuth flow,\n`@earendil-works/pi-ai` `dist/auth/oauth/meta.js`):\n\n1. Read identity token from `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` → `meta.refresh`\n   (`META_AUTH_PATH` overrides the path).\n2. `POST https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key`\n   (`META_KEY_MINT_URL` overrides) with `Authorization: Bearer <identity>`.\n3. Print ONLY the minted `api_key` to stdout.\n\nDirect call example (OpenAI-compatible endpoint, key from step above):\n\n```bash\nexport META_MUSE_API_KEY=\"$(node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs)\"\ncurl -s https://api.meta.ai/v1/chat/completions \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $META_MUSE_API_KEY\" \\\n  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\\n  -d '{\"model\":\"muse-spark-1.3-contributor\",\"messages\":[{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"reply with exactly: META_OK\"}]}'\n```\n\n## Verify\n\n```bash\nnode addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check   # expect: mint OK (key hidden)\n```\n\nIf it reports an expired session (401/403), re-login in pi (`/login meta`\nin pi) — identity tokens are not renewable; pi owns the refresh.\n\n## Rules\n\n- This file and the installer NEVER write the token or minted key anywhere.\n- `auth.json` is read at call time, every time — rotation in pi is picked up\n  automatically.\n- `META_AUTH_PATH` / `META_KEY_MINT_URL` env overrides exist for tests.\n"
}
````

```json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs",
  "instructions": "Create Meta key mint helper for freebuff",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/*\n * meta-muse-key.mjs — mint a Meta Model API key from the pi auth.json identity token.\n *\n * Freebuff edition of addons/droid/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs (same flow — freebuff\n * has no apiKeyHelper surface, so call this live from the shell or a session).\n *\n * Mirrors pi's own Meta OAuth flow (see @earendil-works/pi-ai dist/auth/oauth/meta.js):\n *   1. Read identity token from ~/.pi/agent/auth.json -> meta.refresh\n *   2. POST https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key with Authorization: Bearer <identity>\n *   3. Print ONLY the minted api_key to stdout.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   node meta-muse-key.mjs            # prints minted key\n *   node meta-muse-key.mjs --check     # exit 0 if mint works, 1 otherwise\n *\n * Env overrides:\n *   META_AUTH_PATH      path to pi auth.json (default ~/.pi/agent/auth.json)\n *   META_KEY_MINT_URL   default https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key\n */\n\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport os from \"node:os\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\n\nconst AUTH_PATH =\n  process.env.META_AUTH_PATH || path.join(os.homedir(), \".pi\", \"agent\", \"auth.json\");\nconst MINT_URL = process.env.META_KEY_MINT_URL || \"https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key\";\nconst TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;\n\nfunction fail(msg) {\n  process.stderr.write(`[meta-muse-key] ${msg}\\n`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nfunction readIdentityToken() {\n  let raw;\n  try {\n    raw = fs.readFileSync(AUTH_PATH, \"utf8\");\n  } catch (e) {\n    fail(`cannot read auth file ${AUTH_PATH}: ${e.message}. Run /login meta in pi first.`);\n  }\n  let data;\n  try {\n    data = JSON.parse(raw);\n  } catch {\n    fail(`auth file ${AUTH_PATH} is not valid JSON.`);\n  }\n  const cred = data?.meta;\n  if (!cred || cred.type !== \"oauth\" || typeof cred.refresh !== \"string\" || !cred.refresh) {\n    fail(\n      `no Meta OAuth credential in ${AUTH_PATH} (expected .meta.refresh). Run /login meta in pi to sign in.`\n    );\n  }\n  return cred.refresh;\n}\n\nasync function mintApiKey(identityToken, signal) {\n  const res = await fetch(MINT_URL, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Accept: \"application/json\",\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${identityToken}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n      \"x-api-version\": \"1.0.0\",\n    },\n    body: \"{}\",\n    signal,\n  });\n  let json = null;\n  try {\n    json = await res.json();\n  } catch { /* non-JSON body */ }\n  if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {\n    fail(\n      `Meta session expired (HTTP ${res.status}). Identity tokens are not renewable — run /login meta in pi to sign in again.`\n    );\n  }\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const detail = json && typeof json === \"object\"\n      ? [\"error_description\", \"detail\", \"message\", \"error\"].map((k) => json[k]).find((v) => typeof v === \"string\" && v.trim())\n      : null;\n    fail(`Meta API key mint failed (HTTP ${res.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : \"\"}.`);\n  }\n  const apiKey = json?.api_key;\n  if (typeof apiKey !== \"string\" || !apiKey) {\n    const actionUrl = typeof json?.action_url === \"string\" ? json.action_url : null;\n    fail(`Meta did not issue an API key.${actionUrl ? ` Complete setup at ${actionUrl}` : \"\"}`);\n  }\n  return apiKey;\n}\n\nconst checkOnly = process.argv.includes(\"--check\");\nconst identity = readIdentityToken();\ntry {\n  const key = await mintApiKey(identity, AbortSignal.timeout(TIMEOUT_MS));\n  if (checkOnly) {\n    process.stderr.write(\"[meta-muse-key] mint OK (key hidden)\\n\");\n  } else {\n    process.stdout.write(key);\n  }\n} catch (e) {\n  if (e?.name === \"TimeoutError\" || e?.name === \"AbortError\") fail(`mint request timed out after ${TIMEOUT_MS}ms.`);\n  fail(e.message || String(e));\n}\n"
}
```

````json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/skills/hypernexus/SKILL.md",
  "instructions": "Create freebuff core hypernexus skill",
  "content": "---\nuser-invocable: true\nname: hypernexus\ndescription: \"HyperNexus AI control plane — persistent L2 vector memory, semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, billing, and agent orchestration. Use when the task benefits from cross-session memory (scratchpad, facts, observations), tool discovery across MCP servers, knowledge ingestion, system health checks, or routing through the HyperNexus Go kernel on port 7778. Trigger on mentions of 'hypernexus', 'HN', 'scratchpad', 'cross-session memory', 'MCP tools', 'tool discovery', 'knowledge graph', 'agent memory', or 'control plane'.\"\n---\n\n# HyperNexus Integration (Freebuff)\n\nHyperNexus is a local AI control plane running a Go kernel on port 7778 (dashboard 7779) with persistent multi-tier memory (L1 scratchpad, L2 vector store, L3 cold archive), semantic tool discovery across 60+ MCP servers / 26,000+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, and agent orchestration.\n\nInvoke with `/skill:hypernexus`. Freebuff loads this skill from `.agents/skills/` (or `.claude/skills/`) — no plugin install step.\n\n## When to Use\n\n- Start of any multi-step task → recall context (`search_memory`, fast FTS `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=`, scratchpad `memory_scratchpad_get`)\n- After a decision, gotcha, or non-obvious finding → store it (`add_memory` + `memory_extract_relations`)\n- When you need a capability you don't have → discover tools (`mcp_list_servers` / `mcp_list_tools`, semantic `GET /api/mcp/tools/search?query=`) then route (`mcp_call_tool`)\n- When the user asks about system health, billing, or the repo → `system_status`, `billing_status`, `repomap`, `codebase_search`\n\n## Freebuff Tools (via the hypernexus MCP server, 58 tools)\n\nThe MCP server (`.agents/mcp.json`, stdio `hypernexus mcp`) exposes these exact tool names. Prefer the MCP tool; the HTTP fallback works even when MCP is unreachable.\n\n| MCP tool | Purpose | HTTP fallback |\n|---|---|---|\n| `search_memory` | Semantic + keyword memory search (vector+FTS; slow ~6s on cold start, fast after warmup) | `GET /api/memory/search?query=` / `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=` (fast, ~15ms) |\n| `add_memory` | Store a fact / decision (content, tags, category) | `POST /api/memory/add` |\n| `delete_memory` | Delete a memory by id | `DELETE /api/memory/delete?id=` |\n| `memory_stats` | Tier counts: vault / scratchpad / relations | `GET /api/memory/stats` |\n| `memory_scratchpad_get` / `memory_scratchpad_set` / `memory_scratchpad_append` | L1 working memory (goal, persona, human) | `GET/POST /api/memory/scratchpad/*` |\n| `memory_extract_relations` / `memory_extract_relations_batch` | Knowledge graph: entities + relations | `POST /api/memory/extract-relations` |\n| `mcp_list_servers` | Live MCP server inventory | `GET /api/mcp/servers` |\n| `mcp_list_tools` | Tool inventory across connected servers | `GET /api/mcp/tools` |\n| `mcp_status` | Bridge status: connected count, fallback | `GET /api/mcp/status` |\n| `mcp_server_test` | Probe a downstream server (tools/list, ping) | — |\n| `mcp_call_tool` | Route a call (serverName, toolName, arguments) | `POST /api/agent/tool` |\n| `system_status` | Kernel liveness + version | `GET /health` |\n| `billing_status` | Provider quotas, depletion, fallback chain | `GET /api/billing/status` |\n| `get_system_stats` | OS / runtime metrics | — |\n| `codebase_search` | symbols / definitions / references (empty result when kernel catalog has no match) | `GET /api/mcp/native/search?query=` |\n| `codebase_outline` | Symbol outline for a file | — |\n| `repomap` | Ranked repo map with symbol summaries | — |\n| `read` / `write` / `edit` / `find` / `grep` / `ls` (+ `read_file` / `write_file` / `search` / `run_shell_command` / `bash` / `apply_search_replace`) | File + shell access through the kernel | local tools |\n| `code_interpreter` | Run python/node snippets (stateful session) | — |\n| `add_bookmark` | Save URL with title + tags | — |\n| `jira_create_issue` / `confluence_search` | Atlassian integration | — |\n| `cloud_troubleshoot` / `generate_devops_pipeline` | Ops generators | — |\n| `install_mcp_server` | Install an MCP server via npx | — |\n| `launch_webview` | Open a system browser window | — |\n| `download_llamafile` | Fetch a standalone model binary | — |\n| `list_processes` / `kill_process` | Process management (Windows) | — |\n\n## Discipline loop\n\n```\nRECALL → DISCOVER (if gap) → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY\n```\n\n1. **RECALL** before re-deriving: FTS fast path first, semantic `search_memory` for depth; include scratchpad `goal`.\n2. **DISCOVER** on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` → semantic search → `mcp_call_tool`.\n3. **PERSIST** non-obvious outcomes: `add_memory` (tags `topic,kind`) + `memory_extract_relations`.\n4. **VERIFY** around production changes: `system_status` + `billing_status` before and after.\n\nIf the kernel is down, say so in one short sentence and continue with local tools — never present mock state.\n"
}
````
