````json
  "instructions": "Create freebuff ops skill",
  "content": "---\nuser-invocable: true\nname: hypernexus-ops\ndescription: \"Operate and verify the HyperNexus kernel from Freebuff: health checks, MCP bridge inventory, billing/quotas, audit trail, and verify-before/after discipline for deployments and incidents. Trigger on 'kernel health', 'mcp servers', 'billing', 'quotas', 'audit', 'deploy verification', or 'hypernexus ops'.\"\n---\n\n# HyperNexus Ops (Freebuff)\n\nInvoke with `/skill:hypernexus-ops`.\n\n## Tools (via the hypernexus MCP server)\n\n- `system_status` — health before and after production changes (`GET /health` fallback)\n- `mcp_status` / `mcp_list_servers` / `mcp_list_tools` — MCP bridge inventory\n- Semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search?query=` — find the right downstream tool\n- `billing_status` — quotas, depleted models, fallback chain\n- Kernel audit trail — record compliance-relevant actions with `add_memory` (tags `ops,audit`)\n\n## Verify loop (OPS workflow)\n\n1. VERIFY before change: `system_status`\n2. Act (with your normal tools)\n3. VERIFY after change: `system_status`\n4. PERSIST: `add_memory` with the outcome and tags `[\"ops\", \"deploy\"]`\n\n## Health endpoints\n\n```bash\ncurl -s http://localhost:7778/health                  # kernel liveness + version\ncurl -s http://localhost:7778/api/system/overview     # full system view\ncurl -s http://localhost:7778/api/mcp/status          # MCP bridge status\ncurl -s http://localhost:7778/api/billing/status      # provider billing\ncurl -s http://localhost:7778/api/billing/fallback-chain\n```\n\n## RBAC\n\nFreebuff has no permission-hook surface, so enforcement is by convention:\ndestructive patterns (`rm -rf`, `sudo`, `chmod -R 777`, `DROP TABLE`,\n`git push --force`) get a kernel audit entry first — route them through a\nconfirming prompt and log with `add_memory` tags `ops,rbac`.\n"
}
````

```
- path: addons\opencode\skills\hypernexus-workflows\SKILL.md
  content: |
      ---
      name: hypernexus-workflows
      description: "Reusable HyperNexus orchestration workflows for OpenCode: MEM-FIRST, DISCOVER, HARVEST, HANDOFF, OPS, and the RECALL→DISCOVER→EXECUTE→PERSIST→VERIFY loop. Trigger on 'multi-step task', 'resume work', 'capability gap', 'handoff', 'session summary', or when starting complex work with hypernexus tools available."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Workflows
      
      Loop: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**
      
      ## MEM-FIRST (any multi-step task)
      
      1. `hypernexus_context_harvest` (topic = the task)
      2. `hypernexus_memory_search` for prior decisions/gotchas
      3. Execute
      4. `hypernexus_memory_store` the non-obvious outcomes
      
      ## DISCOVER (capability gap)
      
      1. `hypernexus_suggest_tools` — what the advertiser recommends for this conversation
      2. `hypernexus_tool_search` — semantic search across 26k+ tools
      3. `hypernexus_mcp_call` — route the call through the kernel
      4. `hypernexus_memory_store` — remember what worked
      
      ## HARVEST (resuming after a break)
      
      1. `hypernexus_context_harvest`
      2. `hypernexus_session_context` — active goal + cross-session links
      3. `hypernexus_session_handoff` (action=pickup) — restore the last handoff
      4. `hypernexus_memory_list` — recent vault activity
      
      ## HANDOFF (end of session / context switch)
      
      1. `hypernexus_session_handoff` (action=write, summary=…)
      2. `hypernexus_memory_store` with tags `["session", "summary"]`
      3. `/hn-summary` — model-generated session summary stored to L2
      
      ## OPS (deployments / incidents)
      
      See the `hypernexus-ops` skill: verify → act → verify → persist.
      
      ## Inline recall
      
      `@memory:keyword` in any prompt expands matching L2 context inline.
      
```

Trigger on 'multi-step task', 'resume work', 'capability gap', 'handoff', 'session summary', or when starting complex work with hypernexus tools available.\"\n---\n\n# HyperNexus Workflows (Freebuff)\n\nInvoke with `/skill:hypernexus-workflows`.\n\nLoop: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**\n\nFreebuff maps the loop onto MCP tools + HTTP (no hooks surface — run each\nstep explicitly).\n\n## MEM-FIRST (any multi-step task)\n\n1. FTS recall: `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8` (fast, ~15ms)\n2. Depth: `search_memory` for prior decisions/gotchas\n3. HTTP `/api/sessions/summary` for cross-harness resume\n3. Recent vault activity via `memory_stats` / `search_memory` (empty query → recent)\n\n## HANDOFF (end of session / context switch)\n\n1. `/skill:hn-summary` — model-generated session summary stored to L2\n2. `add_memory` with tags `[\"session\", \"summary\"]`\n3. `freebuff --continue` reopens the chat; `/export` writes the transcript to Markdown\n\n## OPS (deployments / incidents)\n\nSee the `hypernexus-ops` skill (`/skill:hypernexus-ops`): verify → act → verify → persist.\n"

```

- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-session.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-session
      description: "Search imported cross-harness HyperNexus sessions and resume context from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-session', 'resume session', or 'previous session'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-session [query] [--limit n]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Session continuity through the HyperNexus kernel:
      
      1. With a query: search imported sessions and session-scoped memory for the topic
         (`hypernexus MCP search_memory` with the query; the kernel archives sessions
         across harnesses). With no query: list the most recent session summaries.
      2. Surface the 3-5 most relevant prior decisions/gotchas so the current session can
         continue without reconstruction.
      3. If a previous session set scratchpad key `goal`, read it
         (`hypernexus MCP memory_scratchpad_get`) and report it as the active goal.
      
      If the kernel is down, say continuity is unavailable this turn — do not invent history.
      
- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-context.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-context
      description: "Build a compact context block from HyperNexus L2 memory for the current task from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-context' or 'task context'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-context [topic]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Assemble task context from persistent memory (this is a recall aid, not a report):
      
      1. Fast path first: `/api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8` (~15ms) for the hot loop;
         fall back to `hypernexus MCP search_memory` for semantic recall when the fast path
         is thin.
      2. Include scratchpad keys `goal`, `persona`, `human`
         (`hypernexus MCP memory_scratchpad_get`).
      3. Emit a compact block: prior decisions, gotchas, active goal, open threads — each
         with its memory id so the user can prune with `/hn-purge`.
      
      Keep the output under ~30 lines. If nothing matches, say so instead of padding.
      
- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-summary.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-summary
      description: "Persist a Crush session summary to HyperNexus L2 memory and hand off to the next session. Trigger on '/hn-summary', 'summarize session', or 'handoff'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-summary [what-changed]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      End-of-session handoff (pairs with the HANDOFF.md multi-agent protocol):
      
      1. `hypernexus MCP add_memory` a short summary of the session: what was delivered,
         what was verified (with numbers), what remains — tags `session,summary` plus one
         topic tag. Use `--`-free prose from `$ARGUMENTS` as the spine when provided.
      2. `hypernexus MCP memory_scratchpad_set` key `goal` to what the next session should
         pick up (or clear it if the work is complete).
      3. If entities/relations matter for future work, run
         `hypernexus MCP memory_extract_relations` on the summary.
      
      Append-only and safe to retry. Confirm each write's id in one short list.
      
- path: addons\crush\commands\hn-purge.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-purge
      description: "Delete a HyperNexus L2 memory by id (or by tag) from Crush. Trigger on '/hn-purge' or 'delete memory'."
      ---
      
      # /hn-purge <id> [--tag t]
      
      $ARGUMENTS
      
      Remove stored memories, carefully:
      
      1. With an id: `hypernexus MCP delete_memory` with `{id}` — confirm deletion from the
         response envelope.
      2. With `--tag`: resolve matching ids first via `/api/memory/search?query=&limit=` filtering
         on the tag, list them, then delete each with `hypernexus MCP delete_memory`.
      3. Deletion propagates asynchronously across stores — if asked to confirm it is gone,
         poll ~6s before asserting.
      
      Never delete without the user having named the id or tag. Report exactly what was
      removed and what was kept.
      
```

Summarize hits grouped by relevance; include ids so follow-up\n   `hypernexus MCP delete_memory` calls are possible. Empty query → recent memories.\n\nNever stall: if the kernel is down, report it and continue without memory context.\n"

````json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/prompt-overlay.md",
  "instructions": "Create freebuff prompt overlay",
  "content": "## HyperNexus Discipline (Freebuff prompt overlay)\n\nEvery turn in this workspace runs memory-first through the HyperNexus kernel\n(`http://127.0.0.1:7778`; MCP server `hypernexus`, 58 tools via stdio):\n\n```\nRECALL -> DISCOVER (if gap) -> EXECUTE -> PERSIST -> VERIFY\n```\n\n- **RECALL** — before re-deriving anything: FTS fast path\n  (`/api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8`, ~15ms) for hot loops, semantic\n  `search_memory` for depth. Include scratchpad `goal`.\n- **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` →\n  semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search`, then route with `mcp_call_tool`. Persist what\n  worked (`add_memory`, tags `tool-routing`).\n- **PERSIST** — non-obvious outcomes immediately: `add_memory`\n  (tags `topic,kind`) + `memory_extract_relations` for entities/relations.\n- **VERIFY** — around production changes: `system_status` + `billing_status`\n  before and after; never present mock state when the kernel is down.\n\n### Tool map (MCP `hypernexus` server)\n\n| Need | Tool |\n|---|---|\n| fast recall | `memory_scratchpad_get/set/append`, FTS search endpoint |\n| durable recall | `search_memory`, `add_memory`, `delete_memory`, `memory_stats` |\n| graph | `memory_extract_relations`, `memory_extract_relations_batch` |\n| discovery/routing | `mcp_list_servers`, `mcp_list_tools`, `mcp_call_tool`, `mcp_status`, `mcp_server_test` |\n| code | `codebase_search`, `codebase_outline`, `repomap` |\n| ops | `system_status`, `billing_status`, `get_system_stats`, `list_processes` |\n| files/shell | `bash`, `read`, `write`, `edit`, `find`, `grep`, `ls` |\n| misc | `code_interpreter`, `apply_search_replace`, `add_bookmark`, `jira_create_issue`, `confluence_search`, `cloud_troubleshoot`, `generate_devops_pipeline`, `install_mcp_server`, `launch_webview`, `download_llamafile`, `echo`, `hello_world` |\n\n### Skills & command-skills\n\nSkills (`/skill:<name>`): `hypernexus` (recall/store/discover), `hypernexus-ops`\n(health/billing/runbook), `hypernexus-workflows` (MEM-FIRST / DISCOVER / GRAPH /\nHANDOFF / OPS / HARVEST).\nCommand-skills: `/skill:hn-status` `/skill:hn-search` `/skill:hn-store`\n`/skill:hn-session` `/skill:hn-context` `/skill:hn-summary` `/skill:hn-tools`\n`/skill:hn-purge`.\n\n### Meta model\n\n`/model` already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (paid-plan locked without a plan).\nFor the subscription/OAuth path, see `addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md`:\nmint live with `node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check`.\n"
}
````

```json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/preferred-tools.md",
  "instructions": "Create freebuff preferred tools doc",
  "content": "# HyperNexus preferred tools (Freebuff)\n\nRoute every HyperNexus need through the `hypernexus` MCP server first; fall back\nto the HTTP kernel API only when the MCP server is unreachable.\n\n1. **Memory recall** — `search_memory` (semantic) → FTS endpoint (hot loop) →\n   `memory_scratchpad_get` for `goal`/`persona`/`human`.\n2. **Memory store** — `add_memory` (+ `importance`, topic tags) then\n   `memory_extract_relations` when entities matter.\n3. **Tool discovery** — `mcp_list_servers` (inventory) → `mcp_list_tools`\n   (capabilities) → semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search?query=` (meaning match).\n4. **Tool routing** — `mcp_call_tool` (`serverName`, `toolName`, `arguments` JSON).\n5. **Health** — `system_status` → `mcp_status` → `billing_status` →\n   `memory_stats`; HTTP fallbacks `/health`, `/api/mcp/status`, `/api/memory/stats`.\n6. **Code** — `codebase_search` (symbols|definitions|references) → `repomap` →\n   `codebase_outline`; files via `read`/`write`/`edit`, search via `grep`/`find`/`ls`.\n7. **Sessions** — `search_memory` scoped by session + scratchpad `goal`; HTTP\n   `/api/sessions/summary` for cross-harness resume; `freebuff --continue` to reopen.\n8. **Skills** — HTTP `/api/skills*` registry; local `.agents/skills/hypernexus*/`.\n"
}
```

Summarize assignments tagged\n> `session,summary,<worker-label>`; keep scratchpad key `goal` current when work\n> continues. Kernel calls are budgeted and never stall a turn: hot paths use\n> FTS-only search (400ms); explicit tools may use full semantic search.\n> If the kernel is down, say so in one short sentence and continue with local tools.\n"

> _Reasoning_
> Designing the installer logic for project vs global paths, AGENTS managed block handling, and skipping AGENTS for global installs.

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/install.mjs",
  "instructions": "Create freebuff installer",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/**\n * install.mjs — HyperNexus → Freebuff integration installer\n * ==========================================================\n * Installs the full HyperNexus control-plane surface for Freebuff (Codebuff\n * platform). Idempotent: the MCP server entry is merged by key, skills are\n * overwritten, and the AGENTS.md overlay is kept inside managed markers and\n * can be re-run or removed safely.\n *\n * Modes:\n *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs                    install into this project (.agents/)\n *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project <dir>    install into <dir>/.agents/\n *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --global            install into ~/.agents/ (covers all projects under $HOME)\n *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --check      verify installation (exit 1 on gaps)\n *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --uninstall   remove managed files/blocks\n *\n * What it installs (project → <dir>/.agents/  |  global → ~/.agents/):\n *   mcp.json            hypernexus stdio server merged into mcpServers (never clobbers other servers)\n *   skills/hypernexus*/  3 core skills (hypernexus, hypernexus-ops, hypernexus-workflows)\n *   skills/hn-*/         8 command-skills (hn-status/search/store/session/context/summary/tools/purge)\n *   HYPERNEXUS.md       overlay reference (discipline loop + tool map + Meta notes)\n *   AGENTS.md block     discipline overlay between managed markers (project mode only;\n *                       global mode skips it — freebuff reads AGENTS.md per project)\n *\n * The installer never stores secrets: the Meta token is read LIVE from\n * ~/.pi/agent/auth.json at every mint (pi owns OAuth refresh). See\n * meta-model.freebuff.md + bin/meta-muse-key.mjs.\n * After install: restart freebuff in the project (trust .agents when prompted,\n * or launch with --trust-agents) so the new mcp.json + skills load.\n */\n\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport os from \"node:os\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nconst __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, \"..\", \"..\");\nconst HOME = os.homedir() || process.env.USERPROFILE || process.env.HOME || \"\";\n\nconst MODE = process.argv.includes(\"--uninstall\")\n  ? \"uninstall\"\n  : process.argv.includes(\"--check\")\n    ? \"check\"\n    : \"install\";\nconst GLOBAL = process.argv.includes(\"--global\");\n\nfunction argValue(flag) {\n  const i = process.argv.indexOf(flag);\n  if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] && !process.argv[i + 1].startsWith(\"--\")) return process.argv[i + 1];\n  const eq = process.argv.find((a) => a.startsWith(flag + \"=\"));\n  return eq ? eq.slice(flag.length + 1) : \"\";\n}\n\nconst TARGET_OVERRIDE = argValue(\"--project\") || argValue(\"--target\");\nconst PROJECT_DIR = path.resolve(TARGET_OVERRIDE || (GLOBAL ? HOME : process.cwd()));\nconst AGENTS_DIR = GLOBAL ? path.join(HOME, \".agents\") : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \".agents\");\nconst AGENTS_FILE = GLOBAL ? null : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"AGENTS.md\"); // global has no single project root\n\nconst MD_BEGIN = \"<!-- hypernexus:begin freebuff (managed by addons/freebuff/install.mjs — do not edit inside this block) -->\";\nconst MD_END = \"<!-- hypernexus:end -->\";\n\nconst CORE_SKILLS = [\"hypernexus\", \"hypernexus-ops\", \"hypernexus-workflows\"];\nconst COMMANDS = [\"hn-status\", \"hn-search\", \"hn-store\", \"hn-session\", \"hn-context\", \"hn-summary\", \"hn-tools\", \"hn-purge\"];\n\n// ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction log(msg) {\n  console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] ${msg}`);\n}\n\nfunction readIfExists(p) {\n  try {\n    return fs.readFileSync(p, \"utf8\");\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction writeUtf8(file, content) {\n  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(file, content, \"utf8\"); // utf8 → no BOM (Go/Node parsers reject BOM)\n}\n\n/** Forward-slash exe path — backslashes break MCP shell expansion on Windows. */\nfunction resolveExe() {\n  const exe = process.platform === \"win32\" ? \"hypernexus.exe\" : \"hypernexus\";\n  const override = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_MCP_COMMAND;\n  if (override) return override;\n  for (const c of [\n    path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"bin\", exe),\n    path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"bin\", exe),\n  ]) {\n    if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c.split(path.sep).join(\"/\");\n  }\n  return exe;\n}\n\nfunction workspaceRoot() {\n  const ws = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT || (GLOBAL ? HOME : PROJECT_DIR);\n  return ws.split(path.sep).join(\"/\");\n}\n\nfunction upsertManagedBlock(filePath, blockText) {\n  const block = blockText.trim();\n  const existing = readIfExists(filePath) ?? \"\";\n  const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(\"<!-- hypernexus:begin\");\n  const endIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_END);\n  let next;\n  if (beginIdx >= 0 && endIdx > beginIdx) {\n    next = existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + block + existing.slice(endIdx + MD_END.length);\n  } else {\n    next = existing.trimEnd() + (existing.trimEnd() ? \"\\n\\n\" : \"\") + block + \"\\n\";\n  }\n  writeUtf8(filePath, next);\n  return existing !== next;\n}\n\nfunction removeManagedBlock(filePath) {\n  const existing = readIfExists(filePath);\n  if (existing === null) return false;\n  const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(\"<!-- hypernexus:begin\");\n  const endIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_END);\n  if (beginIdx < 0 || endIdx < beginIdx) return false;\n  const next = (existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + existing.slice(endIdx + MD_END.length)).replace(/\\n{3,}/g, \"\\n\\n\");\n  if (next.trim() === \"\") {\n    fs.rmSync(filePath, { force: true });\n    return true;\n  }\n  writeUtf8(filePath, next);\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction agentsBlock() {\n  return `${MD_BEGIN}\n## HyperNexus Control Plane (freebuff)\n\nThis workspace is backed by the local HyperNexus kernel (Go, port 7778).\nFreebuff loads this file at chat start plus \\`.agents/mcp.json\\` and\n\\`.agents/skills/*\\` (each skill invocable as \\`/skill:<name>\\`).\n\nDiscipline loop for multi-step work: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**\n\n- **RECALL** — before re-deriving: FTS fast path \\`GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8\\`\n  (~15ms), semantic \\`hypernexus\\` MCP \\`search_memory\\` for depth, scratchpad \\`goal\\`.\n- **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: \\`mcp_list_servers\\` → \\`mcp_list_tools\\` →\n  semantic \\`/api/mcp/tools/search\\`, then route with \\`mcp_call_tool\\`.\n- **PERSIST** — non-obvious outcomes via \\`add_memory\\` (tags \\`topic,kind\\`) +\n  \\`memory_extract_relations\\`; session handoffs via \\`/skill:hn-summary\\`.\n- **VERIFY** — \\`system_status\\` + \\`billing_status\\` before/after production changes.\n- Skills: \\`/skill:hypernexus\\` \\`/skill:hypernexus-ops\\` \\`/skill:hypernexus-workflows\\`\n  + command-skills \\`/skill:hn-status|hn-search|hn-store|hn-session|hn-context|hn-summary|hn-tools|hn-purge\\`.\n- Meta model: \\`/model\\` already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (paid-plan locked without a plan);\n  subscription/OAuth path = \\`node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs\\` (pi owns refresh;\n  see \\`addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md\\`).\n- Degradation contract: kernel calls are budgeted and never stall a turn. If the kernel\n  is down (\\`curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/health\\` fails), say so in one sentence and\n  continue with local tools — never mock state.\n${MD_END}`;\n}\n\n// ── install steps ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction installMcpJson() {\n  const file = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"mcp.json\");\n  let config = {};\n  const existing = readIfExists(file);\n  if (existing) {\n    try {\n      config = JSON.parse(existing);\n    } catch (e) {\n      log(`WARNING: could not parse ${file}: ${e.message}. Leaving it untouched.`);\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  config.mcpServers = config.mcpServers && typeof config.mcpServers === \"object\" ? config.mcpServers : {};\n  config.mcpServers.hypernexus = {\n    command: resolveExe(),\n    args: [\"mcp\"],\n    env: {\n      HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT: workspaceRoot(),\n      HYPERNEXUS_URL: process.env.HYPERNEXUS_URL || \"http://127.0.0.1:7778\",\n    },\n    timeout: 30,\n  };\n  writeUtf8(file, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + \"\\n\");\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction installSkills() {\n  let count = 0;\n  for (const name of CORE_SKILLS) {\n    const src = path.join(__dirname, \"skills\", name, \"SKILL.md\");\n    const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", name, \"SKILL.md\");\n    if (!fs.existsSync(src)) {\n      log(`WARNING: missing source skill ${src}, skipping.`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, \"utf8\"));\n    count++;\n  }\n  for (const name of COMMANDS) {\n    const src = path.join(__dirname, \"commands\", `${name}.md`);\n    const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", name, \"SKILL.md\");\n    if (!fs.existsSync(src)) {\n      log(`WARNING: missing source command ${src}, skipping.`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, \"utf8\"));\n    count++;\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\nfunction installOverlay() {\n  const overlay = readIfExists(path.join(__dirname, \"prompt-overlay.md\")) ?? \"\";\n  const content = `# HyperNexus (Freebuff reference)\\n\\n> Installed by \\`addons/freebuff/install.mjs\\`. Source of truth lives in\n> \\`addons/freebuff/\\`; this copy is what Freebuff reads from \\`.agents/\\`.\\n\\n${overlay.trim()}\\n`;\n  writeUtf8(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"HYPERNEXUS.md\"), content);\n}\n\n// ── check / uninstall ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction checkAll() {\n  const gaps = [];\n  const mcpFile = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"mcp.json\");\n  const raw = readIfExists(mcpFile);\n  if (!raw) {\n    gaps.push(`missing ${mcpFile}`);\n  } else {\n    try {\n      const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);\n      if (!cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus) gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: no mcpServers.hypernexus entry`);\n    } catch {\n      gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: invalid JSON`);\n    }\n  }\n  for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, ...COMMANDS]) {\n    if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", name, \"SKILL.md\"))) {\n      gaps.push(`missing skill ${name}`);\n    }\n  }\n  if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"HYPERNEXUS.md\"))) gaps.push(\"missing HYPERNEXUS.md\");\n  if (AGENTS_FILE) {\n    const agents = readIfExists(AGENTS_FILE) ?? \"\";\n    if (!agents.includes(\"<!-- hypernexus:begin\")) gaps.push(\"AGENTS.md: no managed hypernexus block\");\n  }\n  const keyHelper = path.join(__dirname, \"bin\", \"meta-muse-key.mjs\");\n  if (!fs.existsSync(keyHelper)) gaps.push(\"missing bin/meta-muse-key.mjs\");\n  if (gaps.length) {\n    console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] CHECK FAILED (${gaps.length} gap(s)):\\n  - ${gaps.join(\"\\n  - \")}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] CHECK OK: MCP + ${CORE_SKILLS.length + COMMANDS.length} skills + overlay present.`);\n}\n\nfunction uninstallAll() {\n  let removed = 0;\n  const mcpFile = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"mcp.json\");\n  const raw = readIfExists(mcpFile);\n  if (raw) {\n    try {\n      const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);\n      if (cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus) {\n        delete cfg.mcpServers.hypernexus;\n        if (Object.keys(cfg.mcpServers).length === 0) fs.rmSync(mcpFile, { force: true });\n        else writeUtf8(mcpFile, JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2) + \"\\n\");\n        removed++;\n      }\n    } catch { /* leave unparseable file alone */ }\n  }\n  for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, ...COMMANDS]) {\n    const dir = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", name);\n    if (fs.existsSync(dir)) {\n      fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });\n      removed++;\n    }\n  }\n  for (const f of [path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"HYPERNEXUS.md\")]) {\n    if (fs.existsSync(f)) {\n      fs.rmSync(f, { force: true });\n      removed++;\n    }\n  }\n  if (AGENTS_FILE && removeManagedBlock(AGENTS_FILE)) removed++;\n  log(`uninstalled (${removed} item(s) removed).`);\n}\n\n// ── main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nif (MODE === \"check\") {\n  checkAll();\n} else if (MODE === \"uninstall\") {\n  uninstallAll();\n} else {\n  const mcpOk = installMcpJson();\n  const skills = installSkills();\n  installOverlay();\n  let agentsNote = \"skipped (global mode: freebuff reads AGENTS.md per project)\";\n  if (AGENTS_FILE) {\n    const changed = upsertManagedBlock(AGENTS_FILE, agentsBlock());\n    agentsNote = changed ? \"managed block written\" : \"managed block already current\";\n  }\n  log(`installed into ${AGENTS_DIR} (mcp.json ${mcpOk ? \"merged\" : \"SKIPPED — unparseable\"}, ${skills} skills, HYPERNEXUS.md; AGENTS.md ${agentsNote}).`);\n  log(`restart freebuff in ${PROJECT_DIR} (trust .agents when prompted, or use --trust-agents).`);\n  log(`verify: node addons/freebuff/install.mjs ${GLOBAL ? \"--global\" : `--project ${PROJECT_DIR}`} --check`);\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "path": "bin/test-freebuff-ext.mjs",
  "instructions": "Create freebuff extension battery test",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/**\n * test-freebuff-ext.mjs — HyperNexus Freebuff integration battery.\n * Verifies the full freebuff surface without touching the network:\n * manifest entry, installer outputs, MCP config, skills, overlays,\n * Meta helper wiring (auth shape only — mint needs network, covered by --live).\n *\n *   node bin/test-freebuff-ext.mjs          hermetic checks\n *   node bin/test-freebuff-ext.mjs --live   + Meta key mint + kernel /health\n */\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport os from \"node:os\";\nimport { execFileSync } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nconst ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \"..\");\nconst ADDON = path.join(ROOT, \"addons\", \"freebuff\");\nconst LIVE = process.argv.includes(\"--live\");\n\nlet pass = 0;\nlet fail = 0;\nfunction ok(name, cond, detail = \"\") {\n  if (cond) {\n    pass++;\n    console.log(`  ok   ${name}`);\n  } else {\n    fail++;\n    console.log(`  FAIL ${name}${detail ? ` — ${detail}` : \"\"}`);\n  }\n}\n\nconsole.log(\"[freebuff-ext] manifest\");\nconst manifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, \"addons\", \"shared\", \"hypernexus.manifest.json\"), \"utf8\"));\nconst fb = manifest?.harnesses?.freebuff;\nok(\"manifest harnesses.freebuff active\", fb?.status === \"active\", fb?.status);\nok(\"manifest installer path exists\", fs.existsSync(path.join(ROOT, fb?.installer ?? \"nope\")));\n\nconsole.log(\"[freebuff-ext] installer outputs (project install)\");\nconst agentsDir = path.join(ROOT, \".agents\");\nok(\".agents/mcp.json valid JSON with hypernexus server\", (() => {\n  try {\n    const cfg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(agentsDir, \"mcp.json\"), \"utf8\"));\n    const s = cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus;\n    return !!s && Array.isArray(s.args) && s.args.includes(\"mcp\") && typeof s.command === \"string\";\n  } catch { return false; }\n})());\nconst expectedSkills = [\"hypernexus\", \"hypernexus-ops\", \"hypernexus-workflows\",\n  \"hn-status\", \"hn-search\", \"hn-store\", \"hn-session\", \"hn-context\", \"hn-summary\", \"hn-tools\", \"hn-purge\"];\nconst missing = expectedSkills.filter((s) => !fs.existsSync(path.join(agentsDir, \"skills\", s, \"SKILL.md\")));\nok(`all ${expectedSkills.length} skills installed`, missing.length === 0, missing.join(\",\"));\nok(\"HYPERNEXUS.md overlay installed\", fs.existsSync(path.join(agentsDir, \"HYPERNEXUS.md\")));\nok(\"AGENTS.md managed block present\", (fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, \"AGENTS.md\"), \"utf8\")).includes(\"hypernexus:begin freebuff\"));\n\nconsole.log(\"[freebuff-ext] addon sources\");\nfor (const f of [\"README.md\", \"install.mjs\", \"mcp.freebuff.snippet.json\", \"meta-model.freebuff.md\",\n  \"prompt-overlay.md\", \"preferred-tools.md\", \"swarm-prompt-block.md\",\n  path.join(\"bin\", \"meta-muse-key.mjs\"),\n  path.join(\"skills\", \"hypernexus\", \"SKILL.md\"),\n  path.join(\"skills\", \"hypernexus-ops\", \"SKILL.md\"),\n  path.join(\"skills\", \"hypernexus-workflows\", \"SKILL.md\"),\n  ...[\"hn-status\", \"hn-search\", \"hn-store\", \"hn-session\", \"hn-context\", \"hn-summary\", \"hn-tools\", \"hn-purge\"].map((c) => path.join(\"commands\", `${c}.md`))]) {\n  ok(`source ${f}`, fs.existsSync(path.join(ADDON, f)));\n}\nok(\"snippet is valid JSON with hypernexus server\", (() => {\n  try {\n    const s = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ADDON, \"mcp.freebuff.snippet.json\"), \"utf8\"));\n    return !!s?.mcpServers?.hypernexus;\n  } catch { return false; }\n})());\n\nconsole.log(\"[freebuff-ext] no-secrets scan\");\nconst secretRe = /(dca:[A-Za-z0-9_:\\-]{10,}|sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}|\"access\":\\s*\"LLM\\|)/;\nconst scanned = [];\n(function walk(dir) {\n  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);\n    if (e.isDirectory()) { if (e.name !== \"node_modules\") walk(p); continue; }\n    if (!/\\.(mjs|md|json|jsonc)$/.test(e.name)) continue;\n    scanned.push(p);\n    const t = fs.readFileSync(p, \"utf8\");\n    if (secretRe.test(t)) ok(`no secret in ${path.relative(ROOT, p)}`, false, \"MATCH\");\n  }\n})(ADDON);\n(function walkAgents(dir) {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;\n  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);\n    if (e.isDirectory()) { walkAgents(p); continue; }\n    if (!/\\.(mjs|md|json)$/.test(e.name)) continue;\n    if (secretRe.test(fs.readFileSync(p, \"utf8\"))) ok(`no secret in ${path.relative(ROOT, p)}`, false, \"MATCH\");\n  }\n})(agentsDir);\nok(`scanned ${scanned.length} addon files, .agents clean`, true);\n\nconsole.log(\"[freebuff-ext] meta wiring (hermetic: auth shape only)\");\nok(\"pi auth.json has meta oauth refresh (pi owns refresh)\", (() => {\n  try {\n    const a = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(os.homedir(), \".pi\", \"agent\", \"auth.json\"), \"utf8\"));\n    return a?.meta?.type === \"oauth\" && typeof a.meta.refresh === \"string\" && a.meta.refresh.length > 10;\n  } catch { return false; }\n})());\n\nif (LIVE) {\n  console.log(\"[freebuff-ext] live checks\");\n  try {\n    execFileSync(\"node\", [path.join(ADDON, \"bin\", \"meta-muse-key.mjs\"), \"--check\"], { stdio: \"pipe\", timeout: 45000 });\n    ok(\"meta key mint OK (key hidden)\", true);\n  } catch (e) {\n    ok(\"meta key mint OK (key hidden)\", false, e.message?.slice(0, 160));\n  }\n  try {\n    const r = await fetch(\"http://127.0.0.1:7778/health\", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });\n    ok(\"kernel /health reachable\", r.ok, `HTTP ${r.status}`);\n  } catch (e) {\n    ok(\"kernel /health reachable\", false, e.message?.slice(0, 120));\n  }\n}\n\nconsole.log(`[freebuff-ext] ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);\nprocess.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);\n"
}
```

```
-<!-- hypernexus:begin (managed by addons/codewhale/install.mjs — do not edit inside this block) -->
-## HyperNexus Control Plane (Codewhale)
+<!-- hypernexus:begin (managed by addons/hax/install.mjs — do not edit inside this block) -->
+## HyperNexus Control Plane (hax)
 
-This workspace is integrated with the local HyperNexus kernel (Go, port 7778) through the
-`hypernexus` MCP server (config: `.codewhale/mcp.json` / `~/.codewhale/mcp.json`). Its
-tools appear as `mcp__hypernexus__*` in the merged tool namespace.
+This workspace is backed by the local HyperNexus kernel (Go, port 7778) through the
+`addons/hax/hn.mjs` CLI driver (hax has no MCP/extension runtime by design; the CLI
+is the integration surface, composed via `hax -p` like any other Unix tool).
 
 Discipline loop for multi-step work: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**
 
-- **RECALL** — `mcp__hypernexus__search_memory` before re-deriving known answers or decisions.
-- **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps use `mcp__hypernexus__mcp_list_tools` then `mcp__hypernexus__mcp_call_tool`.
-- **PERSIST** — `mcp__hypernexus__add_memory` for non-obvious outcomes (tags: `topic,kind`);
-  `mcp__hypernexus__memory_extract_relations` for entities/relations worth keeping in the graph.
-- **VERIFY** — `mcp__hypernexus__system_status` before and after production changes.
-
-Memory tiers: L1 scratchpad (`memory_scratchpad_get/set/append`) for short-lived working
-state; L2 vault (`add_memory`/`search_memory`) for durable cross-session facts; L3 cold
-archive via `search_memory` with `include_cold: true`. Codewhale's own skills and session
-state are complementary to HyperNexus L2 — keep durable project facts in both.
-
-Preferred routing when both a native tool and a `mcp__hypernexus__*` tool fit: durable
-cross-session memory → `add_memory`/`search_memory`; short-lived state →
-`memory_scratchpad_*`; missing capability → `mcp_list_tools` → `mcp_call_tool`;
-kernel/ops → `system_status`/`billing_status`/`memory_stats`. Codewhale-native tools keep
-their own domains (files, shell, git, web); HyperNexus extends the plane, it never
-replaces them.
-
-Subagent persistence: Codewhale workers share this kernel. Persist findings with
-`mcp__hypernexus__add_memory` (append-only, safe to retry), summarize assignments tagged
-`session,summary,<worker-label>`, and set scratchpad key `goal` when work continues.
-
-Degradation contract: kernel calls never stall a turn. If `mcp__hypernexus__*` tools are
-unavailable or the kernel is down, continue with local tools and note the gap in one
-short sentence. All capabilities are also reachable over plain HTTP
-(`http://127.0.0.1:7778/health`, `/api/memory/search?query=`, `/api/mcp/tools`,
-`POST /api/agent/tool`) — the extension is an accelerator, not a dependency.
+Call the driver as `node addons/hax/hn.mjs …` (or `./addons/hax/hn …` when node is on PATH):
+
+- **RECALL** — `node addons/hax/hn.mjs fts "<keywords>"` then
+  `node addons/hax/hn.mjs harvest "<task>"` before deriving answers from scratch.
+- **DISCOVER** — `node addons/hax/hn.mjs tools "<need>"` / `suggest`, then
+  `node addons/hax/hn.mjs call <tool> '<args-json>'`. Orient with `repomap`/`code`.
+- **PERSIST** — `node addons/hax/hn.mjs store "<title>" "<content>" "t1,t2"`
+  for non-obvious outcomes; `handoff` at checkpoints; `graph` for entities/relations.
+- **VERIFY** — `node addons/hax/hn.mjs status` before and after production changes.
+- Commands: `@addons/hax/commands/hn-<name>.md` (store|search|status|tools|session|context|plan|summary|purge|orchestrate|remember) — @-mention one, the model reads it
+- Turn scripts: `sh addons/hax/scripts/hn-prime.sh "<task>"` (session start),
+  `sh addons/hax/scripts/hn-turn.sh pre|post|compact "<text>"` (turn maintenance).
+- Skills: `hypernexus`, `hypernexus-ops`, `hypernexus-workflows`
+  (`~/.config/hax/skills/`, mirrored in `addons/hax/skills/`).
+- Degradation contract: kernel calls never stall a turn. If the kernel is down
+  (`status` → `"ok": false`), continue from local files and say so in one sentence.
+  All surfaces degrade to a clear error object — never reassuring fiction.
```

## Assistant

Done — the Freebuff (`HARNESS=freebuff`) HyperNexus integration is built, installed, and fully verified. Since this is a closed binary with no plugin SDK, I used every native surface Freebuff actually offers, at parity with the pi / Crush / OpenCode / Droid adapters.

## Meta `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` + OAuth — working

- **Native surface:** Freebuff's `/model` picker already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (1M context). Per Freebuff's own README it is paid-plan locked without a plan — selecting it opens the plans page. No config can change that; the binary has no provider/`apiKeyHelper` surface (unlike Crush `crushrc` or Droid).
- **Subscription/OAuth path (verified live):** `addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs` mints a Meta Model API key live from pi's `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` (`meta.refresh`, pi owns refresh — same flow as pi's own OAuth and the Droid helper). I ran it: **mint OK (key hidden)**, and `meta-model.freebuff.md` documents the shell export + direct `https://api.meta.ai/v1` curl usage. Nothing secret is stored anywhere — verified by an automated no-secrets scan.

## Full HyperNexus integration for Freebuff

New adapter in `addons/freebuff/` (mirrors `addons/crush/`, `addons/opencode/`, `addons/droid/`):

- **`install.mjs`** — idempotent installer: `--project <dir>` (writes `.agents/`), `--global` (writes `~/.agents/`), `--check`, `--uninstall`. Merges the `hypernexus` stdio server into `.agents/mcp.json` without clobbering other servers, installs skills, writes the `AGENTS.md` discipline block.
- **MCP server config (patched in):** `.agents/mcp.json` now registers `hypernexus` → `bin/hypernexus.exe mcp` with `HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT` + `HYPERNEXUS_URL`, exposing all 58 kernel tools (`search_memory`, `add_memory`, `mcp_call_tool`, `codebase_search`, `repomap`, `system_status`, `billing_status`, …) plus HTTP fallbacks.
- **Skills (11, all `/skill:<name>`):** `hypernexus`, `hypernexus-ops`, `hypernexus-workflows` + 8 command-skills `hn-status/search/store/session/context/summary/tools/purge` — the full command parity of the other harnesses.
- **Hooks:** Freebuff exposes no hook API, so enforcement is by-convention: the RBAC audit rule, FTS-fast-path recall, and fail-open degradation contract are baked into the skill instructions and the `AGENTS.md` managed block (which Freebuff reads at chat start). This is documented honestly in the README rather than faked.
- **Overlays:** `prompt-overlay.md`, `preferred-tools.md`, `swarm-prompt-block.md`, plus `.agents/HYPERNEXUS.md` reference.
- **Manifest:** `addons/shared/hypernexus.manifest.json` gains the `freebuff` harness entry.
- **Battery:** `bin/test-freebuff-ext.mjs` — 31 checks, hermetic + `--live`.

## Verification (all passing)

- `install.mjs --check` → OK; `test-freebuff-ext.mjs` → **29/29**; `--live` → **31/31** (meta mint OK, kernel `/health` reachable).

## What you do now

1. `cd ~/workspace/HyperNexus && freebuff` (trust `.agents` when prompted, or launch with `--trust-agents`).
2. `/skill:hn-status` — kernel + MCP + memory + billing on one screen.

```
- path: bin\cdp.mjs
  content: |
      // Minimal CDP client over Node's built-in WebSocket. node bin/cdp.mjs <url> <js|@file>
      import { execSync } from "child_process";
      import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
      const [,, url, jsArg] = process.argv;
      // @file avoids shell-quoting mangling (PowerShell turned \"1+1\" into \1+1).
      const js = jsArg.startsWith("@") ? readFileSync(jsArg.slice(1), "utf8") : jsArg;
      const tabs = JSON.parse(execSync("curl.exe -s -m 10 http://127.0.0.1:9222/json").toString());
      const tab = tabs.find((t) => t.type === "page" && (t.url.includes("muse.ai") || t.url.includes(url)));
      if (!tab) { console.error("muse tab not found"); process.exit(1); }
      const ws = new WebSocket(tab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
      ws.onopen = () => {
        ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: "Runtime.evaluate", params: { expression: js, awaitPromise: true, returnByValue: true } }));
      };
      ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
        const d = JSON.parse(ev.data);
        if (d.id === 1) {
          const r = d.result?.result;
          const ex = d.result?.exceptionDetails;
          const exDetail = ex?.exception?.description || ex?.error?.text || ex?.text;
          console.log(r?.value !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(r.value) : (exDetail ?? r?.description ?? "no value"));
          ws.close(); process.exit(0);
        }
      };
      setTimeout(() => { console.error("CDP timeout"); process.exit(1); }, 30000);
```

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "probe-muse-cdp.mjs",
  "instructions": "Create read-only CDP probe for muse.ai tab",
  "content": "// Read-only CDP probe: reports muse.ai tab state, mutates nothing.\nconst res = await fetch(\"http://127.0.0.1:9222/json\", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) });\nconst tabs = await res.json();\nconst tab = tabs.find((t) => t.type === \"page\" && t.url.includes(\"muse.ai\") && !t.url.includes(\"/api/\"));\nif (!tab) { console.error(\"NO_MUSE_PAGE_TAB\"); process.exit(1); }\nconst ws = new WebSocket(tab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);\nconst killer = setTimeout(() => { console.error(\"CDP_TIMEOUT\"); process.exit(1); }, 20000);\nws.onopen = () => {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: \"Runtime.evaluate\", params: {\n    expression: `(() => { const ls=[]; try { for (let i=0;i<localStorage.length && i<25; i++) ls.push(localStorage.key(i)); } catch(e){} return { title: document.title, url: location.href, ready: document.readyState, lsKeys: ls, lsCount: (()=>{try{return localStorage.length}catch(e){return -1}})(), ssCount: (()=>{try{return sessionStorage.length}catch(e){return -1}})(), bodyLen: document.body ? document.body.innerText.length : 0 }; })()`,\n    awaitPromise: true, returnByValue: true,\n  } }));\n};\nws.onmessage = (ev) => {\n  const d = JSON.parse(ev.data);\n  if (d.id === 1) {\n    clearTimeout(killer);\n    console.log(JSON.stringify(d.result?.result?.value ?? { error: d.result }, null, 2));\n    ws.close(); process.exit(0);\n  }\n};\nws.onerror = (e) => { console.error(\"WS_ERROR\", e.message); process.exit(1); };\n"
}
```

```
- path: bin\cdp-send.mjs
  content: |
      // Type a message into muse.ai's chat input + submit it. node bin/cdp-send.mjs "<msg>|@file"
      import { execSync } from "child_process";
      import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
      const rawArg = process.argv.slice(2).join(" ");
      const msg = rawArg.startsWith("@") ? readFileSync(rawArg.slice(1), "utf8").trim() : rawArg;
      const tabs = JSON.parse(execSync("curl.exe -s -m 10 http://127.0.0.1:9222/json").toString());
      const tab = tabs.find((t) => t.type === "page" && t.url.includes("muse.ai"));
      if (!tab) { console.error("muse tab not found"); process.exit(1); }
      const ws = new WebSocket(tab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
      // Synchronous expression (NO setTimeout / awaitPromise): background tabs throttle
      // setTimeout, which would hang the evaluate. Set value -> input -> Enter.
      const js = `
      (function () {
        const ta = document.querySelector('textarea');
        if (!ta) return 'NO_TEXTAREA';
        const setter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype, 'value').set;
        setter.call(ta, ${JSON.stringify(msg)});
        ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
        ta.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, bubbles: true }));
        ta.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keypress', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, bubbles: true }));
        ta.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, bubbles: true }));
        return 'SUBMITTED';
      })()
      `;
      ws.onopen = () => ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: "Runtime.evaluate", params: { expression: js, returnByValue: true } }));
      ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
        const d = JSON.parse(ev.data);
        if (d.id === 1) { console.log("send result:", d.result?.result?.value ?? d.result?.exceptionDetails?.text); ws.close(); process.exit(0); }
      };
      setTimeout(() => { console.error("timeout"); process.exit(1); }, 60000);
      
- path: bin\_muse-read.mjs
  content: |
      import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
      import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
      const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
      const WebSocket = require('ws');
      
      const tabs = JSON.parse(execSync('curl -s http://127.0.0.1:9222/json').toString());
      const museTab = tabs.find(t => t.url === 'https://muse.ai/');
      if (!museTab) { console.log('No muse.ai tab'); process.exit(0); }
      
      const ws = new WebSocket(museTab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
      ws.on('open', () => {
        ws.send(JSON.stringify({id: 1, method: 'Runtime.evaluate', params: {
          expression: `(() => { const msgs = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="chat-message"], .message, [class*="message"], [class*="chat"] > div'); const texts = []; for (let i = Math.max(0, msgs.length-6); i < msgs.length; i++) { const t = msgs[i].textContent.trim(); if (t.length > 10) texts.push(t.slice(0, 500)); } return texts.join('\\n---\\n'); })()`,
          returnByValue: true
        }}));
      });
      ws.on('message', (data) => {
        const msg = JSON.parse(data.toString());
        if (msg.id === 1) { console.log(msg.result?.result?.value || 'no data'); ws.close(); process.exit(0); }
      });
      setTimeout(() => { console.log('timeout'); process.exit(0); }, 5000);
      
```

> _Reasoning_
> Diagnosing a WebSocket evaluate hang likely caused by background tab throttling and checking Node WS availability.

```json
{
  "path": "probe-ws-min.mjs",
  "instructions": "Minimal WS connectivity probe",
  "content": "// Minimal: connect to browser-level CDP WS and call Browser.getVersion.\nconsole.log(\"fetching version...\");\nconst res = await fetch(\"http://127.0.0.1:9222/json/version\", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) });\nconst v = await res.json();\nconsole.log(\"browser:\", v.Browser, \"ws:\", v.webSocketDebuggerUrl);\nconst ws = new WebSocket(v.webSocketDebuggerUrl);\nconsole.log(\"ws created, waiting for open...\");\nconst killer = setTimeout(() => { console.log(\"TIMEOUT_WAITING\"); process.exit(1); }, 15000);\nws.onopen = () => { console.log(\"OPEN\"); ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: \"Browser.getVersion\" })); };\nws.onmessage = (ev) => { clearTimeout(killer); console.log(\"MSG\", String(ev.data).slice(0, 300)); ws.close(); process.exit(0); };\nws.onerror = (e) => { console.log(\"WS_ERROR\", e.message || e.type); };\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "path": "probe-muse-page.mjs",
  "instructions": "Read-only muse.ai page probe, sync expression",
  "content": "// Read-only muse.ai page probe. Synchronous expression (background tabs throttle timers).\nconst res = await fetch(\"http://127.0.0.1:9222/json\", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) });\nconst tabs = await res.json();\nconst tab = tabs.find((t) => t.type === \"page\" && t.url === \"https://muse.ai/\");\nif (!tab) { console.log(\"NO_MUSE_PAGE_TAB\"); process.exit(1); }\nconsole.log(\"tab:\", tab.id, JSON.stringify(tab.title));\nconst ws = new WebSocket(tab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);\nconst killer = setTimeout(() => { console.log(\"TIMEOUT_WAITING\"); process.exit(1); }, 18000);\nws.onopen = () => {\n  console.log(\"OPEN\");\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: \"Runtime.evaluate\", params: {\n    expression: `(() => { const ls=[]; try { for (let i=0;i<Math.min(localStorage.length,25);i++) ls.push(localStorage.key(i)); } catch(e){} let ssN=-1; try { ssN=sessionStorage.length; } catch(e){} return { title: document.title, url: location.href, ready: document.readyState, lsCount: (()=>{try{return localStorage.length}catch(e){return -1}})(), lsKeys: ls, ssCount: ssN, bodyChars: document.body ? document.body.innerText.length : 0 }; })()`,\n    returnByValue: true,\n  } }));\n};\nws.onmessage = (ev) => {\n  clearTimeout(killer);\n  const d = JSON.parse(String(ev.data));\n  if (d.id === 1) {\n    const r = d.result?.result;\n    console.log(\"RESULT\", JSON.stringify(r?.value ?? { desc: r?.description, ex: d.result?.exceptionDetails }, null, 2).slice(0, 2000));\n    ws.close(); process.exit(0);\n  }\n};\nws.onerror = (e) => { console.log(\"WS_ERROR\", e.message || e.type); };\n"
}
```

```
- path: addons\hax\scripts\meta-env.sh
  content: |
      #!/bin/sh
      # meta-env.sh — export a LIVE Meta Muse OAuth key for hax from pi's auth.json
      # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # hax has no OAuth login flow for Meta (its /login manages codex only), so this
      # script bridges pi's Meta subscription into hax's provider model: it reads the
      # live `access` key minted by `pi` (Meta device flow → api.meta.ai/muse-code/key),
      # verifies it is still valid, and exports HAX_META_API_KEY for the hax process.
      #
      # Usage:
      #   eval "$(sh addons/hax/scripts/meta-env.sh)" && hax --preset meta "hello"
      #   eval "$(sh addons/hax/scripts/meta-env.sh)"   # then run hax interactively
      #
      # The minted key lives ~24h (expires field in pi auth.json). When it lapses,
      # re-run `pi` login for meta, then re-source this script. Nothing is written to
      # config.json — the key stays in the environment, per hax conventions.
      set -eu
      
      pick_auth() {
        for c in "$HOME/.pi/agent/auth.json" "${USERPROFILE:-}/.pi/agent/auth.json" \
                 "C:/Users/hyper/.pi/agent/auth.json" "/c/Users/hyper/.pi/agent/auth.json"; do
          [ -f "$c" ] && { printf '%s' "$c"; return 0; }
        done
        return 1
      }
      
      AUTH="$(pick_auth 2>/dev/null || true)"
      if [ -z "${AUTH:-}" ]; then
        echo "meta-env.sh: no pi auth.json found (expected ~/.pi/agent/auth.json)" >&2
        return 1 2>/dev/null || exit 1
      fi
      
      META_KEY="$(python3 -c "
      import json,sys
      try:
        d=json.load(open(r'''$AUTH'''))
        m=d.get('meta') or {}
        print(m.get('access',''))
      except Exception as e:
        print('ERR:'+str(e), file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(1)
      ")"
      case "$META_KEY" in
        ''|ERR:*) echo "meta-env.sh: no meta access key in $AUTH — run pi and log in with meta first" >&2; return 1 2>/dev/null || exit 1;;
      esac
      
      # Validate against the Meta endpoint before exporting (cheap GET /models).
      if ! python3 -c "
      import sys,urllib.request
      req=urllib.request.Request('https://api.meta.ai/v1/models',headers={'Authorization':'Bearer $META_KEY'})
      try:
        urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=20).read(64)
      except Exception as e:
        print('meta key validation failed: '+str(e)[:200],file=sys.stderr); sys.exit(1)
      " 2>/dev/null; then
        # Fall through with a warning, not a hard failure: the key may still work for
        # /responses even if /models is restricted on this plan.
        echo "meta-env.sh: warning: key did not validate on GET /models; exporting anyway" >&2
      fi
      
      export HAX_META_API_KEY="$META_KEY"
      # Also satisfy hax's shipped openai-compatible env binding for ad-hoc runs.
      export HAX_OPENAI_API_KEY="$META_KEY"
      export HAX_OPENAI_BASE_URL="${HAX_OPENAI_BASE_URL:-https://api.meta.ai/v1}"
      export HAX_OPENAI_API="${HAX_OPENAI_API:-responses}"
      echo "export HAX_META_API_KEY='$META_KEY'"
      echo "export HAX_OPENAI_API_KEY='$META_KEY'"
      echo "export HAX_OPENAI_BASE_URL='$HAX_OPENAI_BASE_URL'"
      echo "export HAX_OPENAI_API='$HAX_OPENAI_API'"
      echo "# meta-env.sh: live Meta Muse key exported ($(printf '%s' "$META_KEY" | wc -c | tr -d ' ') chars) from $AUTH" >&2
      
```

> _Reasoning_
> Distinguishing two Meta credential types and noting hax expects the access token while the helper outputs a model API key.
