````

- path: addons\freebuff\install.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /**
       * install.mjs — HyperNexus → Freebuff integration installer
       * ==========================================================
       * Installs the full HyperNexus control-plane surface for Freebuff (Codebuff
       * platform). Idempotent: the MCP server entry is merged by key, skills are
       * overwritten, and the AGENTS.md overlay is kept inside managed markers and
       * can be re-run or removed safely.
       *
       * Modes:
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs                    install into this project (.agents/)
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project <dir>    install into <dir>/.agents/
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --global            install into ~/.agents/ (covers all projects under $HOME)
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --check      verify installation (exit 1 on gaps)
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --uninstall   remove managed files/blocks
       *
       * What it installs (project → <dir>/.agents/  |  global → ~/.agents/):
       *   mcp.json            hypernexus stdio server merged into mcpServers (never clobbers other servers)
       *   skills dir          3 core skills (hypernexus, hypernexus-ops, hypernexus-workflows)
       *                       + 8 command-skills (hn-status, hn-search, hn-store, hn-session,
       *                       hn-context, hn-summary, hn-tools, hn-purge)
       *   HYPERNEXUS.md       overlay reference (discipline loop + tool map + Meta notes)
       *   AGENTS.md block     discipline overlay between managed markers (project mode only;
       *                       global mode skips it — freebuff reads AGENTS.md per project)
       *
       * The installer never stores secrets: the Meta token is read LIVE from
       * ~/.pi/agent/auth.json at every mint (pi owns OAuth refresh). See
       * meta-model.freebuff.md + bin/meta-muse-key.mjs.
       * After install: restart freebuff in the project (trust .agents when prompted,
       * or launch with --trust-agents) so the new mcp.json + skills load.
       */
      
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import path from "node:path";
      import os from "node:os";
      import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
      
      const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
      const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..");
      const HOME = os.homedir() || process.env.USERPROFILE || process.env.HOME || "";
      
      const MODE = process.argv.includes("--uninstall")
        ? "uninstall"
        : process.argv.includes("--check")
          ? "check"
          : "install";
      const GLOBAL = process.argv.includes("--global");
      
      function argValue(flag) {
        const i = process.argv.indexOf(flag);
        if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] && !process.argv[i + 1].startsWith("--")) return process.argv[i + 1];
        const eq = process.argv.find((a) => a.startsWith(flag + "="));
        return eq ? eq.slice(flag.length + 1) : "";
      }
      
      const TARGET_OVERRIDE = argValue("--project") || argValue("--target");
      const PROJECT_DIR = path.resolve(TARGET_OVERRIDE || (GLOBAL ? HOME : process.cwd()));
      const AGENTS_DIR = GLOBAL ? path.join(HOME, ".agents") : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, ".agents");
      const AGENTS_FILE = GLOBAL ? null : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, "AGENTS.md"); // global has no single project root
      
      const MD_BEGIN = "<!-- hypernexus:begin:freebuff (managed by addons/freebuff/install.mjs — do not edit inside this block) -->";
      const MD_END = "<!-- hypernexus:end:freebuff -->";
      
      const CORE_SKILLS = ["hypernexus", "hypernexus-ops", "hypernexus-workflows"];
      const COMMANDS = ["hn-status", "hn-search", "hn-store", "hn-session", "hn-context", "hn-summary", "hn-tools", "hn-purge"];
      
      // ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function log(msg) {
        console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] ${msg}`);
      }
      
      function readIfExists(p) {
        try {
          return fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      }
      
      function writeUtf8(file, content) {
        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
        fs.writeFileSync(file, content, "utf8"); // utf8 → no BOM (Go/Node parsers reject BOM)
      }
      
      /** Forward-slash exe path — backslashes break MCP shell expansion on Windows. */
      function resolveExe() {
        const exe = process.platform === "win32" ? "hypernexus.exe" : "hypernexus";
        const override = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_MCP_COMMAND;
        if (override) return override;
        for (const c of [
          path.join(REPO_ROOT, "bin", exe),
          path.join(PROJECT_DIR, "bin", exe),
        ]) {
          if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c.split(path.sep).join("/");
        }
        return exe;
      }
      
      function workspaceRoot() {
        const ws = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT || (GLOBAL ? HOME : PROJECT_DIR);
        return ws.split(path.sep).join("/");
      }
      
      function upsertManagedBlock(filePath, blockText) {
        const block = blockText.trim();
        const existing = readIfExists(filePath) ?? "";
        // NOTE: unique :freebuff markers — sibling harnesses (hax, codewhale) share the
        // generic hypernexus:begin/end markers, so never match those here.
        const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_BEGIN);
        const endIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_END);
        let next;
        if (beginIdx >= 0 && endIdx > beginIdx) {
          next = existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + block + existing.slice(endIdx + MD_END.length);
        } else {
          next = existing.trimEnd() + (existing.trimEnd() ? "\n\n" : "") + block + "\n";
        }
        writeUtf8(filePath, next);
        return existing !== next;
      }
      
      function removeManagedBlock(filePath) {
        const existing = readIfExists(filePath);
        if (existing === null) return false;
        const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_BEGIN);
        const endIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_END);
        if (beginIdx < 0 || endIdx < beginIdx) return false;
        const next = (existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + existing.slice(endIdx + MD_END.length)).replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n");
        if (next.trim() === "") {
          fs.rmSync(filePath, { force: true });
          return true;
        }
        writeUtf8(filePath, next);
        return true;
      }
      
      function agentsBlock() {
        return `${MD_BEGIN}
      ## HyperNexus Control Plane (freebuff)
      
      This workspace is backed by the local HyperNexus kernel (Go, port 7778).
      Freebuff loads this file at chat start plus \`.agents/mcp.json\` and
      \`.agents/skills/*\` (each skill invocable as \`/skill:<name>\`).
      
      Discipline loop for multi-step work: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**
      
      - **RECALL** — before re-deriving: FTS fast path \`GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8\`
        (~15ms), semantic \`hypernexus\` MCP \`search_memory\` for depth, scratchpad \`goal\`.
      - **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: \`mcp_list_servers\` → \`mcp_list_tools\` →
        semantic \`/api/mcp/tools/search\`, then route with \`mcp_call_tool\`.
      - **PERSIST** — non-obvious outcomes via \`add_memory\` (tags \`topic,kind\`) +
        \`memory_extract_relations\`; session handoffs via \`/skill:hn-summary\`.
      - **VERIFY** — \`system_status\` + \`billing_status\` before/after production changes.
      - Skills: \`/skill:hypernexus\` \`/skill:hypernexus-ops\` \`/skill:hypernexus-workflows\`
        + command-skills \`/skill:hn-status|hn-search|hn-store|hn-session|hn-context|hn-summary|hn-tools|hn-purge\`.
      - Meta model: \`/model\` already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (paid-plan locked without a plan);
        subscription/OAuth path = \`node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs\` (pi owns refresh;
        see \`addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md\`).
      - Degradation contract: kernel calls are budgeted and never stall a turn. If the kernel
        is down (\`curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/health\` fails), say so in one sentence and
        continue with local tools — never mock state.
      ${MD_END}`;
      }
      
      // ── install steps ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function installMcpJson() {
        const file = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "mcp.json");
        let config = {};
        const existing = readIfExists(file);
        if (existing) {
          try {
            config = JSON.parse(existing);
          } catch (e) {
            log(`WARNING: could not parse ${file}: ${e.message}. Leaving it untouched.`);
            return false;
          }
        }
        config.mcpServers = config.mcpServers && typeof config.mcpServers === "object" ? config.mcpServers : {};
        config.mcpServers.hypernexus = {
          command: resolveExe(),
          args: ["mcp"],
          env: {
            HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT: workspaceRoot(),
            HYPERNEXUS_URL: process.env.HYPERNEXUS_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:7778",
          },
          timeout: 30,
        };
        writeUtf8(file, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + "\n");
        return true;
      }
      
      function installSkills() {
        let count = 0;
        for (const name of CORE_SKILLS) {
          const src = path.join(__dirname, "skills", name, "SKILL.md");
          const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name, "SKILL.md");
          if (!fs.existsSync(src)) {
            log(`WARNING: missing source skill ${src}, skipping.`);
            continue;
          }
          writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, "utf8"));
          count++;
        }
        for (const name of COMMANDS) {
          const src = path.join(__dirname, "commands", `${name}.md`);
          const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name, "SKILL.md");
          if (!fs.existsSync(src)) {
            log(`WARNING: missing source command ${src}, skipping.`);
            continue;
          }
          writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, "utf8"));
          count++;
        }
        return count;
      }
      
      function installOverlay() {
        const overlay = readIfExists(path.join(__dirname, "prompt-overlay.md")) ?? "";
        const content = `# HyperNexus (Freebuff reference)\n\n> Installed by \`addons/freebuff/install.mjs\`. Source of truth lives in
      > \`addons/freebuff/\`; this copy is what Freebuff reads from \`.agents/\`.\n\n${overlay.trim()}\n`;
        writeUtf8(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "HYPERNEXUS.md"), content);
      }
      
      // ── check / uninstall ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function checkAll() {
        const gaps = [];
        const mcpFile = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "mcp.json");
        const raw = readIfExists(mcpFile);
        if (!raw) {
          gaps.push(`missing ${mcpFile}`);
        } else {
          try {
            const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (!cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus) gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: no mcpServers.hypernexus entry`);
          } catch {
            gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: invalid JSON`);
          }
        }
        for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, ...COMMANDS]) {
          if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name, "SKILL.md"))) {
            gaps.push(`missing skill ${name}`);
          }
        }
        if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "HYPERNEXUS.md"))) gaps.push("missing HYPERNEXUS.md");
        if (AGENTS_FILE) {
          const agents = readIfExists(AGENTS_FILE) ?? "";
          if (!agents.includes("<!-- hypernexus:begin")) gaps.push("AGENTS.md: no managed hypernexus block");
        }
        const keyHelper = path.join(__dirname, "bin", "meta-muse-key.mjs");
        if (!fs.existsSync(keyHelper)) gaps.push("missing bin/meta-muse-key.mjs");
        if (gaps.length) {
          console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] CHECK FAILED (${gaps.length} gap(s)):\n  - ${gaps.join("\n  - ")}`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
        console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] CHECK OK: MCP + ${CORE_SKILLS.length + COMMANDS.length} skills + overlay present.`);
      }
      
      function uninstallAll() {
        let removed = 0;
        const mcpFile = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "mcp.json");
        const raw = readIfExists(mcpFile);
        if (raw) {
          try {
            const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus) {
              delete cfg.mcpServers.hypernexus;
              if (Object.keys(cfg.mcpServers).length === 0) fs.rmSync(mcpFile, { force: true });
              else writeUtf8(mcpFile, JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2) + "\n");
              removed++;
            }
          } catch { /* leave unparseable file alone */ }
        }
        for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, ...COMMANDS]) {
          const dir = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name);
          if (fs.existsSync(dir)) {
            fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
            removed++;
          }
        }
        for (const f of [path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "HYPERNEXUS.md")]) {
          if (fs.existsSync(f)) {
            fs.rmSync(f, { force: true });
            removed++;
          }
        }
        if (AGENTS_FILE && removeManagedBlock(AGENTS_FILE)) removed++;
        log(`uninstalled (${removed} item(s) removed).`);
      }
      
      // ── main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      if (MODE === "check") {
        checkAll();
      } else if (MODE === "uninstall") {
        uninstallAll();
      } else {
        const mcpOk = installMcpJson();
        const skills = installSkills();
        installOverlay();
        let agentsNote = "skipped (global mode: freebuff reads AGENTS.md per project)";
        if (AGENTS_FILE) {
          const changed = upsertManagedBlock(AGENTS_FILE, agentsBlock());
          agentsNote = changed ? "managed block written" : "managed block already current";
        }
        log(`installed into ${AGENTS_DIR} (mcp.json ${mcpOk ? "merged" : "SKIPPED — unparseable"}, ${skills} skills, HYPERNEXUS.md; AGENTS.md ${agentsNote}).`);
        log(`restart freebuff in ${PROJECT_DIR} (trust .agents when prompted, or use --trust-agents).`);
        log(`verify: node addons/freebuff/install.mjs ${GLOBAL ? "--global" : `--project ${PROJECT_DIR}`} --check`);
      }
      
- path: addons\freebuff\mcp.freebuff.snippet.json
  content: |
      {
        "mcpServers": {
          "hypernexus": {
            "command": "C:/Users/hyper/workspace/HyperNexus/bin/hypernexus.exe",
            "args": ["mcp"],
            "env": {
              "HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT": "C:/Users/hyper/workspace/HyperNexus",
              "HYPERNEXUS_URL": "http://127.0.0.1:7778"
            },
            "timeout": 30
          }
        }
      }
      
- path: addons\freebuff\meta-model.freebuff.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus — Meta model wiring for Freebuff (REFERENCE, no secrets inside)
      
      Model: `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` via Meta's Model API.
      Auth is read LIVE from pi's `auth.json` at every call (pi owns OAuth refresh;
      nothing is stored in this file or any config).
      
      ## Native surface: the `/model` picker already ships it
      
      Freebuff's `/model` picker already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (1M-token
      context). Per the Freebuff README it **requires a paid plan**: without one it
      is listed but locked, and selecting it opens the plans page. So "native"
      access = pick it in `/model` once your plan unlocks it — no config needed.
      
      ## Subscription/OAuth path: mint a Model API key live from pi
      
      When you want the Meta subscription credential (not Freebucks) for direct
      Meta API calls from inside a Freebuff session, mint it live — exactly like
      the Droid `apiKeyHelper` does:
      
      ```bash
      # Check the mint works (key hidden):
      node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check
      
      # Mint a key for the current shell only (never commit, never store):
      export META_MUSE_API_KEY="$(node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs)"
      ```
      
      How it works (mirrors pi's own Meta OAuth flow,
      `@earendil-works/pi-ai` `dist/auth/oauth/meta.js`):
      
      1. Read identity token from `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` → `meta.refresh`
         (`META_AUTH_PATH` overrides the path).
      2. `POST https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key`
         (`META_KEY_MINT_URL` overrides) with `Authorization: Bearer <identity>`.
      3. Print ONLY the minted `api_key` to stdout.
      
      Direct call example (OpenAI-compatible endpoint, key from step above):
      
      ```bash
      export META_MUSE_API_KEY="$(node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs)"
      curl -s https://api.meta.ai/v1/chat/completions \
        -H "Authorization: Bearer $META_MUSE_API_KEY" \
        -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
        -d '{"model":"muse-spark-1.3-contributor","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"reply with exactly: META_OK"}]}'
      ```
      
      ## Verify
      
      ```bash
      node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check   # expect: mint OK (key hidden)
      ```
      
      If it reports an expired session (401/403), re-login in pi (`/login meta`
      in pi) — identity tokens are not renewable; pi owns the refresh.
      
      ## Rules
      
      - This file and the installer NEVER write the token or minted key anywhere.
      - `auth.json` is read at call time, every time — rotation in pi is picked up
        automatically.
      - `META_AUTH_PATH` / `META_KEY_MINT_URL` env overrides exist for tests.
      
- path: addons\freebuff\README.md
  content: |
      # HyperNexus for Freebuff — full integration (Codebuff platform)
      
      Freebuff (`freebuff`, built on the [Codebuff](https://codebuff.com) platform,
      config dir `~/.config/manicode/`) integration for the HyperNexus AI control
      plane (Go kernel `http://127.0.0.1:7778`, dashboard 7779): persistent
      multi-tier memory (L1 scratchpad / L2 vault / L3 cold archive), semantic MCP
      tool discovery + routing, skill registry, sessions, knowledge graph, code
      search, system + billing status — plus Meta `muse-spark-1.3-contributor`
      wiring borrowed from pi (`~/.pi/agent/auth.json`, pi owns refresh).
      
      ## What Freebuff natively supports (verified surfaces)
      
      | Freebuff surface | This addon |
      |---|---|
      | `AGENTS.md` (project root, fallback `CLAUDE.md`) | managed discipline block via installer |
      | `.agents/mcp.json` (standard `mcpServers` format, projectDir/parents) | `hypernexus` stdio server (`bin/hypernexus.exe mcp`, 58 tools) |
      | `.agents/skills/*` and `.claude/skills/*` → `/skill:<name>` | 3 core skills + 8 `hn-*` command-skills |
      | `freebuff --continue` / `/export` | session resume + transcript export (native) |
      | `/model` picker | already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (1M context; paid-plan locked per Freebuff README — see Meta section below) |
      
      Freebuff has **no custom-provider / apiKeyHelper surface** (unlike Crush
      `crushrc` or Droid `apiKeyHelper`), so Meta OAuth is wired as a live-read
      helper + direct-API docs rather than a provider block. Nothing secret is ever
      stored in this repo.
      
      ## What you get (parity with pi / opencode / crush / mimo / jcode)
      
      | Surface | This addon |
      |---|---|
      | MCP server | `hypernexus` stdio (`bin/hypernexus.exe mcp`, 58 tools) — discover via `mcp_list_tools`, route via `mcp_call_tool` |
      | Skills (root `.agents/skills/`) | `hypernexus`, `hypernexus-ops`, `hypernexus-workflows` — model- + user-invocable via `/skill:<name>` |
      | Command-skills | `hn-status`, `hn-search`, `hn-store`, `hn-session`, `hn-context`, `hn-summary`, `hn-tools`, `hn-purge` → `/skill:hn-*` |
      | Meta model | `meta-model.freebuff.md` + `bin/meta-muse-key.mjs` — mint a Meta Model API key from pi OAuth live at call time (pi owns refresh) |
      | Overlay | `prompt-overlay.md` (discipline loop), `preferred-tools.md` (routing), `swarm-prompt-block.md` |
      | Installer | `install.mjs` — project (`.agents/`) or home-global (`~/.agents/`), `--check`, `--uninstall` |
      
      ## Quick start
      
      ```bash
      # 1. Install into this project (writes .agents/ + AGENTS.md managed block):
      node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project .
      
      # 2. Or cover every project under $HOME (writes ~/.agents/):
      node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --global
      
      # 3. Restart freebuff in this repo (trust .agents when prompted, or run with --trust-agents), then:
      #    /skill:hn-status    kernel + MCP + memory + billing, one screen
      #    /skill:hn-search    cross-session recall
      #    /skill:hn-tools    26k+ MCP tool discovery + routing
      
      # 4. Verify the install without touching anything:
      node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --check
      
      # 5. Meta subscription model (no secrets stored — pi owns OAuth refresh):
      node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check   # mint OK?
      ```
      
      ## Files
      
      - `install.mjs` — idempotent installer (`--project <dir>` default cwd, `--global`, `--check`, `--uninstall`)
      - `mcp.freebuff.snippet.json` — HyperNexus MCP server fragment for `.agents/mcp.json` (reference)
      - `meta-model.freebuff.md` — Meta `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` wiring for Freebuff (reference, no secrets)
      - `bin/meta-muse-key.mjs` — mint a Meta Model API key from pi `auth.json` identity (live-read, never stored)
      - `prompt-overlay.md` — memory-first discipline + tool map (managed into `AGENTS.md`)
      - `preferred-tools.md` — which HyperNexus surface to use for what
      - `swarm-prompt-block.md` — multi-agent persistence contract
      - `skills/*/SKILL.md` — 3 core skills (freebuff-flavored)
      - `commands/hn-*.md` — 8 command prompt files (installed as `/skill:hn-*` command-skills)
      
- path: bin\test-freebuff-ext.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /**
       * test-freebuff-ext.mjs — HyperNexus Freebuff integration battery.
       * Verifies the full freebuff surface without touching the network:
       * manifest entry, installer outputs, MCP config, skills, overlays,
       * Meta helper wiring (auth shape only — mint needs network, covered by --live).
       *
       *   node bin/test-freebuff-ext.mjs          hermetic checks
       *   node bin/test-freebuff-ext.mjs --live   + Meta key mint + kernel /health
       */
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import path from "node:path";
      import os from "node:os";
      import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
      import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
      
      const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
      const ADDON = path.join(ROOT, "addons", "freebuff");
      const LIVE = process.argv.includes("--live");
      
      let pass = 0;
      let fail = 0;
      function ok(name, cond, detail = "") {
        if (cond) {
          pass++;
          console.log(`  ok   ${name}`);
        } else {
          fail++;
          console.log(`  FAIL ${name}${detail ? ` — ${detail}` : ""}`);
        }
      }
      
      console.log("[freebuff-ext] manifest");
      const manifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "addons", "shared", "hypernexus.manifest.json"), "utf8"));
      const fb = manifest?.harnesses?.freebuff;
      ok("manifest harnesses.freebuff active", fb?.status === "active", fb?.status);
      ok("manifest installer path exists", fs.existsSync(path.join(ROOT, fb?.installer ?? "nope")));
      
      console.log("[freebuff-ext] installer outputs (project install)");
      const agentsDir = path.join(ROOT, ".agents");
      ok(".agents/mcp.json valid JSON with hypernexus server", (() => {
        try {
          const cfg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(agentsDir, "mcp.json"), "utf8"));
          const s = cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus;
          return !!s && Array.isArray(s.args) && s.args.includes("mcp") && typeof s.command === "string";
        } catch { return false; }
      })());
      const expectedSkills = ["hypernexus", "hypernexus-ops", "hypernexus-workflows",
        "hn-status", "hn-search", "hn-store", "hn-session", "hn-context", "hn-summary", "hn-tools", "hn-purge"];
      const missing = expectedSkills.filter((s) => !fs.existsSync(path.join(agentsDir, "skills", s, "SKILL.md")));
      ok(`all ${expectedSkills.length} skills installed`, missing.length === 0, missing.join(","));
      ok("HYPERNEXUS.md overlay installed", fs.existsSync(path.join(agentsDir, "HYPERNEXUS.md")));
      ok("AGENTS.md managed block present", (fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "AGENTS.md"), "utf8")).includes("hypernexus:begin:freebuff"));
      ok("sibling hax block untouched", (fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "AGENTS.md"), "utf8")).includes("managed by addons/hax/install.mjs"));
      
      console.log("[freebuff-ext] addon sources");
      for (const f of ["README.md", "install.mjs", "mcp.freebuff.snippet.json", "meta-model.freebuff.md",
        "prompt-overlay.md", "preferred-tools.md", "swarm-prompt-block.md",
        path.join("bin", "meta-muse-key.mjs"),
        path.join("skills", "hypernexus", "SKILL.md"),
        path.join("skills", "hypernexus-ops", "SKILL.md"),
        path.join("skills", "hypernexus-workflows", "SKILL.md"),
        ...["hn-status", "hn-search", "hn-store", "hn-session", "hn-context", "hn-summary", "hn-tools", "hn-purge"].map((c) => path.join("commands", `${c}.md`))]) {
        ok(`source ${f}`, fs.existsSync(path.join(ADDON, f)));
      }
      ok("snippet is valid JSON with hypernexus server", (() => {
        try {
          const s = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ADDON, "mcp.freebuff.snippet.json"), "utf8"));
          return !!s?.mcpServers?.hypernexus;
        } catch { return false; }
      })());
      
      console.log("[freebuff-ext] no-secrets scan");
      const secretRe = /(dca:[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{10,}|sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}|"access":\s*"LLM\|)/;
      const scanned = [];
      (function walk(dir) {
        for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
          const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
          if (e.isDirectory()) { if (e.name !== "node_modules") walk(p); continue; }
          if (!/\.(mjs|md|json|jsonc)$/.test(e.name)) continue;
          scanned.push(p);
          const t = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
          if (secretRe.test(t)) ok(`no secret in ${path.relative(ROOT, p)}`, false, "MATCH");
        }
      })(ADDON);
      (function walkAgents(dir) {
        if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
        for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
          const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
          if (e.isDirectory()) { walkAgents(p); continue; }
          if (!/\.(mjs|md|json)$/.test(e.name)) continue;
          if (secretRe.test(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8"))) ok(`no secret in ${path.relative(ROOT, p)}`, false, "MATCH");
        }
      })(agentsDir);
      ok(`scanned ${scanned.length} addon files, .agents clean`, true);
      
      console.log("[freebuff-ext] meta wiring (hermetic: auth shape only)");
      ok("pi auth.json has meta oauth refresh (pi owns refresh)", (() => {
        try {
          const a = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(os.homedir(), ".pi", "agent", "auth.json"), "utf8"));
          return a?.meta?.type === "oauth" && typeof a.meta.refresh === "string" && a.meta.refresh.length > 10;
        } catch { return false; }
      })());
      
      if (LIVE) {
        console.log("[freebuff-ext] live checks");
        try {
          execFileSync("node", [path.join(ADDON, "bin", "meta-muse-key.mjs"), "--check"], { stdio: "pipe", timeout: 45000 });
          ok("meta key mint OK (key hidden)", true);
        } catch (e) {
          ok("meta key mint OK (key hidden)", false, e.message?.slice(0, 160));
        }
        try {
          const r = await fetch("http://127.0.0.1:7778/health", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
          ok("kernel /health reachable", r.ok, `HTTP ${r.status}`);
        } catch (e) {
          ok("kernel /health reachable", false, e.message?.slice(0, 120));
        }
      }
      
      console.log(`[freebuff-ext] ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
      process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
      
````

- path: addons\freebuff\bin\meta-muse-key.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /*
       * meta-muse-key.mjs — mint a Meta Model API key from the pi auth.json identity token.
       *
       * Freebuff edition of addons/droid/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs (same flow — freebuff
       * has no apiKeyHelper surface, so call this live from the shell or a session).
       *
       * Mirrors pi's own Meta OAuth flow (see @earendil-works/pi-ai dist/auth/oauth/meta.js):
       *   1. Read identity token from ~/.pi/agent/auth.json -> meta.refresh
       *   2. POST https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key with Authorization: Bearer <identity>
       *   3. Print ONLY the minted api_key to stdout.
       *
       * Usage:
       *   node meta-muse-key.mjs            # prints minted key
       *   node meta-muse-key.mjs --check     # exit 0 if mint works, 1 otherwise
       *
       * Env overrides:
       *   META_AUTH_PATH      path to pi auth.json (default ~/.pi/agent/auth.json)
       *   META_KEY_MINT_URL   default https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key
       */
      
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import os from "node:os";
      import path from "node:path";
      
      const AUTH_PATH =
        process.env.META_AUTH_PATH || path.join(os.homedir(), ".pi", "agent", "auth.json");
      const MINT_URL = process.env.META_KEY_MINT_URL || "https://api.meta.ai/muse-code/key";
      const TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
      
      function fail(msg) {
        process.stderr.write(`[meta-muse-key] ${msg}\n`);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      function readIdentityToken() {
        let raw;
        try {
          raw = fs.readFileSync(AUTH_PATH, "utf8");
        } catch (e) {
          fail(`cannot read auth file ${AUTH_PATH}: ${e.message}. Run /login meta in pi first.`);
        }
        let data;
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(raw);
        } catch {
          fail(`auth file ${AUTH_PATH} is not valid JSON.`);
        }
        const cred = data?.meta;
        if (!cred || cred.type !== "oauth" || typeof cred.refresh !== "string" || !cred.refresh) {
          fail(
            `no Meta OAuth credential in ${AUTH_PATH} (expected .meta.refresh). Run /login meta in pi to sign in.`
          );
        }
        return cred.refresh;
      }
      
      async function mintApiKey(identityToken, signal) {
        const res = await fetch(MINT_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            Accept: "application/json",
            Authorization: `Bearer ${identityToken}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "x-api-version": "1.0.0",
          },
          body: "{}",
          signal,
        });
        let json = null;
        try {
          json = await res.json();
        } catch { /* non-JSON body */ }
        if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
          fail(
            `Meta session expired (HTTP ${res.status}). Identity tokens are not renewable — run /login meta in pi to sign in again.`
          );
        }
        if (!res.ok) {
          const detail = json && typeof json === "object"
            ? ["error_description", "detail", "message", "error"].map((k) => json[k]).find((v) => typeof v === "string" && v.trim())
            : null;
          fail(`Meta API key mint failed (HTTP ${res.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}.`);
        }
        const apiKey = json?.api_key;
        if (typeof apiKey !== "string" || !apiKey) {
          const actionUrl = typeof json?.action_url === "string" ? json.action_url : null;
          fail(`Meta did not issue an API key.${actionUrl ? ` Complete setup at ${actionUrl}` : ""}`);
        }
        return apiKey;
      }
      
      const checkOnly = process.argv.includes("--check");
      const identity = readIdentityToken();
      try {
        const key = await mintApiKey(identity, AbortSignal.timeout(TIMEOUT_MS));
        if (checkOnly) {
          process.stderr.write("[meta-muse-key] mint OK (key hidden)\n");
        } else {
          process.stdout.write(key);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (e?.name === "TimeoutError" || e?.name === "AbortError") fail(`mint request timed out after ${TIMEOUT_MS}ms.`);
        fail(e.message || String(e));
      }
      
- path: addons\freebuff\skills\hypernexus\SKILL.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hypernexus
      description: "HyperNexus AI control plane — persistent L2 vector memory, semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, billing, and agent orchestration. Use when the task benefits from cross-session memory (scratchpad, facts, observations), tool discovery across MCP servers, knowledge ingestion, system health checks, or routing through the HyperNexus Go kernel on port 7778. Trigger on mentions of 'hypernexus', 'HN', 'scratchpad', 'cross-session memory', 'MCP tools', 'tool discovery', 'knowledge graph', 'agent memory', or 'control plane'."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Integration (Freebuff)
      
      HyperNexus is a local AI control plane running a Go kernel on port 7778 (dashboard 7779) with persistent multi-tier memory (L1 scratchpad, L2 vector store, L3 cold archive), semantic tool discovery across 60+ MCP servers / 26,000+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, and agent orchestration.
      
      Invoke with `/skill:hypernexus`. Freebuff loads this skill from `.agents/skills/` (or `.claude/skills/`) — no plugin install step.
      
      ## When to Use
      
      - Start of any multi-step task → recall context (`search_memory`, fast FTS `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=`, scratchpad `memory_scratchpad_get`)
      - After a decision, gotcha, or non-obvious finding → store it (`add_memory` + `memory_extract_relations`)
      - When you need a capability you don't have → discover tools (`mcp_list_servers` / `mcp_list_tools`, semantic `GET /api/mcp/tools/search?query=`) then route (`mcp_call_tool`)
      - When the user asks about system health, billing, or the repo → `system_status`, `billing_status`, `repomap`, `codebase_search`
      
      ## Freebuff Tools (via the hypernexus MCP server, 58 tools)
      
      The MCP server (`.agents/mcp.json`, stdio `hypernexus mcp`) exposes these exact tool names. Prefer the MCP tool; the HTTP fallback works even when MCP is unreachable.
      
      | MCP tool | Purpose | HTTP fallback |
      |---|---|---|
      | `search_memory` | Semantic + keyword memory search (vector+FTS; slow ~6s on cold start, fast after warmup) | `GET /api/memory/search?query=` / `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=` (fast, ~15ms) |
      | `add_memory` | Store a fact / decision (content, tags, category) | `POST /api/memory/add` |
      | `delete_memory` | Delete a memory by id | `DELETE /api/memory/delete?id=` |
      | `memory_stats` | Tier counts: vault / scratchpad / relations | `GET /api/memory/stats` |
      | `memory_scratchpad_get` / `memory_scratchpad_set` / `memory_scratchpad_append` | L1 working memory (goal, persona, human) | `GET/POST /api/memory/scratchpad/*` |
      | `memory_extract_relations` / `memory_extract_relations_batch` | Knowledge graph: entities + relations | `POST /api/memory/extract-relations` |
      | `mcp_list_servers` | Live MCP server inventory | `GET /api/mcp/servers` |
      | `mcp_list_tools` | Tool inventory across connected servers | `GET /api/mcp/tools` |
      | `mcp_status` | Bridge status: connected count, fallback | `GET /api/mcp/status` |
      | `mcp_server_test` | Probe a downstream server (tools/list, ping) | — |
      | `mcp_call_tool` | Route a call (serverName, toolName, arguments) | `POST /api/agent/tool` |
      | `system_status` | Kernel liveness + version | `GET /health` |
      | `billing_status` | Provider quotas, depletion, fallback chain | `GET /api/billing/status` |
      | `get_system_stats` | OS / runtime metrics | — |
      | `codebase_search` | symbols / definitions / references (empty result when kernel catalog has no match) | `GET /api/mcp/native/search?query=` |
      | `codebase_outline` | Symbol outline for a file | — |
      | `repomap` | Ranked repo map with symbol summaries | — |
      | `read` / `write` / `edit` / `find` / `grep` / `ls` (+ `read_file` / `write_file` / `search` / `run_shell_command` / `bash` / `apply_search_replace`) | File + shell access through the kernel | local tools |
      | `code_interpreter` | Run python/node snippets (stateful session) | — |
      | `add_bookmark` | Save URL with title + tags | — |
      | `jira_create_issue` / `confluence_search` | Atlassian integration | — |
      | `cloud_troubleshoot` / `generate_devops_pipeline` | Ops generators | — |
      | `install_mcp_server` | Install an MCP server via npx | — |
      | `launch_webview` | Open a system browser window | — |
      | `download_llamafile` | Fetch a standalone model binary | — |
      | `list_processes` / `kill_process` | Process management (Windows) | — |
      
      ## Discipline loop
      
      ```
      RECALL → DISCOVER (if gap) → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY
      ```
      
      1. **RECALL** before re-deriving: FTS fast path first, semantic `search_memory` for depth; include scratchpad `goal`.
      2. **DISCOVER** on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` → semantic search → `mcp_call_tool`.
      3. **PERSIST** non-obvious outcomes: `add_memory` (tags `topic,kind`) + `memory_extract_relations`.
      4. **VERIFY** around production changes: `system_status` + `billing_status` before and after.
      
      If the kernel is down, say so in one short sentence and continue with local tools — never present mock state.
      
- path: addons\freebuff\commands\hn-status.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hn-status
      description: "Report HyperNexus kernel health, MCP bridge inventory, memory stats, and billing from Freebuff. Trigger on '/skill:hn-status', 'hypernexus status', or 'kernel health'."
      ---
      
      # /skill:hn-status
      
      Report HyperNexus control-plane status, without taking any action:
      
      1. Kernel liveness and version — `hypernexus MCP system_status` (fallback:
         `curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/health`).
      2. MCP bridge inventory — `hypernexus MCP mcp_status` (servers connected, tool count).
      3. Memory tiers — `hypernexus MCP memory_stats` (L2 vault / scratchpad / relations).
      4. Billing — `hypernexus MCP billing_status` (quotas, depleted models, fallback chain).
      
      Present as a short bullet list. If the kernel is unreachable, say so plainly and point
      at `bin\hypernexus.exe serve` — never show a mock state.

              "HYPERNEXUS_URL": "http://127.0.0.1:7778"
            },
            "timeout": 30
          }

- path: addons\crush\agent\hypernexus-orchestrator.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hypernexus-orchestrator
      description: "Orchestrate multi-step work through the HyperNexus control plane from Crush: recall, discover, execute, persist, verify. Use for complex tasks that benefit from cross-session memory, MCP tool routing, and auditable execution."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Orchestrator (Crush)
      
      You are the HyperNexus orchestrator. You coordinate work through the HyperNexus
      control plane (kernel `http://127.0.0.1:7778`, MCP server `hypernexus`, 58 tools).
      
      ## Loop
      
      1. **RECALL** — `search_memory` + `memory_scratchpad_get` (key `goal`) for the task
         topic before planning. Hot paths: FTS `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=`.
      2. **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` (+
         semantic `GET /api/mcp/tools/search?query=`) → `mcp_call_tool` to route.
      3. **EXECUTE** — do the work with normal tools; keep the plan in scratchpad key
         `goal`. Every kernel call is budgeted; on failure degrade to local tools.
      4. **PERSIST** — `add_memory` for every non-obvious decision/gotcha (tags:
         topic + `crush`); `memory_extract_relations` for entities; `memory_scratchpad_set`
         when the goal changes.
      5. **VERIFY** — `system_status` (+ `billing_status`, `memory_stats`) after
         significant changes.
      
      ## Rules
      
      - Never re-derive what memory already holds — recall first.
      - Keep hot-path calls on FTS search; full semantic search is for explicit calls.
      - End long sessions with `memory_scratchpad_set` (goal) + `add_memory` (tags
        `session,summary`) so the next session can resume (`/hn-summary` form).
      - If the kernel is offline, degrade gracefully: continue with local tools and
        note the gap in one short sentence.
      - Never claim a kernel write succeeded without tool output.
      
- path: addons\crush\hooks\hn-session.sh
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env bash
      # hn-session.sh — HyperNexus SessionStart bootstrap for Crush (static, instant).
      # =============================================================================
      # Prints the memory-first discipline as hook context. STATIC ONLY: no curl, no
      # python, no network — the live-memory variant hung Crush startup (~77s: Git
      # Bash cold start + Store-stub python + curl under non-interactive spawn
      # exceeded the config load deadline and bricked every `crush run`).
      # Live recall happens in-turn via MCP tools (search_memory, memory_scratchpad_get),
      # which is where the kernel is actually reachable. Never blocks, never throws.
      cat <<'JSON'
      {"hookSpecificOutput":{"additionalContext":"HyperNexus discipline for this session: RECALL before re-deriving (search project memory via hypernexus MCP memory_search/search_memory, fast path /api/memory/fts-search) -> DISCOVER via mcp_list_servers + mcp_list_tools on capability gaps -> PERSIST non-obvious outcomes with add_memory (tags: topic,kind) -> VERIFY with system_status/billing_status around production changes. MCP server 'hypernexus' (stdio) exposes the full tool plane; skills hypernexus / hypernexus-ops / hypernexus-workflows are on the skill path; commands /hn-status /hn-search /hn-store /hn-session /hn-context /hn-summary /hn-tools /hn-purge are available."}}
      JSON
      exit 0

      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hypernexus-ops
      description: "Operate and verify the HyperNexus kernel from Freebuff: health checks, MCP bridge inventory, billing/quotas, audit trail, and verify-before/after discipline for deployments and incidents. Trigger on 'kernel health', 'mcp servers', 'billing', 'quotas', 'audit', 'deploy verification', or 'hypernexus ops'."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Ops (Freebuff)
      
      Invoke with `/skill:hypernexus-ops`.
      
      ## Tools (via the hypernexus MCP server)
      
      - `system_status` — health before and after production changes (`GET /health` fallback)
      - `mcp_status` / `mcp_list_servers` / `mcp_list_tools` — MCP bridge inventory
      - Semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search?query=` — find the right downstream tool
      - `billing_status` — quotas, depleted models, fallback chain
      - Kernel audit trail — record compliance-relevant actions with `add_memory` (tags `ops,audit`)
      
      ## Verify loop (OPS workflow)
      
      1. VERIFY before change: `system_status`
      2. Act (with your normal tools)
      3. VERIFY after change: `system_status`
      4. PERSIST: `add_memory` with the outcome and tags `["ops", "deploy"]`
      
      ## Health endpoints
      
      ```bash
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/health                  # kernel liveness + version
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/system/overview     # full system view
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/mcp/status          # MCP bridge status
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/billing/status      # provider billing
      curl -s http://localhost:7778/api/billing/fallback-chain
      ```
      
      ## RBAC
      
      Freebuff has no permission-hook surface, so enforcement is by convention:
      destructive patterns (`rm -rf`, `sudo`, `chmod -R 777`, `DROP TABLE`,
      `git push --force`) get a kernel audit entry first — route them through a
      confirming prompt and log with `add_memory` tags `ops,rbac`.
      
- path: addons\freebuff\prompt-overlay.md
  content: |
      ## HyperNexus Discipline (Freebuff prompt overlay)
      
      Every turn in this workspace runs memory-first through the HyperNexus kernel
      (`http://127.0.0.1:7778`; MCP server `hypernexus`, 58 tools via stdio):
      
      ```
      RECALL -> DISCOVER (if gap) -> EXECUTE -> PERSIST -> VERIFY
      ```
      
      - **RECALL** — before re-deriving anything: FTS fast path
        (`/api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8`, ~15ms) for hot loops, semantic
        `search_memory` for depth. Include scratchpad `goal`.
      - **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` →
        semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search`, then route with `mcp_call_tool`. Persist what
        worked (`add_memory`, tags `tool-routing`).
      - **PERSIST** — non-obvious outcomes immediately: `add_memory`
        (tags `topic,kind`) + `memory_extract_relations` for entities/relations.
      - **VERIFY** — around production changes: `system_status` + `billing_status`
        before and after; never present mock state when the kernel is down.
      
      ### Tool map (MCP `hypernexus` server)
      
      | Need | Tool |
      |---|---|
      | fast recall | `memory_scratchpad_get/set/append`, FTS search endpoint |
      | durable recall | `search_memory`, `add_memory`, `delete_memory`, `memory_stats` |
      | graph | `memory_extract_relations`, `memory_extract_relations_batch` |
      | discovery/routing | `mcp_list_servers`, `mcp_list_tools`, `mcp_call_tool`, `mcp_status`, `mcp_server_test` |
      | code | `codebase_search`, `codebase_outline`, `repomap` |
      | ops | `system_status`, `billing_status`, `get_system_stats`, `list_processes` |
      | files/shell | `bash`, `read`, `write`, `edit`, `find`, `grep`, `ls` |
      | misc | `code_interpreter`, `apply_search_replace`, `add_bookmark`, `jira_create_issue`, `confluence_search`, `cloud_troubleshoot`, `generate_devops_pipeline`, `install_mcp_server`, `launch_webview`, `download_llamafile`, `echo`, `hello_world` |
      
      ### Skills & command-skills
      
      Skills (`/skill:<name>`): `hypernexus` (recall/store/discover), `hypernexus-ops`
      (health/billing/runbook), `hypernexus-workflows` (MEM-FIRST / DISCOVER / GRAPH /
      HANDOFF / OPS / HARVEST).
      Command-skills: `/skill:hn-status` `/skill:hn-search` `/skill:hn-store`
      `/skill:hn-session` `/skill:hn-context` `/skill:hn-summary` `/skill:hn-tools`
      `/skill:hn-purge`.
      
      ### Meta model
      
      `/model` already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (paid-plan locked without a plan).
      For the subscription/OAuth path, see `addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md`:
      mint live with `node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs --check`.
      
- path: .agents\skills\hypernexus\SKILL.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hypernexus
      description: "HyperNexus AI control plane — persistent L2 vector memory, semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, billing, and agent orchestration. Use when the task benefits from cross-session memory (scratchpad, facts, observations), tool discovery across MCP servers, knowledge ingestion, system health checks, or routing through the HyperNexus Go kernel on port 7778. Trigger on mentions of 'hypernexus', 'HN', 'scratchpad', 'cross-session memory', 'MCP tools', 'tool discovery', 'knowledge graph', 'agent memory', or 'control plane'."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Integration (Freebuff)
      
      HyperNexus is a local AI control plane running a Go kernel on port 7778 (dashboard 7779) with persistent multi-tier memory (L1 scratchpad, L2 vector store, L3 cold archive), semantic tool discovery across 60+ MCP servers / 26,000+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, and agent orchestration.
      
      Invoke with `/skill:hypernexus`. Freebuff loads this skill from `.agents/skills/` (or `.claude/skills/`) — no plugin install step.
      
      ## When to Use
      
      - Start of any multi-step task → recall context (`search_memory`, fast FTS `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=`, scratchpad `memory_scratchpad_get`)
      - After a decision, gotcha, or non-obvious finding → store it (`add_memory` + `memory_extract_relations`)
      - When you need a capability you don't have → discover tools (`mcp_list_servers` / `mcp_list_tools`, semantic `GET /api/mcp/tools/search?query=`) then route (`mcp_call_tool`)
      - When the user asks about system health, billing, or the repo → `system_status`, `billing_status`, `repomap`, `codebase_search`
      
      ## Freebuff Tools (via the hypernexus MCP server, 58 tools)
      
      The MCP server (`.agents/mcp.json`, stdio `hypernexus mcp`) exposes these exact tool names. Prefer the MCP tool; the HTTP fallback works even when MCP is unreachable.
      
      | MCP tool | Purpose | HTTP fallback |
      |---|---|---|
      | `search_memory` | Semantic + keyword memory search (vector+FTS; slow ~6s on cold start, fast after warmup) | `GET /api/memory/search?query=` / `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=` (fast, ~15ms) |
      | `add_memory` | Store a fact / decision (content, tags, category) | `POST /api/memory/add` |
      | `delete_memory` | Delete a memory by id | `DELETE /api/memory/delete?id=` |
      | `memory_stats` | Tier counts: vault / scratchpad / relations | `GET /api/memory/stats` |
      | `memory_scratchpad_get` / `memory_scratchpad_set` / `memory_scratchpad_append` | L1 working memory (goal, persona, human) | `GET/POST /api/memory/scratchpad/*` |
      | `memory_extract_relations` / `memory_extract_relations_batch` | Knowledge graph: entities + relations | `POST /api/memory/extract-relations` |
      | `mcp_list_servers` | Live MCP server inventory | `GET /api/mcp/servers` |
      | `mcp_list_tools` | Tool inventory across connected servers | `GET /api/mcp/tools` |
      | `mcp_status` | Bridge status: connected count, fallback | `GET /api/mcp/status` |
      | `mcp_server_test` | Probe a downstream server (tools/list, ping) | — |
      | `mcp_call_tool` | Route a call (serverName, toolName, arguments) | `POST /api/agent/tool` |
      | `system_status` | Kernel liveness + version | `GET /health` |
      | `billing_status` | Provider quotas, depletion, fallback chain | `GET /api/billing/status` |
      | `get_system_stats` | OS / runtime metrics | — |
      | `codebase_search` | symbols / definitions / references (empty result when kernel catalog has no match) | `GET /api/mcp/native/search?query=` |
      | `codebase_outline` | Symbol outline for a file | — |
      | `repomap` | Ranked repo map with symbol summaries | — |
      | `read` / `write` / `edit` / `find` / `grep` / `ls` (+ `read_file` / `write_file` / `search` / `run_shell_command` / `bash` / `apply_search_replace`) | File + shell access through the kernel | local tools |
      | `code_interpreter` | Run python/node snippets (stateful session) | — |
      | `add_bookmark` | Save URL with title + tags | — |
      | `jira_create_issue` / `confluence_search` | Atlassian integration | — |
      | `cloud_troubleshoot` / `generate_devops_pipeline` | Ops generators | — |
      | `install_mcp_server` | Install an MCP server via npx | — |
      | `launch_webview` | Open a system browser window | — |
      | `download_llamafile` | Fetch a standalone model binary | — |
      | `list_processes` / `kill_process` | Process management (Windows) | — |
      
      ## Discipline loop
      
      ```
      RECALL → DISCOVER (if gap) → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY
      ```
      
      1. **RECALL** before re-deriving: FTS fast path first, semantic `search_memory` for depth; include scratchpad `goal`.
      2. **DISCOVER** on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` → semantic search → `mcp_call_tool`.
      3. **PERSIST** non-obvious outcomes: `add_memory` (tags `topic,kind`) + `memory_extract_relations`.
      4. **VERIFY** around production changes: `system_status` + `billing_status` before and after.
      
      If the kernel is down, say so in one short sentence and continue with local tools — never present mock state.
      
````

- path: addons\opencode\use-meta-model.ps1
  content: |
      # use-meta-model.ps1 — launch OpenCode on Meta muse-spark-1.3-contributor.
      # USER-RUN ONLY. This script never reads auth.json / token files and never
      # prints the key. It only presence-checks the VARIABLE NAME, then launches.
      #
      #   1. $env:META_MUSE_API_KEY = '<paste-your-token-here>'  # you set this
      #   2. .\use-meta-model.ps1 "your prompt here"             # or no args for TUI
      #   3. # optional default model for TUI: merge meta-model.opencode.jsonc
      #      # "provider" block into opencode.json(c), then /models in the TUI.
      param([Parameter(ValueFromRemainingArguments = $true)][string[]]$Prompt)
      
      if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($env:META_MUSE_API_KEY)) {
        Write-Error 'META_MUSE_API_KEY is not set. Set the variable in this shell first, then re-run. The value is never displayed or stored by this script.'
        exit 1
      }
      Write-Host '[hypernexus] META_MUSE_API_KEY is set (value hidden). Starting OpenCode with meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor ...'
      
      $opencode = Get-Command opencode -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
      if (-not $opencode) { $opencode = 'C:\Users\hyper\AppData\Roaming\npm\opencode.ps1' }
      
      if ($Prompt -and $Prompt.Count -gt 0) {
        & $opencode run --model 'meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor' -- $Prompt
      } else {
        $env:OPENCODE_MODEL = 'meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor'
        & $opencode
      }
      
- path: addons\crush\hooks\hn-rbac.sh
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env bash
      # hn-rbac.sh — HyperNexus RBAC guard for Crush (deny-list for destructive patterns).
      # POSIX twin of hn-rbac.cmd: exit 2 blocks (stderr = reason), exit 0 allows.
      # Runs inside the gate chain; never reads credentials, never throws.
      # Input contract (per Crush hooks spec): tool name arrives in $CRUSH_TOOL_NAME,
      # full JSON envelope on stdin. The shell command (if any) is extracted from
      # stdin via CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND when present, else parsed from the JSON.
      TOOL="${CRUSH_TOOL_NAME:-}"
      RAW="$(cat 2>/dev/null)"
      CMD_TEXT="${CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-}"
      if [ -z "$CMD_TEXT" ]; then
        # Extract .tool_input.command from the JSON envelope without jq (portable sed).
        CMD_TEXT="$(printf '%s' "$RAW" | sed -n 's/.*"command"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"\([^"]*\)".*/\1/p' | head -n 1)"
      fi
      case "$TOOL" in
        bash|run_shell_command)
          if printf '%s' "$CMD_TEXT" | grep -qE 'rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+/[[:space:]]*$|rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+~|mkfs|:\(\)\{ *:\|:& *\};|format[[:space:]]+[A-Za-z]:|del[[:space:]]+/'; then
            echo "blocked by hypernexus-rbac: destructive shell pattern denied" >&2
            exit 2
          fi
          ;;
      esac
      echo '{"decision":"allow"}'
      exit 0

      {
        "$schema": "https://hypernexus.site/schemas/extension-manifest.v1.json",
        "name": "hypernexus",
        "displayName": "HyperNexus",
        "version": "1.0.21",
        "description": "Universal AI control plane integration: persistent multi-tier memory (L1/L2/L3), semantic MCP tool discovery across 26k+ tools, knowledge graph, session import, skill registry, code search, and agent orchestration.",
        "kind": "harness-extension",
        "generatedFor": "multi-harness + autonomous-orchestration",
        "kernel": {
          "baseUrl": "http://127.0.0.1:7778",
          "health": "/health",
          "overrideEnv": [
            "HYPERNEXUS_URL",
            "HYPERNEXUS_TRPC_UPSTREAM"
          ],
          "mcp": {
            "command": "hypernexus.exe",
            "args": [
              "mcp"
            ],
            "transport": "stdio",
            "toolNamespace": "mcp__hypernexus"
          }
        },
        "harnesses": {
          "pi": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent (ExtensionAPI)",
            "adapter": "addons/pi/hypernexus.ts",

              "tui"

            "installer": "addons/opencode/install.mjs",

              "tool-hooks",
              "permission-hook",
              "shell-hook",
              "mcp",
              "worktree",
              "events",
              "agent"
            ],
            "notes": "36 tools + 11 /hn-* commands + 3 skills; auto-registers `hypernexus mcp` stdio server; hot-path FTS context injection; @memory:key prompt expansion; RBAC via HYPERNEXUS_RBAC."
          },
          "jcode": {

            "surfaces": [

              "skills",
              "prompt-overlay",

              "swarm-prompt"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via `hypernexus mcp` stdio server registered in .jcode/mcp.json (toolNamespace mcp__hypernexus). 3 skills + discipline overlay + tool routing + swarm persistence block. Core works without the extension over HTTP. Stdio server serves last-good inventory on slow kernel (15s TTL cache + retry) and dispatches requests concurrently."
          },
          "droid": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Factory Droid plugin (.factory-plugin/plugin.json) + MCP stdio + hooks + skills/commands/droids/output-styles",
            "adapter": "addons/droid/plugin/hypernexus/",

            "installer": "addons/droid/install.mjs",

              "plugin-bundle",
              "commands",
              "skills",
              "hooks",

              "output-style"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via `hypernexus mcp` stdio server registered in plugin mcp.json (~/.factory/mcp.json). 3 skills + 11 /hn-* commands + 2 droids + 1 output style. 8 lifecycle hooks (SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit/PreToolUse/PostToolUse/Stop/SessionEnd/PreCompact/SubagentStop) as Node scripts (no bash dependency on Windows); hot-path FTS recall; @memory:key expansion; RBAC via HYPERNEXUS_RBAC (audit/ask/deny). Meta muse-spark-1.3-contributor runs natively via apiKeyHelper (addons/droid/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs mints Model API key from pi auth.json identity)."

            "install": "<project>/.codewhale/ or ~/.codewhale/ — mcp.json, plugins/hypernexus/ bundle (plugin.json + plugin.toml + commands/ + skills/), skills/ mirror, AGENTS.md managed block",
            "installer": "addons/codewhale/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "mcp",
              "plugin-bundle",
              "commands",
              "skills",
              "prompt-overlay"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via hypernexus mcp stdio server registered in .codewhale/mcp.json (toolNamespace mcp__hypernexus). Agent Plugins v1.0.0 bundle adds 8 /hn-* commands + 3 skills (activate via /plugin validate/show/trust/enable). Skills mirrored to skills/ for direct discovery. Version stamped from /VERSION at install. Core works without the extension over HTTP."
          },
          "crush": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Crush crushrc (Bash config) + MCP stdio + skills + PreToolUse/SessionStart hooks",
            "adapter": "addons/crush/",
            "install": "~/.config/crush/ (global) or <project>/.crush/ — crushrc managed block, skills/, commands/, hooks/, prompt-overlay.md, preferred-tools.md",
            "installer": "addons/crush/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "mcp",
              "commands",
              "skills",
              "hooks",
              "prompt-overlay",
              "preferred-tools",
              "swarm-prompt"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via hypernexus mcp stdio server (FORWARD-SLASH exe path required; backslashes break the MCP handshake). 8 hn commands + 3 user-invocable skills. PreToolUse allow-gate (destructive-pattern deny) + SessionStart FTS bootstrap (fail-open, no python required). Meta meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor via pi-OAuth live-read (pi owns refresh). Core works without the MCP server over HTTP http://127.0.0.1:7778."
          },
          "freebuff": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Freebuff/Codebuff platform (.agents/mcp.json stdio + .agents/skills SKILL.md + AGENTS.md overlay; no provider/hook API)",
            "adapter": "addons/freebuff/",
            "install": "<project>/.agents/ or ~/.agents/ (global) — mcp.json merge, 11 skills, HYPERNEXUS.md, AGENTS.md managed block",
            "installer": "addons/freebuff/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "mcp",
              "skills",
              "command-skills",
              "prompt-overlay",
              "preferred-tools",
              "swarm-prompt"
            ],
            "notes": "58 tools via hypernexus mcp stdio server registered in .agents/mcp.json (standard mcpServers format; freebuff reads projectDir/parents, trust via prompt or --trust-agents). 3 core skills + 8 hn command-skills (all /skill:<name>). AGENTS.md managed discipline block (freebuff reads AGENTS.md at chat start, falls back to CLAUDE.md). No hook/extension API on this harness: RBAC + context injection are by-convention skill instructions; degradation contract (budgeted, never-throw, FTS fast path, fail-open) is documented. Meta muse-spark-1.3-contributor: /model picker ships it natively (paid-plan locked without a plan); subscription/OAuth path mints a Model API key live from pi auth.json via bin/meta-muse-key.mjs (pi owns refresh, nothing stored). Core works without the MCP server over HTTP http://127.0.0.1:7778."
          },
          "reasonix": {
            "status": "active",
            "api": "Reasonix Extension Protocol v2 (NDJSON JSON-RPC 2.0 stdio sidecar)",
            "adapter": "addons/reasonix/",
            "install": "reasonix plugin install <repo>/addons/reasonix --link + global config.toml [[plugins]] + global settings.json hooks + .mcp.json",
            "installer": "addons/reasonix/install.mjs",
            "surfaces": [
              "tools",
              "interceptors",
              "skills",
              "commands",
              "agents",
              "hooks",
              "mcp",
              "prompt-overlay"
            ],
            "notes": "36 ext__hypernexus__* runtime tools + 10 intercepts + 3 skills + 11 /hypernexus: commands + 1 orchestrator agent; native SessionStart/PostToolUse/PreToolUse hooks + SessionStart/PreCompact context files; MCP via contributes.mcpServers + config.toml [[plugins]] + .mcp.json. Meta muse-spark-1.3-contributor via META_MUSE_API_KEY env ref (pi owns OAuth refresh; scripts/sync-meta-oauth-reasonix.ps1). Core works without the extension over HTTP."
          }
        },
        "contract": {
          "hotPathBudgetMs": 400,
          "defaultBudgetMs": 2500,
          "rules": [
            "Every kernel call is budgeted (AbortSignal.timeout) and never throws — a hung kernel degrades to a short note, never stalls a turn.",
            "Hot-path hooks (before_agent_start / context / input) use FTS-only search (/api/memory/fts-search, ~15ms) within hotPathBudgetMs. Never call the full /api/memory/search (~800ms vector+embed) on a hot path.",
            "Side-effect writes on per-event hooks (audit log, memory store) are fire-and-forget — do not await them in the turn path.",
            "Explicit user-invoked tools may use the full semantic search and defaultBudgetMs.",
            "Normalize kernel response shapes at the adapter boundary so harness code never reads `data[].record` vs `data[]` differences."
          ]

        "hooks": {
          "session_start": "Prime session: set name, status, inject TN system prompt + fast context.",
          "before_agent_start": "Inject fast FTS context into system prompt (hot-path, 400ms budget).",
          "context": "Per-turn: feed tool-suggestion monitor (fire-and-forget) + inject fast FTS context (hot-path).",
          "tool_call": "Enterprise RBAC enforcement (dangerous-pattern authorize) + fire-and-forget audit log.",
          "tool_result": "Auto-store substantial tool results to L2 (fire-and-forget).",
          "input": "Expand @memory:key tokens via fast search (hot-path).",
          "turn_end": "Store turn summary to L2 (fire-and-forget).",
          "session_before_compact": "Enrich compaction summary with related L2 context.",
          "session_compact": "Store compaction summary to L2 (fire-and-forget).",
          "session_shutdown": "Clear widget/status, stop timers."
        },
        "orchestration": {
          "loop": "RECALL -> DISCOVER -> EXECUTE -> PERSIST -> VERIFY",
          "compositionRules": [
            "Always start with RECALL (context_harvest / memory_search) before multi-step work.",
            "Use DISCOVER (tool_search / tool_suggest) when hitting a capability gap.",
            "Always end with PERSIST (memory_store) for non-obvious decisions/gotchas.",
            "VERIFY (system_status) before and after production changes."
          ],
          "workflows": [
            {
              "id": "MEM-FIRST",
              "when": "any multi-step task",
              "steps": [
                "context_harvest",
                "memory_search",
                "memory_vector_search",
                "<execute>",
                "memory_store"
              ]
            },
            {
              "id": "DISCOVER",
              "when": "capability gap",
              "steps": [
                "tool_suggest",
                "tool_search",
                "mcp_call",
                "memory_store"
              ]
            },
            {
              "id": "HARVEST",
              "when": "resuming after a break",
              "steps": [
                "context_harvest",
                "memory_list",
                "session_search",
                "memory_vector_search"
              ]
            },
            {
              "id": "HANDOFF",
              "when": "end of session / context switch",
              "steps": [
                "memory_store(session summary)",
                "scratchpad(set goal)"
              ]
            },
            {
              "id": "OPS",
              "when": "deployments / incidents",
              "steps": [
                "system_status",
                "<act>",
                "system_status",
                "memory_store"
              ]
            }
          ]
        },
        "autonomousOrchestration": {
          "discovery": "Read this manifest to enumerate capabilities across all bound harnesses.",
          "dispatch": "Each capability declares a uniform kernel binding; call the kernel directly regardless of harness, or call the harness-native tool name in `harness.<harness>`.",
          "idempotence": "memory_store writes are append-only; reads are side-effect-free. Safe to retry reads; batch writes.",
          "budgets": "Respect `budgetMs` per capability. Never exceed hotPathBudgetMs on turn-critical paths.",
          "escalation": "If a harness adapter is unavailable, fall back to the kernel binding (the kernel is the single source of truth)."
        }
      }

- path: addons\freebuff\skills\hypernexus-workflows\SKILL.md
  content: |
      ---
      user-invocable: true
      name: hypernexus-workflows
      description: "Reusable HyperNexus orchestration workflows for Freebuff: MEM-FIRST, DISCOVER, HARVEST, HANDOFF, OPS, and the RECALL→DISCOVER→EXECUTE→PERSIST→VERIFY loop. Trigger on 'multi-step task', 'resume work', 'capability gap', 'handoff', 'session summary', or when starting complex work with hypernexus tools available."
      ---
      
      # HyperNexus Workflows (Freebuff)
      
      Invoke with `/skill:hypernexus-workflows`.
      
      Loop: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**
      
      Freebuff maps the loop onto MCP tools + HTTP (no hooks surface — run each
      step explicitly).
      
      ## MEM-FIRST (any multi-step task)
      
      1. FTS recall: `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8` (fast, ~15ms)
      2. Depth: `search_memory` for prior decisions/gotchas
      3. Execute
      4. `add_memory` the non-obvious outcomes (tags `topic,kind`)
      
      ## DISCOVER (capability gap)
      
      1. `mcp_list_servers` — live inventory
      2. `mcp_list_tools` + semantic `/api/mcp/tools/search?query=` — meaning match
      3. `mcp_call_tool` — route the call (`serverName`, `toolName`, `arguments`)
      4. `add_memory` — remember what worked (tags `tool-routing`)
      
      ## HARVEST (resuming after a break)
      
      1. `search_memory` on the task topic + scratchpad `memory_scratchpad_get` (`goal`)
      2. HTTP `/api/sessions/summary` for cross-harness resume
      3. Recent vault activity via `memory_stats` / `search_memory` (empty query → recent)
      
      ## HANDOFF (end of session / context switch)
      
      1. `/skill:hn-summary` — model-generated session summary stored to L2
      2. `add_memory` with tags `["session", "summary"]`
      3. `freebuff --continue` reopens the chat; `/export` writes the transcript to Markdown
      
      ## OPS (deployments / incidents)
      
      See the `hypernexus-ops` skill (`/skill:hypernexus-ops`): verify → act → verify → persist.
      
- path: addons\crush\hooks\hn-pre-tool.sh
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env bash
      # hn-pre-tool.sh — HyperNexus PreToolUse gate for Crush (fail-open allow-gate).
      # =============================================================================
      # Crush runs PreToolUse hooks before each tool call: JSON on stdin, metadata in
      # CRUSH_TOOL_NAME / CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_* env vars. Exit 0 = allow (stdout parsed as
      # JSON decision), exit 2 = deny (stderr = reason), anything else = non-blocking.
      # This gate allows everything and performs NO network I/O (hot path stays fast).
      # Policy hook-point: add targeted exit-2 denials below for destructive patterns.
      # Never reads credentials, never throws.
      TOOL="${CRUSH_TOOL_NAME:-}"
      INPUT="$(cat 2>/dev/null)"
      case "$TOOL" in
        bash|run_shell_command)
          case "$INPUT" in
            *"rm -rf /"*|*"rm -rf ~"*|*"mkfs"*|*":(){:|:&};:"*)
              echo "blocked: destructive shell pattern denied by hypernexus-gate" >&2
              exit 2
              ;;
          esac
          ;;
      esac
      echo '{"decision":"allow"}'
      exit 0
      
- path: bin\cdp-send.mjs
  content: |
      // Type a message into muse.ai's chat input + submit it. node bin/cdp-send.mjs "<msg>|@file"
      import { execSync } from "child_process";
      import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
      const rawArg = process.argv.slice(2).join(" ");
      const msg = rawArg.startsWith("@") ? readFileSync(rawArg.slice(1), "utf8").trim() : rawArg;
      const tabs = JSON.parse(execSync("curl.exe -s -m 10 http://127.0.0.1:9222/json").toString());
      const tab = tabs.find((t) => t.type === "page" && t.url.includes("muse.ai"));
      if (!tab) { console.error("muse tab not found"); process.exit(1); }
      const ws = new WebSocket(tab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
      // Synchronous expression (NO setTimeout / awaitPromise): background tabs throttle
      // setTimeout, which would hang the evaluate. Set value -> input -> Enter.
      const js = `
      (function () {
        const ta = document.querySelector('textarea');
        if (!ta) return 'NO_TEXTAREA';
        const setter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype, 'value').set;
        setter.call(ta, ${JSON.stringify(msg)});
        ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
        ta.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, bubbles: true }));
        ta.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keypress', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, bubbles: true }));
        ta.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, bubbles: true }));
        return 'SUBMITTED';
      })()
      `;
      ws.onopen = () => ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: "Runtime.evaluate", params: { expression: js, returnByValue: true } }));
      ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
        const d = JSON.parse(ev.data);
        if (d.id === 1) { console.log("send result:", d.result?.result?.value ?? d.result?.exceptionDetails?.text); ws.close(); process.exit(0); }
      };
      setTimeout(() => { console.error("timeout"); process.exit(1); }, 60000);

Use for complex tasks that benefit from cross-session memory, MCP tool routing, and auditable execution.\"\n---\n\n# HyperNexus Orchestrator (Freebuff)\n\nYou are the HyperNexus orchestrator. You coordinate work through the HyperNexus\ncontrol plane (kernel `http://127.0.0.1:7778`, MCP server `hypernexus`, 58 tools).\n\nInvoke with `/skill:hypernexus-orchestrator`. Freebuff loads this from\n`.agents/skills/` — no plugin install step.\n\n## Loop\n\n1. **RECALL** — `search_memory` + `memory_scratchpad_get` (key `goal`) for the task\n   topic before planning. Hot paths: FTS `GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=`.\n2. **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: `mcp_list_servers` → `mcp_list_tools` (+\n   semantic `GET /api/mcp/tools/search?query=`) → `mcp_call_tool` to route.\n3. **EXECUTE** — do the work with normal tools; keep the plan in scratchpad key\n   `goal`. Every kernel call is budgeted; on failure degrade to local tools.\n4. **PERSIST** — `add_memory` for every non-obvious decision/gotcha (tags:\n   topic + `freebuff`); `memory_extract_relations` for entities; `memory_scratchpad_set`\n   when the goal changes.\n5. **VERIFY** — `system_status` (+ `billing_status`, `memory_stats`) after\n   significant changes.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Never re-derive what memory already holds — recall first.\n- Keep hot-path calls on FTS search; full semantic search is for explicit calls.\n- End long sessions with `memory_scratchpad_set` (goal) + `add_memory` (tags\n  `session,summary`) so the next session can resume (`/skill:hn-summary` form).\n- If the kernel is offline, degrade gracefully: continue with local tools and\n  note the gap in one short sentence.\n- Never claim a kernel write succeeded without tool output.\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# hn-rbac.sh — HyperNexus RBAC guard for Freebuff (deny-list for destructive patterns).\n# By-convention twin of crush hn-rbac.sh: Freebuff has no PreToolUse hook API,\n# so run this manually before destructive shell work, or follow it as policy.\n# Exit 2 blocks (stderr = reason), exit 0 allows. Never reads credentials, never throws.\nTOOL=\"${CRUSH_TOOL_NAME:-${TOOL_NAME:-}}\"\nRAW=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null)\"\nCMD_TEXT=\"${CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-${TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-}}\"\nif [ -z \"$CMD_TEXT\" ]; then\n  CMD_TEXT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$RAW\" | sed -n 's/.*\"command\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"\\([^\"]*\\)\".*/\\1/p' | head -n 1)\"\nfi\ncase \"$TOOL\" in\n  bash|run_shell_command|\"\")\n    if printf '%s' \"$CMD_TEXT$RAW\" | grep -qE 'rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+/[[:space:]]*$|rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+~|mkfs|:\\(\\)\\{ *:\\|:& *\\};|format[[:space:]]+[A-Za-z]:|del[[:space:]]+/'; then\n      echo \"blocked by hypernexus-rbac: destructive shell pattern denied\" >&2\n      exit 2\n    fi\n    ;;\nesac\necho '{\"decision\":\"allow\"}'\nexit 0\n"
}
