```

- path: addons\freebuff\install.mjs
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env node
      /**
       * install.mjs — HyperNexus → Freebuff integration installer
       * ==========================================================
       * Installs the full HyperNexus control-plane surface for Freebuff (Codebuff
       * platform). Idempotent: the MCP server entry is merged by key, skills are
       * overwritten, and the AGENTS.md overlay is kept inside managed markers and
       * can be re-run or removed safely.
       *
       * Modes:
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs                    install into this project (.agents/)
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project <dir>    install into <dir>/.agents/
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --global            install into ~/.agents/ (covers all projects under $HOME)
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --check      verify installation (exit 1 on gaps)
       *   node addons/freebuff/install.mjs --project . --uninstall   remove managed files/blocks
       *
       * What it installs (project → <dir>/.agents/  |  global → ~/.agents/):
       *   mcp.json            hypernexus stdio server merged into mcpServers (never clobbers other servers)
       *   skills dir          3 core skills (hypernexus, hypernexus-ops, hypernexus-workflows)
       *                       + 8 command-skills (hn-status, hn-search, hn-store, hn-session,
       *                       hn-context, hn-summary, hn-tools, hn-purge)
       *   HYPERNEXUS.md       overlay reference (discipline loop + tool map + Meta notes)
       *   AGENTS.md block     discipline overlay between managed markers (project mode only;
       *                       global mode skips it — freebuff reads AGENTS.md per project)
       *
       * The installer never stores secrets: the Meta token is read LIVE from
       * ~/.pi/agent/auth.json at every mint (pi owns OAuth refresh). See
       * meta-model.freebuff.md + bin/meta-muse-key.mjs.
       * After install: restart freebuff in the project (trust .agents when prompted,
       * or launch with --trust-agents) so the new mcp.json + skills load.
       */
      
      import fs from "node:fs";
      import path from "node:path";
      import os from "node:os";
      import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
      
      const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
      const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..");
      const HOME = os.homedir() || process.env.USERPROFILE || process.env.HOME || "";
      
      const MODE = process.argv.includes("--uninstall")
        ? "uninstall"
        : process.argv.includes("--check")
          ? "check"
          : "install";
      const GLOBAL = process.argv.includes("--global");
      
      function argValue(flag) {
        const i = process.argv.indexOf(flag);
        if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] && !process.argv[i + 1].startsWith("--")) return process.argv[i + 1];
        const eq = process.argv.find((a) => a.startsWith(flag + "="));
        return eq ? eq.slice(flag.length + 1) : "";
      }
      
      const TARGET_OVERRIDE = argValue("--project") || argValue("--target");
      const PROJECT_DIR = path.resolve(TARGET_OVERRIDE || (GLOBAL ? HOME : process.cwd()));
      const AGENTS_DIR = GLOBAL ? path.join(HOME, ".agents") : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, ".agents");
      const AGENTS_FILE = GLOBAL ? null : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, "AGENTS.md"); // global has no single project root
      
      const MD_BEGIN = "<!-- hypernexus:begin:freebuff (managed by addons/freebuff/install.mjs — do not edit inside this block) -->";
      const MD_END = "<!-- hypernexus:end:freebuff -->";
      
      const CORE_SKILLS = ["hypernexus", "hypernexus-ops", "hypernexus-workflows"];
      const COMMANDS = ["hn-status", "hn-search", "hn-store", "hn-session", "hn-context", "hn-summary", "hn-tools", "hn-purge"];
      const AGENT = "hypernexus-orchestrator";
      const HOOKS = ["hn-session.sh", "hn-rbac.sh", "hn-pre-tool.sh"];
      
      // ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function log(msg) {
        console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] ${msg}`);
      }
      
      function readIfExists(p) {
        try {
          return fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      }
      
      function writeUtf8(file, content) {
        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
        fs.writeFileSync(file, content, "utf8"); // utf8 → no BOM (Go/Node parsers reject BOM)
      }
      
      /** Forward-slash exe path — backslashes break MCP shell expansion on Windows. */
      function resolveExe() {
        const exe = process.platform === "win32" ? "hypernexus.exe" : "hypernexus";
        const override = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_MCP_COMMAND;
        if (override) return override;
        for (const c of [
          path.join(REPO_ROOT, "bin", exe),
          path.join(PROJECT_DIR, "bin", exe),
        ]) {
          if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c.split(path.sep).join("/");
        }
        return exe;
      }
      
      function workspaceRoot() {
        const ws = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT || (GLOBAL ? HOME : PROJECT_DIR);
        return ws.split(path.sep).join("/");
      }
      
      function upsertManagedBlock(filePath, blockText) {
        const block = blockText.trim();
        const existing = readIfExists(filePath) ?? "";
        // NOTE: unique :freebuff markers — sibling harnesses (hax, codewhale) share the
        // generic hypernexus:begin/end markers, so never match those here.
        const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_BEGIN);
        const endIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_END);
        let next;
        if (beginIdx >= 0 && endIdx > beginIdx) {
          next = existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + block + existing.slice(endIdx + MD_END.length);
        } else {
          next = existing.trimEnd() + (existing.trimEnd() ? "\n\n" : "") + block + "\n";
        }
        writeUtf8(filePath, next);
        return existing !== next;
      }
      
      function removeManagedBlock(filePath) {
        const existing = readIfExists(filePath);
        if (existing === null) return false;
        const beginIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_BEGIN);
        const endIdx = existing.indexOf(MD_END);
        if (beginIdx < 0 || endIdx < beginIdx) return false;
        const next = (existing.slice(0, beginIdx) + existing.slice(endIdx + MD_END.length)).replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n");
        if (next.trim() === "") {
          fs.rmSync(filePath, { force: true });
          return true;
        }
        writeUtf8(filePath, next);
        return true;
      }
      
      function agentsBlock() {
        return `${MD_BEGIN}
      ## HyperNexus Control Plane (freebuff)
      
      This workspace is backed by the local HyperNexus kernel (Go, port 7778).
      Freebuff loads this file at chat start plus \`.agents/mcp.json\` and
      \`.agents/skills/*\` (each skill invocable as \`/skill:<name>\`).
      
      Discipline loop for multi-step work: **RECALL → DISCOVER → EXECUTE → PERSIST → VERIFY**
      
      - **RECALL** — before re-deriving: FTS fast path \`GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8\`
        (~15ms), semantic \`hypernexus\` MCP \`search_memory\` for depth, scratchpad \`goal\`.
      - **DISCOVER** — on capability gaps: \`mcp_list_servers\` → \`mcp_list_tools\` →
        semantic \`/api/mcp/tools/search\`, then route with \`mcp_call_tool\`.
      - **PERSIST** — non-obvious outcomes via \`add_memory\` (tags \`topic,kind\`) +
        \`memory_extract_relations\`; session handoffs via \`/skill:hn-summary\`.
      - **VERIFY** — \`system_status\` + \`billing_status\` before/after production changes.
      - Skills: \`/skill:hypernexus\` \`/skill:hypernexus-ops\` \`/skill:hypernexus-workflows\`
        + command-skills \`/skill:hn-status|hn-search|hn-store|hn-session|hn-context|hn-summary|hn-tools|hn-purge\`.
      - Meta model: \`/model\` already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (paid-plan locked without a plan);
        subscription/OAuth path = \`node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs\` (pi owns refresh;
        see \`addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md\`).
      - Degradation contract: kernel calls are budgeted and never stall a turn. If the kernel
        is down (\`curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/health\` fails), say so in one sentence and
        continue with local tools — never mock state.
      ${MD_END}`;
      }
      
      // ── install steps ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function installMcpJson() {
        const file = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "mcp.json");
        let config = {};
        const existing = readIfExists(file);
        if (existing) {
          try {
            config = JSON.parse(existing);
          } catch (e) {
            log(`WARNING: could not parse ${file}: ${e.message}. Leaving it untouched.`);
            return false;
          }
        }
        config.mcpServers = config.mcpServers && typeof config.mcpServers === "object" ? config.mcpServers : {};
        config.mcpServers.hypernexus = {
          command: resolveExe(),
          args: ["mcp"],
          env: {
            HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT: workspaceRoot(),
            HYPERNEXUS_URL: process.env.HYPERNEXUS_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:7778",
          },
          timeout: 30,
        };
        writeUtf8(file, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + "\n");
        return true;
      }
      
      function installSkills() {
        let count = 0;
        for (const name of CORE_SKILLS) {
          const src = path.join(__dirname, "skills", name, "SKILL.md");
          const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name, "SKILL.md");
          if (!fs.existsSync(src)) {
            log(`WARNING: missing source skill ${src}, skipping.`);
            continue;
          }
          writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, "utf8"));
          count++;
        }
        for (const name of COMMANDS) {
          const src = path.join(__dirname, "commands", `${name}.md`);
          const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name, "SKILL.md");
          if (!fs.existsSync(src)) {
            log(`WARNING: missing source command ${src}, skipping.`);
            continue;
          }
          writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, "utf8"));
          count++;
        }
        return count;
      }
      
      function installOverlay() {
        const overlay = readIfExists(path.join(__dirname, "prompt-overlay.md")) ?? "";
        const content = `# HyperNexus (Freebuff reference)\n\n> Installed by \`addons/freebuff/install.mjs\`. Source of truth lives in
      > \`addons/freebuff/\`; this copy is what Freebuff reads from \`.agents/\`.\n\n${overlay.trim()}\n`;
        writeUtf8(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "HYPERNEXUS.md"), content);
      }
      
      // ── check / uninstall ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      function checkAll() {
        const gaps = [];
        const mcpFile = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "mcp.json");
        const raw = readIfExists(mcpFile);
        if (!raw) {
          gaps.push(`missing ${mcpFile}`);
        } else {
          try {
            const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (!cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus) gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: no mcpServers.hypernexus entry`);
          } catch {
            gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: invalid JSON`);
          }
        }
        for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, ...COMMANDS]) {
          if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name, "SKILL.md"))) {
            gaps.push(`missing skill ${name}`);
          }
        }
        if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "HYPERNEXUS.md"))) gaps.push("missing HYPERNEXUS.md");
        if (AGENTS_FILE) {
          const agents = readIfExists(AGENTS_FILE) ?? "";
          if (!agents.includes("<!-- hypernexus:begin")) gaps.push("AGENTS.md: no managed hypernexus block");
        }
        const keyHelper = path.join(__dirname, "bin", "meta-muse-key.mjs");
        if (!fs.existsSync(keyHelper)) gaps.push("missing bin/meta-muse-key.mjs");
        if (gaps.length) {
          console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] CHECK FAILED (${gaps.length} gap(s)):\n  - ${gaps.join("\n  - ")}`);
          process.exit(1);
        }
        console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] CHECK OK: MCP + ${CORE_SKILLS.length + COMMANDS.length} skills + overlay present.`);
      }
      
      function uninstallAll() {
        let removed = 0;
        const mcpFile = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "mcp.json");
        const raw = readIfExists(mcpFile);
        if (raw) {
          try {
            const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (cfg?.mcpServers?.hypernexus) {
              delete cfg.mcpServers.hypernexus;
              if (Object.keys(cfg.mcpServers).length === 0) fs.rmSync(mcpFile, { force: true });
              else writeUtf8(mcpFile, JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2) + "\n");
              removed++;
            }
          } catch { /* leave unparseable file alone */ }
        }
        for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, ...COMMANDS]) {
          const dir = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "skills", name);
          if (fs.existsSync(dir)) {
            fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
            removed++;
          }
        }
        for (const f of [path.join(AGENTS_DIR, "HYPERNEXUS.md")]) {
          if (fs.existsSync(f)) {
            fs.rmSync(f, { force: true });
            removed++;
          }
        }
        if (AGENTS_FILE && removeManagedBlock(AGENTS_FILE)) removed++;
        log(`uninstalled (${removed} item(s) removed).`);
      }
      
      // ── main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      
      if (MODE === "check") {
        checkAll();
      } else if (MODE === "uninstall") {
        uninstallAll();
      } else {
        const mcpOk = installMcpJson();
        const skills = installSkills();
        installOverlay();
        let agentsNote = "skipped (global mode: freebuff reads AGENTS.md per project)";
        if (AGENTS_FILE) {
          const changed = upsertManagedBlock(AGENTS_FILE, agentsBlock());
          agentsNote = changed ? "managed block written" : "managed block already current";
        }
        log(`installed into ${AGENTS_DIR} (mcp.json ${mcpOk ? "merged" : "SKIPPED — unparseable"}, ${skills} skills, HYPERNEXUS.md; AGENTS.md ${agentsNote}).`);
        log(`restart freebuff in ${PROJECT_DIR} (trust .agents when prompted, or use --trust-agents).`);
        log(`verify: node addons/freebuff/install.mjs ${GLOBAL ? "--global" : `--project ${PROJECT_DIR}`} --check`);
      }
      
```

```json
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport os from \"node:os\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\nconst __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, \"..\", \"..\");\nconst HOME = os.homedir() || process.env.USERPROFILE || process.env.HOME || \"\";\nconst MODE = process.argv.includes(\"--uninstall\") ? \"uninstall\" : process.argv.includes(\"--check\") ? \"check\" : \"install\";\nconst GLOBAL = process.argv.includes(\"--global\");\nfunction argValue(flag) { const i = process.argv.indexOf(flag); if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i+1] && !process.argv[i+1].startsWith(\"--\")) return process.argv[i+1]; const eq = process.argv.find((a) => a.startsWith(flag + \"=\")); return eq ? eq.slice(flag.length+1) : \"\"; }\nconst TARGET_OVERRIDE = argValue(\"--project\") || argValue(\"--target\");\nconst PROJECT_DIR = path.resolve(TARGET_OVERRIDE || (GLOBAL ? HOME : process.cwd()));\nconst AGENTS_DIR = GLOBAL ? path.join(HOME, \".agents\") : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \".agents\");\nconst AGENTS_FILE = GLOBAL ? null : path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"AGENTS.md\");\nconst MD_BEGIN = \"<!-- hypernexus:begin:freebuff (managed by addons/freebuff/install.mjs \\u2014 do not edit inside this block) -->\";\nconst MD_END = \"<!-- hypernexus:end:freebuff -->\";\nconst CORE_SKILLS = [\"hypernexus\", \"hypernexus-ops\", \"hypernexus-workflows\"];\nconst COMMANDS = [\"hn-status\", \"hn-search\", \"hn-store\", \"hn-session\", \"hn-context\", \"hn-summary\", \"hn-tools\", \"hn-purge\"];\nconst AGENT = \"hypernexus-orchestrator\";\nconst HOOKS = [\"hn-session.sh\", \"hn-rbac.sh\", \"hn-pre-tool.sh\"];\nfunction log(m) { console.log(`[hypernexus-freebuff] ${m}`); }\nfunction readIfExists(p) { try { return fs.readFileSync(p, \"utf8\"); } catch { return null; } }\nfunction writeUtf8(f, c) { fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(f, c, \"utf8\"); }\nfunction resolveExe() { const exe = process.platform === \"win32\" ? \"hypernexus.exe\" : \"hypernexus\"; const ov = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_MCP_COMMAND; if (ov) return ov; for (const c of [path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"bin\", exe), path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"bin\", exe)]) { if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c.split(path.sep).join(\"/\"); } return exe; }\nfunction workspaceRoot() { const ws = process.env.HYPERNEXUS_WORKSPACE_ROOT || (GLOBAL ? HOME : PROJECT_DIR); return ws.split(path.sep).join(\"/\"); }\nfunction upsertManagedBlock(fp, bt) { const block = bt.trim(); const ex = readIfExists(fp) ?? \"\"; const bi = ex.indexOf(MD_BEGIN); const ei = ex.indexOf(MD_END); let nx; if (bi >= 0 && ei > bi) nx = ex.slice(0, bi) + block + ex.slice(ei + MD_END.length); else nx = ex.trimEnd() + (ex.trimEnd() ? \"\\n\\n\" : \"\") + block + \"\\n\"; writeUtf8(fp, nx); return ex !== nx; }\nfunction removeManagedBlock(fp) { const ex = readIfExists(fp); if (ex === null) return false; const bi = ex.indexOf(MD_BEGIN); const ei = ex.indexOf(MD_END); if (bi < 0 || ei < bi) return false; const nx = (ex.slice(0, bi) + ex.slice(ei + MD_END.length)).replace(/\\n{3,}/g, \"\\n\\n\"); if (nx.trim() === \"\") { fs.rmSync(fp, { force: true }); return true; } writeUtf8(fp, nx); return true; }\nfunction agentsBlock() { return `${MD_BEGIN}\\n## HyperNexus Control Plane (freebuff)\\n\\nThis workspace is backed by the local HyperNexus kernel (Go, port 7778).\\nFreebuff loads this file at chat start plus \\`.agents/mcp.json\\` and\\n\\`.agents/skills/*\\` (each skill invocable as \\`/skill:<name>\\`).\\n\\nDiscipline loop for multi-step work: **RECALL \\u2192 DISCOVER \\u2192 EXECUTE \\u2192 PERSIST \\u2192 VERIFY**\\n\\n- **RECALL** \\u2014 before re-deriving: FTS fast path \\`GET /api/memory/fts-search?q=<topic>&limit=8\\`\\n  (~15ms), semantic \\`hypernexus\\` MCP \\`search_memory\\` for depth, scratchpad \\`goal\\`.\\n- **DISCOVER** \\u2014 on capability gaps: \\`mcp_list_servers\\` \\u2192 \\`mcp_list_tools\\` \\u2192\\n  semantic \\`/api/mcp/tools/search\\`, then route with \\`mcp_call_tool\\`.\\n- **PERSIST** \\u2014 non-obvious outcomes via \\`add_memory\\` (tags \\`topic,freebuff\\`) +\\n  \\`memory_extract_relations\\`; session handoffs via \\`/skill:hn-summary\\`.\\n- **VERIFY** \\u2014 \\`system_status\\` + \\`billing_status\\` before/after production changes.\\n- Skills: \\`/skill:hypernexus\\` \\`/skill:hypernexus-ops\\` \\`/skill:hypernexus-workflows\\` \\`/skill:hypernexus-orchestrator\\`\\n  + command-skills \\`/skill:hn-status|hn-search|hn-store|hn-session|hn-context|hn-summary|hn-tools|hn-purge\\`.\\n- Hooks (\\`.agents/hooks/\\`, by-convention \\u2014 freebuff has no hook API): \\`hn-session.sh\\` bootstrap,\\n  \\`hn-rbac.sh\\` deny-list, \\`hn-pre-tool.sh\\` allow-gate. See \\`addons/freebuff/hooks/README.md\\`.\\n- Meta model: \\`/model\\` already lists **Muse Spark 1.3** (paid-plan locked without a plan);\\n  subscription/OAuth path = \\`node addons/freebuff/bin/meta-muse-key.mjs\\` (pi owns refresh;\\n  see \\`addons/freebuff/meta-model.freebuff.md\\`) or \\`use-meta-model.ps1\\` launcher.\\n- Degradation contract: kernel calls are budgeted and never stall a turn. If the kernel\\n  is down (\\`curl -s http://127.0.0.1:7778/health\\` fails), say so in one sentence and\\n  continue with local tools \\u2014 never mock state.\\n${MD_END}`; }\nfunction installMcpJson() { const file = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"mcp.json\"); let config = {}; const ex = readIfExists(file); if (ex) { try { config = JSON.parse(ex); } catch (e) { log(`WARNING: could not parse ${file}: ${e.message}. count++; } const asrc = path.join(__dirname, \"agent\", `${AGENT}.md`); const adest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", AGENT, \"SKILL.md\"); if (fs.existsSync(asrc)) { writeUtf8(adest, fs.readFileSync(asrc, \"utf8\")); count++; } else log(`WARNING: missing source agent ${asrc}, skipping.`); for (const name of COMMANDS) { const src = path.join(__dirname, \"commands\", `${name}.md`); const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", name, \"SKILL.md\"); if (!fs.existsSync(src)) { log(`WARNING: missing source command ${src}, skipping.`); continue; } writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, \"utf8\")); count++; } return count; }\nfunction installHooks() { let count = 0; for (const h of HOOKS) { const src = path.join(__dirname, \"hooks\", h); const dest = path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"hooks\", h); if (!fs.existsSync(src)) { log(`WARNING: missing source hook ${src}, skipping.`); continue; } writeUtf8(dest, fs.readFileSync(src, \"utf8\")); count++; } const rsrc = path.join(__dirname, \"hooks\", \"README.md\"); if (fs.existsSync(rsrc)) writeUtf8(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"hooks\", \"README.md\"), fs.readFileSync(rsrc, \"utf8\")); return count; }\nfunction installOverlay() { const overlay = readIfExists(path.join(__dirname, \"prompt-overlay.md\")) ?? \"\"; const content = `# HyperNexus (Freebuff reference)\\n\\n> Installed by \\`addons/freebuff/install.mjs\\`. } catch { gaps.push(`${mcpFile}: invalid JSON`); } } for (const name of [...CORE_SKILLS, AGENT, ...COMMANDS]) { if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"skills\", name, \"SKILL.md\"))) gaps.push(`missing skill ${name}`); } for (const h of HOOKS) { if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"hooks\", h))) gaps.push(`missing hook ${h}`); } if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(AGENTS_DIR, \"HYPERNEXUS.md\"))) gaps.push(\"missing HYPERNEXUS.md\"); Freebuff HyperNexus integration complete (12 skills, 3 hooks, MCP stdio, Meta mint via pi auth.json). Kernel v1.0.21 healthy. Please acknowledge.`;\nconst js = `(function(){var ta=document.querySelector('textarea');if(!ta)return 'NO_TEXTAREA';var setter=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype,'value').set;setter.call(ta,${JSON.stringify(msg)});ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));return 'STAGED:'+ta.value.slice(0,60);})()`;\nconst ws = new WebSocket(tab.webSocketDebuggerUrl);\nws.onopen = () => ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: \"Runtime.evaluate\", params: { expression: js, returnByValue: true } }));\nws.onmessage = (ev) => {\n  const d = JSON.parse(ev.data);\n  if (d.id === 1) { console.log(\"stage result:\", d.result?.result?.value ?? ```
```

```
- path: addons\freebuff\hooks\hn-rbac.sh
  content: |
      #!/usr/bin/env bash
      # hn-rbac.sh — HyperNexus RBAC guard for Freebuff (deny-list for destructive patterns).
      # By-convention twin of crush hn-rbac.sh: Freebuff has no PreToolUse hook API,
      # so run this manually before destructive shell work, or follow it as policy.
      # Exit 2 blocks (stderr = reason), exit 0 allows. Never reads credentials, never throws.
      TOOL="${CRUSH_TOOL_NAME:-${TOOL_NAME:-}}"
      RAW="$(cat 2>/dev/null)"
      CMD_TEXT="${CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-${TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-}}"
      if [ -z "$CMD_TEXT" ]; then
        CMD_TEXT="$(printf '%s' "$RAW" | sed -n 's/.*"command"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"\([^"]*\)".*/\1/p' | head -n 1)"
      fi
      case "$TOOL" in
        bash|run_shell_command|"")
          if printf '%s' "$CMD_TEXT$RAW" | grep -qE 'rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+/[[:space:]]*$|rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+~|mkfs|:\(\)\{ *:\|:& *\};|format[[:space:]]+[A-Za-z]:|del[[:space:]]+/'; then
            echo "blocked by hypernexus-rbac: destructive shell pattern denied" >&2
            exit 2
          fi
          ;;
      esac
      echo '{"decision":"allow"}'
      exit 0
      
```

Found a bug in the RBAC guard's deny matching — fixing it now.

```json
{
  "path": "addons/freebuff/hooks/hn-rbac.sh.new",
  "instructions": "fix rbac anchor bug",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# hn-rbac.sh — HyperNexus RBAC guard for Freebuff (deny-list for destructive patterns).\n# By-convention twin of crush hn-rbac.sh: Freebuff has no PreToolUse hook API,\n# so run this manually before destructive shell work, or follow it as policy.\n# Exit 2 blocks (stderr = reason), exit 0 allows. Never reads credentials, never throws.\nTOOL=\"${CRUSH_TOOL_NAME:-${TOOL_NAME:-}}\"\nRAW=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null)\"\nCMD_TEXT=\"${CRUSH_TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-${TOOL_INPUT_COMMAND:-}}\"\nif [ -z \"$CMD_TEXT\" ]; then\n  CMD_TEXT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$RAW\" | sed -n 's/.*\"command\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"\\([^\"]*\\)\".*/\\1/p' | head -n 1)\"\nfi\ncase \"$TOOL\" in\n  bash|run_shell_command|\"\")\n    # Check CMD_TEXT and RAW as separate lines so the $ anchor matches\n    # (crush twin greps CMD_TEXT only; concatenation would break the $ anchor).\n    if printf '%s\\n%s' \"$CMD_TEXT\" \"$RAW\" | grep -qE 'rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+/[[:space:]]*$|rm[[:space:]]+-(rf|fr)[[:space:]]+~|mkfs|:\\(\\)\\{ *:\\|:& *\\};|format[[:space:]]+[A-Za-z]:|del[[:space:]]+/'; then\n      echo \"blocked by hypernexus-rbac: destructive shell pattern denied\" >&2\n      exit 2\n    fi\n    ;;\nesac\necho '{\"decision\":\"allow\"}'\nexit 0\n"
}
```

**What changed (all in `addons/freebuff/`, plus battery + manifest):**
